




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

majjhimanikāyai


majjhimapaṇṇāsapāḷi


1. gahapativaggau

1. kandarakasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| atha khau paissau paiyau (ka॰) ca hatthārauhaputtau kandarakau ca paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā paissau hatthārauhaputtau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| kandarakau pana paribbājakau bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ pī॰) vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kandarakau paribbājakau tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama, yāvañ‍cidaṃ bhautā gautamaina  sammā bhikkhusaṅghau paṭipāditau! yaipi tai, bhau gautama, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau aitaparamaṃyaiva sammā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭipādaisuṃ – saiyyathāpi aitarahi bhautā gautamaina sammā bhikkhusaṅghau paṭipāditau| yaipi tai, bhau gautama, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau aitaparamaṃyaiva sammā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭipādaissanti – saiyyathāpi aitarahi bhautā gautamaina sammā bhikkhusaṅghau paṭipāditau’’ti|

2. ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, kandaraka, aivamaitaṃ, kandaraka| yaipi tai, kandaraka, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau aitaparamaṃyaiva sammā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭipādaisuṃ – saiyyathāpi aitarahi mayā sammā bhikkhusaṅghau paṭipāditau| yaipi tai, kandaraka, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau aitaparamaṃyaiva sammā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṭipādaissanti – saiyyathāpi aitarahi mayā sammā bhikkhusaṅghau paṭipāditau|

‘‘santi hi, kandaraka, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā| santi hi, kandaraka, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai saikkhā santatasīlā santatavuttinau nipakā nipakavuttinau; tai catūsu nipakavuttinau catūsu (sī॰) satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā supaṭṭhitacittā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) viharanti| katamaisu catūsu? idha, kandaraka, bhikkhu  kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai  abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti|

3. aivaṃ vuttai, paissau hatthārauhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva supañ‍ñattā cimai, bhantai, bhagavatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ saukapariddavānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya| mayampi hi, bhantai, gihī audātavasanā kālaina kālaṃ imaisu catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu  suppatiṭṭhitacittā viharāma| idha mayaṃ, bhantai, kāyai kāyānupassinau viharāma ātāpinau sampajānā satimantau, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharāma ātāpinau sampajānā satimantau, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; cittai  cittānupassinau viharāma ātāpinau sampajānā satimantau, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharāma ātāpinau sampajānā satimantau, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivaṃ manussagahanai aivaṃ manussakasaṭai aivaṃ manussasāṭhaiyyai  vattamānai sattānaṃ hitāhitaṃ jānāti| gahanañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yadidaṃ manussā; uttānakañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yadidaṃ pasavau| ahañhi, bhantai, pahaumi hatthidammaṃ sāraituṃ| yāvatakaina antaraina campaṃ gatāgataṃ karissati sabbāni tāni sāṭhaiyyāni kūṭaiyyāni vaṅkaiyyāni jimhaiyyāni pātukarissati| amhākaṃ pana, bhantai, dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā añ‍ñathāva kāyaina samudācaranti añ‍ñathāva vācāya añ‍ñathāva naisaṃ cittaṃ hauti| acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivaṃ manussagahanai aivaṃ manussakasaṭai aivaṃ manussasāṭhaiyyai vattamānai sattānaṃ hitāhitaṃ jānāti| gahanañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yadidaṃ manussā; uttānakañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yadidaṃ pasavau’’ti|

4. ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, paissa, aivamaitaṃ, paissa| gahanañhaitaṃ, paissa, yadidaṃ manussā; uttānakañhaitaṃ, paissa, yadidaṃ pasavau| cattāraumai, paissa, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, paissa, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; idha pana, paissa, aikac‍cau puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; idha pana, paissa, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; idha pana, paissa, aikac‍cau puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sītibhūtau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| imaisaṃ, paissa, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti?

‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ayaṃ mai puggalau cittaṃ  nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ayampi mai puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ayampi mai puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yau ca khau ayaṃ, bhantai, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati – ayamaiva ayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mai puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti|

5. ‘‘kasmā pana tai, paissa, imai tayau puggalā cittaṃ nārādhaintī’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau attānaṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti – iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau  cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau paraṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti – iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhantai, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ sukhakāmai dukkhapaṭik‍kūlai (sī॰ pī॰) ātāpaiti paritāpaiti – iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yau ca  khau ayaṃ, bhantai, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| iminā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viharati; sau attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ naiva ātāpaiti na paritāpaiti – iminā viharati| iminā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaiti| handa, ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma; bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, paissa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau paissau hatthārauhaputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

6. atha  khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantai paissai hatthārauhaputtai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, paissau hatthārauhaputtau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, paissau hatthārauhaputtau| sacai, bhikkhavai, paissau hatthārauhaputtau muhuttaṃ nisīdaiyya yāvassāhaṃ imai cattārau puggalai vitthāraina vibhajissāmi vibhajāmi (sī॰ pī॰), mahatā atthaina saṃyuttau abhavissa| api ca, bhikkhavai, aittāvatāpi  paissau hatthārauhaputtau mahatā atthaina saṃyuttau’’ti| ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau, aitassa, sugata, kālau, yaṃ  bhagavā imai cattārau puggalai vitthāraina vibhajaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

7. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau hatthāvalaikhanau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) naaihibhaddantikau natiṭṭhabhaddantikau naaihibhadantikau, natiṭṭhabhadantikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); nābhihaṭaṃ na uddissakataṃ na nimantanaṃ sādiyati; sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti na kaḷaupimukhā khaḷaupimukhau (sī॰) paṭiggaṇhāti na aiḷakamantaraṃ na daṇḍamantaraṃ na musalamantaraṃ na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ na gabbhiniyā na pāyamānāya na purisantaragatāya na saṅkittīsu na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī; na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ na suraṃ na mairayaṃ na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau; aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti…pai॰… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi  āhāraṃ āhāraiti…pai॰… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti – iti aivarūpaṃ aḍḍhamāsikaṃ pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau  sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā  hauti; vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti; kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti, kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.155 mahāsīhanādasuttai; sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati – iti  aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

8. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau aurabbhikau hauti sūkarikau sākuṇikau māgavikau luddau macchaghātakau caurau cauraghātakau gaughātakau bandhanāgārikau yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kurūrakammantā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

9. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau rājā vā hauti khattiyau muddhāvasittau brāhmaṇau vā mahāsālau| sau puratthimaina nagarassa navaṃ santhāgāraṃ sandhāgāraṃ (ṭīkā) kārāpaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kharājinaṃ nivāsaitvā sappitailaina kāyaṃ abbhañ‍jitvā magavisāṇaina piṭṭhiṃ kaṇḍuvamānau navaṃ santhāgāraṃ pavisati saddhiṃ mahaisiyā brāhmaṇaina ca purauhitaina| sau tattha anantarahitāya bhūmiyā haritupalittāya saiyyaṃ kappaiti| aikissāya gāviyā sarūpavacchāya yaṃ aikasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti  taina rājā yāpaiti, yaṃ dutiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina mahaisī yāpaiti, yaṃ tatiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina brāhmaṇau purauhitau yāpaiti, yaṃ catutthasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina aggiṃ juhati, avasaisaina vacchakau yāpaiti| sau aivamāha – ‘aittakā usabhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatarā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatariyau hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā ajā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā urabbhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, (aittakā assā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu, aittakā rukkhā chij‍jantu yūpatthāya, aittakā dabbhā lūyantu barihisatthāyā’ti  parihiṃsatthāya (ka॰)| yaipissa tai haunti dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā taipi daṇḍataj‍jitā  bhayataj‍jitā assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni karaunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

10. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau  sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ  vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā, kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

11. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau, laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati| abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya – iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī  vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ  vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| sau bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti, aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā; nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti; mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti; uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti; jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti; dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti; kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau  hauti; tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau  hauti; uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā sāviyaugā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sāci kuṭilapariyāyau paṭiviratau hauti; chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.293 cūḷahatthipadaupamai|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti, sapattabhārauva ḍaiti; aivamaiva bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

12. ‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai  saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

13. ‘‘sau  iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau,) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.296 cūḷahatthipadaupamai iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina  saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau  abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thīnamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa  ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

14. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi  jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau,  sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

15. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai  hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

16. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai  ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa  aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau attantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kandarakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aṭṭhakanāgarasuttaṃ

17. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau vaisāliyaṃ viharati bailuvagāmakai vaiḷuvagāmakai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| taina khau pana samayaina dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau pāṭaliputtaṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau yaina kuk‍kuṭārāmau yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau aitarahi viharati? dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ āyasmantaṃ ānanda’’nti| ‘‘aisau, gahapati, āyasmā ānandau vaisāliyaṃ viharati bailuvagāmakai’’ti| atha khau dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau pāṭaliputtai taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā yaina vaisālī yaina bailuvagāmakau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

18. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai ānanda, taina bhagavatā  jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aikadhammau akkhātau yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttañ‍caiva cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇātī’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, gahapati, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aikadhammau akkhātau yattha  bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttañ‍caiva cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇātī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aikadhammau  akkhātau yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttañ‍caiva cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇātī’’ti?

19. ‘‘idha, gahapati, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘idampi paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, gahapati, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aikadhammau akkhātau yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa  viharatau avimuttañ‍caiva cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

20. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘idampi khau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ…  anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘idampi khau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ…pai॰… anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā  …pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘idampi khau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ… anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (sī॰ pī॰)| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina abyāpajjhaina (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), abyāpaj‍jaina (ka॰) aṅguttaratikanipātaṭīkā aulaukaitabbā pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayampi khau maittācaitauvimutti abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau…pai॰…  anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayampi khau upaikkhācaitauvimutti abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti  pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau… anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayampi khau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ  niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayampi khau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpatti abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayampi khau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ tadanic‍caṃ niraudhadhamma’nti pajānāti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, gahapati, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aikadhammau akkhātau yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttañ‍caiva  cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇātī’’ti|

21. aivaṃ vuttai, dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai ānanda, purisau aikaṃva nidhimukhaṃ gavaisantau sakidaiva aikādasa nidhimukhāni  adhigacchaiyya; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ amatadvāraṃ gavaisantau sakidaiva sakiṃ daiva (ka॰) aikādasa amatadvārāni alatthaṃ bhāvanāya| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, purisassa agāraṃ aikādasadvāraṃ, sau tasmiṃ agārai ādittai aikamaikainapi dvāraina sak‍kuṇaiyya  attānaṃ sautthiṃ kātuṃ; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhantai, imaisaṃ aikādasan‍naṃ amatadvārānaṃ aikamaikainapi amatadvāraina sak‍kuṇissāmi attānaṃ sautthiṃ kātuṃ| imaihi nāma, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisissanti, kimaṅgaṃ kiṃ (sī॰ pī॰) panāhaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa pūjaṃ na karissāmī’’ti! atha khau dasamau gahapati aṭṭhakanāgarau pāṭaliputtakañ‍ca vaisālikañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātaitvā paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi, aikamaikañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ pac‍caikaṃ dussayugaina acchādaisi, āyasmantañ‍ca ānandaṃ ticīvaraina acchādaisi, āyasmatau ca ānandassa pañ‍casatavihāraṃ kārāpaisīti|

aṭṭhakanāgarasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. saikhasuttaṃ

22. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kāpilavatthavānaṃ kapilavatthuvāsīnaṃ (ka॰) sakyānaṃ navaṃ santhāgāraṃ acirakāritaṃ hauti anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ anajjhāvutthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā kainaci vā manussabhūtaina| atha khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ navaṃ santhāgāraṃ acirakāritaṃ acirakāritaṃ hauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā kainaci vā manussabhūtaina| taṃ, bhantai, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ paribhuñ‍jatu| bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ paribhuttaṃ pacchā kāpilavatthavā sakyā paribhuñ‍jissanti| tadassa kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina navaṃ santhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sabbasanthariṃ santhāgāraṃ sabbasanthariṃ santhataṃ (ka॰) santharitvā āsanāni pañ‍ñapaitvā udakamaṇikaṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā tailappadīpaṃ āraupaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sabbasanthariṃ santhataṃ, bhantai, santhāgāraṃ, āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni, udakamaṇikau upaṭṭhāpitau, tailappadīpau āraupitau| yassadāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina santhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā majjhimaṃ thambhaṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi| bhikkhusaṅghaupi khau pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā pacchimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi, bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| kāpilavatthavāpi khau sakyā pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā puratthimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya pacchimābhimukhā nisīdiṃsu, bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| atha khau bhagavā kāpilavatthavai sakyai bahudaiva rattiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, ānanda, kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ saikhau pāṭipadau paṭipadau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| piṭṭhi  mai āgilāyati; tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi, pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, satau sampajānau, uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā|

23. atha khau āyasmā ānandau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlasampan‍nau hauti, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti, sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

24. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā  pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya; yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti, pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, satau sampajānau, uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā, rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti|

25. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau  sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau  sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| hirimā hauti, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| auttappī hauti, auttappati kāyaduc‍caritaina  vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthā sabyañ‍janā kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā bahū sutā (?) haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| satimā hauti, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau, ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

26. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi, savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

27. ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau hauti, aivaṃ indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, aivaṃ bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, aivaṃ jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti, aivaṃ sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  hauti, aivaṃ catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau saikhau pāṭipadau apuc‍caṇḍatāya  samāpan‍nau, bhabbau abhinibbhidāya, bhabbau sambaudhāya, bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā tānāssu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni sammā parisaiditāni sammā paribhāvitāni, kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā na  aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vatimai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau bhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, yatau ariyasāvakau aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau hauti, aivaṃ indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, aivaṃ bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, aivaṃ jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti, aivaṃ sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, aivaṃ catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau saikhau pāṭipadau apuc‍caṇḍatāya samāpan‍nau, bhabbau abhinibbhidāya, bhabbau sambaudhāya, bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya|

28. ‘‘sa khau sau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, ayamassa paṭhamābhinibbhidā hauti kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

‘‘sa khau sau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, ayamassa dutiyābhinibbhidā hauti kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

‘‘sa khau sau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja  viharati, ayamassa tatiyābhinibbhidā hauti kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

29. ‘‘yampi  yampi khau (ka॰), mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlasampan‍nau hauti, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ  – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, idampissa hauti vij‍jāya; yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, idampissa hauti vij‍jāya| yampi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, idampissa hauti vij‍jāya|

‘‘ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau vij‍jāsampan‍nau itipi caraṇasampan‍nau itipi vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau itipi|

30. ‘‘brahmunāpaisā, mahānāma, sanaṅkumāraina gāthā bhāsitā –

‘khattiyau saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’ti||

‘‘sā khau panaisā, mahānāma, brahmunā sanaṅkumāraina gāthā sugītā nau duggītā, subhāsitā nau dubbhāsitā, atthasaṃhitā nau anatthasaṃhitā, anumatā bhagavatā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā uṭṭhahitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, ānanda, sādhu khau tvaṃ, ānanda, kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ saikhaṃ pāṭipadaṃ abhāsī’’ti|

idamavaucāyasmā  ānandau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| attamanā kāpilavatthavā sakyā āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

saikhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. pautaliyasuttaṃ

31. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅguttarāpaisu viharati āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅguttarāpānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āpaṇaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| āpaṇai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yainañ‍ñatarau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā ajjhaugahaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), ajjhaugāhitvā (pī॰ ka॰) añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| pautaliyaupi khau gahapati sampan‍nanivāsanapāvuraṇau pāpuraṇau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) chattupāhanāhi chattupāhanau (ka॰) jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina sau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau pautaliyaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃvij‍janti khau, gahapati, āsanāni; sacai ākaṅkhasi nisīdā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, pautaliyau gahapati ‘‘gahapativādaina maṃ samaṇau gautamau samudācaratī’’ti kupitau anattamanau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰…  tatiyampi khau bhagavā pautaliyaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃvij‍janti khau, gahapati, āsanāni; sacai ākaṅkhasi nisīdā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, pautaliyau gahapati gahapativādaina maṃ samaṇau gautamau samudācaratī’’ti kupitau anattamanau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhau  gautama, nacchan‍naṃ, tayidaṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ gahapativādaina samudācarasī’’ti| ‘‘tai hi tai, gahapati, ākārā, tai liṅgā, tai nimittā yathā taṃ gahapatissā’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi pana mai, bhau gautama, sabbai kammantā paṭikkhittā, sabbai vauhārā samucchin‍nā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tai, gahapati, sabbai kammantā paṭikkhittā, sabbai vauhārā samucchin‍nā’’ti? ‘‘idha mai, bhau gautama, yaṃ ahausi dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ puttānaṃ dāyaj‍jaṃ niyyātaṃ, tatthāhaṃ anauvādī anupavādī ghāsacchādanaparamau viharāmi| aivaṃ khau mai aivañ‍ca mai (syā॰), aivaṃ mai (ka॰), bhau gautama, sabbai kammantā paṭikkhittā, sabbai vauhārā samucchin‍nā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñathā khau tvaṃ, gahapati, vauhārasamucchaidaṃ vadasi, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidau hautī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidau hauti? sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidau  hautī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, gahapati, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau pautaliyau gahapati bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

32. bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭha khau imai, gahapati, dhammā ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidāya saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? apāṇātipātaṃ nissāya pāṇātipātau pahātabbau; din‍nādānaṃ nissāya adin‍nādānaṃ pahātabbaṃ; sac‍cavācaṃ sac‍caṃ vācaṃ (syā॰) nissāya musāvādau pahātabbau; apisuṇaṃ vācaṃ nissāya pisuṇā vācā pahātabbā; agiddhilaubhaṃ nissāya giddhilaubhau pahātabbau; anindārausaṃ nissāya nindārausau pahātabbau; ak‍kaudhūpāyāsaṃ nissāya kaudhūpāyāsau pahātabbau; anatimānaṃ nissāya atimānau pahātabbau| imai khau, gahapati, aṭṭha dhammā saṃkhittaina vuttā, vitthāraina avibhattā, ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidāya saṃvattantī’’ti| ‘‘yai mai yai mai pana (syā॰ ka॰), bhantai, bhagavatā aṭṭha dhammā saṃkhittaina vuttā, vitthāraina avibhattā, ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidāya saṃvattanti, sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā imai aṭṭha dhammai vitthāraina vitthāraitvā (ka॰) vibhajatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, gahapati, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau pautaliyau gahapati bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

33. ‘‘‘apāṇātipātaṃ  nissāya pāṇātipātau pahātabbau’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu pāṇātipātī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau pana pāṇātipātī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya pāṇātipātapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) garahaiyyuṃ pāṇātipātapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā pāṇātipātapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ pāṇātipātau| yai ca pāṇātipātapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘apāṇātipātaṃ nissāya pāṇātipātau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

34. ‘‘‘din‍nādānaṃ  nissāya adin‍nādānaṃ pahātabba’nti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu adin‍nādāyī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana adin‍nādāyī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya adin‍nādānapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ adin‍nādānapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā adin‍nādānapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ adin‍nādānaṃ| yai ca adin‍nādānapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā adin‍nādānā  paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘din‍nādānaṃ nissāya adin‍nādānaṃ pahātabba’nti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

35. ‘‘‘sac‍cavācaṃ nissāya musāvādau pahātabbau’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu musāvādī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana musāvādī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya musāvādapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ musāvādapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā musāvādapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ  musāvādau| yai ca musāvādapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, musāvādā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘sac‍cavācaṃ nissāya musāvādau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

36. ‘‘‘apisuṇaṃ vācaṃ nissāya pisuṇā vācā pahātabbā’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu pisuṇavācau assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana pisuṇavācau assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ pisuṇā vācā| yai ca pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, pisuṇāya  vācāya paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘apisuṇaṃ vācaṃ nissāya pisuṇā vācā pahātabbā’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

37. ‘‘‘agiddhilaubhaṃ nissāya giddhilaubhau pahātabbau’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu giddhilaubhī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana giddhilaubhī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya giddhilaubhapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ giddhilaubhapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā giddhilaubhapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ giddhilaubhau| yai ca giddhilaubhapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, giddhilaubhā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘agiddhilaubhaṃ nissāya giddhilaubhau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

38. ‘‘‘anindārausaṃ nissāya nindārausau pahātabbau’ti iti khau  panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu nindārausī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya  samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana nindārausī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya nindārausapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ nindārausapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā nindārausapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ nindārausau| yai ca nindārausapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, anindārausissa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘anindārausaṃ nissāya nindārausau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

39. ‘‘‘ak‍kaudhūpāyāsaṃ nissāya kaudhūpāyāsau pahātabbau’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu kaudhūpāyāsī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana kaudhūpāyāsī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya kaudhūpāyāsapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ kaudhūpāyāsapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā kaudhūpāyāsapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ kaudhūpāyāsau| yai ca kaudhūpāyāsapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, ak‍kaudhūpāyāsissa  aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘ak‍kaudhūpāyāsaṃ nissāya kaudhūpāyāsau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

40. ‘‘‘anatimānaṃ nissāya atimānau pahātabbau’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ haitu atimānī assaṃ, taisāhaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya paṭipan‍nau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana atimānī assaṃ, attāpi maṃ upavadaiyya atimānapac‍cayā, anuvic‍cāpi maṃ viñ‍ñū garahaiyyuṃ atimānapac‍cayā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā atimānapac‍cayā| aitadaiva khau pana saṃyaujanaṃ aitaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ atimānau| yai ca atimānapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, anatimānissa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti’| ‘anatimānaṃ nissāya atimānau pahātabbau’ti – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

41. ‘‘imai  khau, gahapati, aṭṭha dhammā saṃkhittaina vuttā, vitthāraina vibhattā avibhattā (syā॰ ka॰), yai ariyassa vinayai vauhārasamucchaidāya saṃvattanti; na tvaiva tāva ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau hautī’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau hauti? sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa  vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau hautī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, gahapati, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau pautaliyau gahapati bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

kāmādīnavakathā

42. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, kuk‍kurau jighacchādubbalyaparaitau gaughātakasūnaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau assa| tamainaṃ dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā aṭṭhikaṅkalaṃ sunik‍kantaṃ nik‍kantaṃ nimmaṃsaṃ lauhitamakkhitaṃ upasumbhaiyya upacchubhaiyya (sī॰ pī॰), upacchūbhaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), upac‍cumbhaiyya (ka॰)| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, api nu khau sau kuk‍kurau amuṃ aṭṭhikaṅkalaṃ sunik‍kantaṃ nik‍kantaṃ nimmaṃsaṃ lauhitamakkhitaṃ palaihantau jighacchādubbalyaṃ paṭivinaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’?

‘‘aduñhi, bhantai, aṭṭhikaṅkalaṃ sunik‍kantaṃ nik‍kantaṃ nimmaṃsaṃ lauhitamakkhitaṃ| yāvadaiva pana sau kuk‍kurau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assāti| aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā bahūpāyāsā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā yāyaṃ upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā taṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā, yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā yattha sabbasau laukāmisūpādānā aparisaisā nirujjhanti tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

43. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, gijjhau vā kaṅkau vā kulalau vā maṃsapaisiṃ  ādāya uḍḍīyaiyya uḍḍayaiyya (syā॰ pī॰)| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kaṅkāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā  anupatitvā vitacchaiyyuṃ vissaj‍jaiyyuṃ virājaiyyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, sacai sau gijjhau vā kaṅkau vā kulalau vā taṃ maṃsapaisiṃ na khippamaiva paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, sau tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā yāyaṃ upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā taṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā yattha sabbasau laukāmisūpādānā aparisaisā nirujjhanti tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

44. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau ādittaṃ tiṇuk‍kaṃ ādāya paṭivātaṃ gacchaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, sacai sau purisau taṃ ādittaṃ tiṇuk‍kaṃ na khippamaiva paṭinissaj‍jaiyya tassa sā ādittā tiṇuk‍kā hatthaṃ vā dahaiyya bāhuṃ vā dahaiyya añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ dahaiyya| añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅga (sī॰ pī॰) dahaiyya, sau tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā…pai॰… tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

45. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, aṅgārakāsu sādhikapaurisā, pūrā aṅgārānaṃ vītac‍cikānaṃ vītadhūmānaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭik‍kūlau| tamainaṃ dvai balavantau purisā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuṃ upakaḍḍhaiyyuṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, api nu sau purisau iticiticaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’?

‘‘viditañhi, bhantai, tassa purisassa imañ‍cāhaṃ aṅgārakāsuṃ papatissāmi, tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchissāmi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā…pai॰… tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

46. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau supinakaṃ passaiyya ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ| sau paṭibuddhau na kiñ‍ci paṭipassaiyya passaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti…pai॰… tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

47. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau yācitakaṃ bhaugaṃ yācitvā yānaṃ  vā yānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paurisaiyyaṃ paurausaiyyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), auraupaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pavaramaṇikuṇḍalaṃ| sau taihi yācitakaihi bhaugaihi purakkhatau parivutau  antarāpaṇaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya| tamainaṃ janau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘bhaugī vata, bhau, purisau, aivaṃ kira bhauginau bhaugāni bhuñ‍jantī’ti| tamainaṃ sāmikā yattha yatthaiva passaiyyuṃ tattha tatthaiva sāni haraiyyuṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, alaṃ nu khau tassa purisassa añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’?

‘‘sāminau hi, bhantai, sāni harantī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti…pai॰…  tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

48. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai tibbau vanasaṇḍau| tatrassa rukkhau sampan‍naphalau ca upapan‍naphalau uppan‍naphalau (syā॰) ca, na cassu kānici phalāni bhūmiyaṃ patitāni| atha purisau āgacchaiyya phalatthikau phalagavaisī phalapariyaisanaṃ caramānau| sau taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā taṃ rukkhaṃ passaiyya sampan‍naphalañ‍ca upapan‍naphalañ‍ca| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau rukkhau sampan‍naphalau ca upapan‍naphalau ca, natthi ca kānici phalāni bhūmiyaṃ patitāni| jānāmi khau panāhaṃ rukkhaṃ ārauhituṃ āruhituṃ (sī॰)| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ rukkhaṃ ārauhitvā yāvadatthañ‍ca khādaiyyaṃ ucchaṅgañ‍ca pūraiyya’nti| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ ārauhitvā yāvadatthañ‍ca khādaiyya ucchaṅgañ‍ca pūraiyya| atha  dutiyau purisau āgacchaiyya phalatthikau phalagavaisī phalapariyaisanaṃ caramānau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ādāya| sau taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā  taṃ rukkhaṃ passaiyya sampan‍naphalañ‍ca upapan‍naphalañ‍ca| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau rukkhau sampan‍naphalau ca upapan‍naphalau ca, natthi ca kānici phalāni bhūmiyaṃ patitāni| na khau panāhaṃ jānāmi rukkhaṃ ārauhituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ rukkhaṃ mūlatau chaitvā yāvadatthañ‍ca khādaiyyaṃ ucchaṅgañ‍ca pūraiyya’nti| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ mūlatauva chindaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, amukau asu (sī॰ pī॰) yau sau purisau paṭhamaṃ rukkhaṃ ārūḷhau sacai sau na khippamaiva aurauhaiyya tassa sau rukkhau papatantau hatthaṃ vā bhañ‍jaiyya pādaṃ vā bhañ‍jaiyya añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ bhañ‍jaiyya, sau tataunidānaṃ  maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā yāyaṃ upaikkhā nānattā nānattasitā taṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā yāyaṃ upaikkhā aikattā aikattasitā yattha sabbasau laukāmisūpādānā aparisaisā nirujjhanti tamaivūpaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti|

49. ‘‘sa  khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ  – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘sa khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘sa khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau imaṃyaiva anuttaraṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ āgamma āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatā khau, gahapati, ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau hauti|

50. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, yathā ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau hauti, api nu tvaṃ aivarūpaṃ vauhārasamucchaidaṃ attani samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘kau cāhaṃ, bhantai, kau ca ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidau! ārakā ahaṃ, bhantai, ariyassa vinayai sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ vauhārasamucchaidā| mayañhi, bhantai, pubbai añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai anājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyāti amañ‍ñimha, anājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyabhaujanaṃ bhaujimha, anājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyaṭhānai ṭhapimha; bhikkhū pana mayaṃ, bhantai, ājānīyaiva samānai anājānīyāti amañ‍ñimha, ājānīyaiva  samānai anājānīyabhaujanaṃ bhaujimha, ājānīyaiva samānai anājānīyaṭhānai ṭhapimha; idāni pana mayaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyai  paribbājakai anājānīyaiva samānai anājānīyāti jānissāma, anājānīyaiva samānai anājānīyabhaujanaṃ bhaujaissāma, anājānīyaiva samānai anājānīyaṭhānai ṭhapaissāma| bhikkhū pana mayaṃ, bhantai, ājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyāti jānissāma ājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyabhaujanaṃ bhaujaissāma, ājānīyaiva samānai ājānīyaṭhānai ṭhapaissāma| ajanaisi vata mai, bhantai, bhagavā samaṇaisu samaṇappaimaṃ, samaṇaisu samaṇappasādaṃ, samaṇaisu samaṇagāravaṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

pautaliyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. jīvakasuttaṃ

51. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa ambavanai| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ uddissa pāṇaṃ ārabhanti ārambhanti (ka॰), taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ uddissakaṭaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’nti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ uddissa pāṇaṃ ārabhanti, taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’nti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti?

52. ‘‘yai  tai, jīvaka, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ uddissa pāṇaṃ ārabhanti, taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’nti na mai tai vuttavādinau, abbhācikkhanti ca maṃ tai asatā abhūtaina| tīhi khau ahaṃ, jīvaka, ṭhānaihi maṃsaṃ aparibhauganti vadāmi| diṭṭhaṃ, sutaṃ, parisaṅkitaṃ – imaihi khau ahaṃ, jīvaka, tīhi ṭhānaihi maṃsaṃ aparibhauganti vadāmi| tīhi khau ahaṃ, jīvaka, ṭhānaihi maṃsaṃ paribhauganti vadāmi| adiṭṭhaṃ, asutaṃ, aparisaṅkitaṃ – imaihi khau ahaṃ, jīvaka, tīhi ṭhānaihi maṃsaṃ paribhauganti vadāmi|

53. ‘‘idha, jīvaka, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| tamainaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upasaṅkamitvā svātanāya bhattaina nimantaiti| ākaṅkhamānauva ākaṅkhamānau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), jīvaka, bhikkhu adhivāsaiti| sau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamati; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdati| tamainaṃ sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina parivisati| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘sādhu vata māyaṃ maṃ + ayaṃ = māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina  parivisaiyyāti! ahau vata māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā āyatimpi aivarūpaina paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina parivisaiyyā’ti – aivampissa na hauti| sau taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ agathitau agadhitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau anajjhāpan‍nau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, jīvaka, api nu sau bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai attabyābādhāya vā caitaiti, parabyābādhāya vā caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāya vā caitaitī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘nanu sau, jīvaka, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai anavaj‍jaṃyaiva āhāraṃ āhāraitī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘brahmā maittāvihārī’ti| taṃ mai idaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā sakkhidiṭṭhau; bhagavā hi, bhantai, maittāvihārī’’ti| ‘‘yaina khau, jīvaka, rāgaina yaina dausaina  yaina mauhaina byāpādavā assa sau rāgau sau dausau sau mauhau tathāgatassa pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau anabhāvakatau (sī॰ pī॰), anabhāvaṃgatau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| sacai khau tai, jīvaka, idaṃ sandhāya bhāsitaṃ anujānāmi tai aita’’nti| ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mai, bhantai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ’’ bhāsitanti (syā॰)|

54. ‘‘idha, jīvaka, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| tamainaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upasaṅkamitvā svātanāya bhattaina nimantaiti| ākaṅkhamānauva, jīvaka, bhikkhu adhivāsaiti| sau  tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  yaina gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamati; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdati| tamainaṃ sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina parivisati| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘sādhu vata māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina parivisaiyyāti! ahau vata māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā āyatimpi  aivarūpaina paṇītaina piṇḍapātaina parivisaiyyā’ti – aivampissa na hauti| sau taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ agathitau amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, jīvaka, api nu sau bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai attabyābādhāya vā caitaiti, parabyābādhāya vā caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāya vā caitaitī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘nanu sau, jīvaka, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai anavaj‍jaṃyaiva āhāraṃ āhāraitī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘brahmā upaikkhāvihārī’ti| taṃ mai idaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā sakkhidiṭṭhau; bhagavā hi, bhantai, upaikkhāvihārī’’ti| ‘‘yaina khau, jīvaka, rāgaina yaina dausaina yaina mauhaina vihaisavā assa arativā assa paṭighavā assa sau rāgau sau dausau sau mauhau tathāgatassa pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| sacai khau tai, jīvaka, idaṃ sandhāya bhāsitaṃ, anujānāmi tai  aita’’nti| ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mai, bhantai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ’’|

55. ‘‘yau  khau, jīvaka, tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā uddissa pāṇaṃ ārabhati sau pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yampi sau, gahapati, aivamāha – ‘gacchatha, amukaṃ nāma pāṇaṃ ānaithā’ti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yampi sau pāṇau galappavaiṭhakaina galappavaidhakaina (bahūsu) ānīyamānau dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yampi sau aivamāha – ‘gacchatha imaṃ pāṇaṃ ārabhathā’ti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yampi sau pāṇau ārabhiyamānau dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, iminā catutthaina ṭhānaina bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yampi sau tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā akappiyaina āsādaiti, iminā pañ‍camaina ṭhānaina bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| yau khau, jīvaka, tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā uddissa pāṇaṃ ārabhati sau imaihi pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! kappiyaṃ vata, bhantai, bhikkhū āhāraṃ āhārainti; anavaj‍jaṃ vata, bhantai, bhikkhū āhāraṃ āhārainti| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰…  upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

jīvakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upālisuttaṃ

56. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| taina khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰), nātaputtau (pī॰) nāḷandāyaṃ paṭivasati mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ| atha khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nāḷandāyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina pāvārikambavanaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃvij‍janti khau, tapassi dīghatapassi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), āsanāni; sacai ākaṅkhasi nisīdā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kati pana, tapassi, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau kammāni pañ‍ñapaiti pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau gautama, āciṇṇaṃ nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa ‘kammaṃ, kamma’nti pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘daṇḍaṃ, daṇḍa’nti khau, āvusau gautama, āciṇṇaṃ nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa pañ‍ñapaitu’’nti|

‘‘kati pana, tapassi, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau daṇḍāni pañ‍ñapaiti pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti?

‘‘tīṇi khau, āvusau gautama, nigaṇṭhau  nāṭaputtau daṇḍāni pañ‍ñapaiti pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyāti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāyadaṇḍaṃ, vacīdaṇḍaṃ, manaudaṇḍa’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, tapassi, añ‍ñadaiva kāyadaṇḍaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vacīdaṇḍaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ manaudaṇḍa’’nti?

‘‘añ‍ñadaiva, āvusau gautama, kāyadaṇḍaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vacīdaṇḍaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ manaudaṇḍa’’nti|

‘‘imaisaṃ pana, tapassi, tiṇṇaṃ daṇḍānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivibhattānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivisiṭṭhānaṃ katamaṃ daṇḍaṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau mahāsāvaj‍jataraṃ pañ‍ñapaiti pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, yadi vā kāyadaṇḍaṃ, yadi vā vacīdaṇḍaṃ, yadi vā manaudaṇḍa’’nti?

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, āvusau gautama, tiṇṇaṃ daṇḍānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivibhattānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivisiṭṭhānaṃ kāyadaṇḍaṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau mahāsāvaj‍jataraṃ pañ‍ñapaiti pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍaṃ, nau tathā manaudaṇḍa’’nti|

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, tapassi, vadaisi’’?

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, āvusau gautama, vadāmi’’|

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, tapassi, vadaisi’’?

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, āvusau gautama, vadāmi’’|

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, tapassi, vadaisi’’?

‘‘kāyadaṇḍanti, āvusau gautama, vadāmī’’ti|

itiha bhagavā dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ yāvatatiyakaṃ patiṭṭhāpaisi|

57. aivaṃ  vuttai, dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tvaṃ panāvusau gautama, kati daṇḍāni pañ‍ñapaisi pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya  pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti?

‘‘na  khau, tapassi, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgatassa ‘daṇḍaṃ, daṇḍa’nti pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘kammaṃ, kamma’nti khau, tapassi, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgatassa pañ‍ñapaitu’’nti?

‘‘tvaṃ panāvusau gautama, kati kammāni pañ‍ñapaisi pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā’’ti?

‘‘tīṇi khau ahaṃ, tapassi, kammāni pañ‍ñapaimi pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, saiyyathidaṃ – kāyakammaṃ, vacīkammaṃ, manaukamma’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau gautama, añ‍ñadaiva kāyakammaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vacīkammaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ manaukamma’’nti?

‘‘añ‍ñadaiva, tapassi, kāyakammaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vacīkammaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ manaukamma’’nti|

‘‘imaisaṃ panāvusau gautama, tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivibhattānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivisiṭṭhānaṃ katamaṃ kammaṃ mahāsāvaj‍jataraṃ pañ‍ñapaisi pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, yadi vā kāyakammaṃ, yadi vā vacīkammaṃ, yadi vā manaukamma’’nti?

‘‘imaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, tapassi, tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivibhattānaṃ aivaṃ paṭivisiṭṭhānaṃ manaukammaṃ mahāsāvaj‍jataraṃ pañ‍ñapaimi pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā kāyakammaṃ, nau tathā vacīkamma’’nti|

‘‘manaukammanti, āvusau gautama, vadaisi’’?

‘‘manaukammanti, tapassi, vadāmi’’|

‘‘manaukammanti, āvusau gautama, vadaisi’’?

‘‘manaukammanti, tapassi, vadāmi’’|

‘‘manaukammanti, āvusau gautama, vadaisi’’?

‘‘manaukammanti, tapassi, vadāmī’’ti|

itiha dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau bhagavantaṃ imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ yāvatatiyakaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami|

58. taina  khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau mahatiyā gihiparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti bālakiniyā parisāya upālipamukhāya| addasā khau nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvāna dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa, kutau nu tvaṃ, tapassi, āgacchasi divā divassā’’ti? ‘‘itau hi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, āgacchāmi samaṇassa gautamassa santikā’’ti| ‘‘ahu pana tai, tapassi, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? ‘‘ahu khau mai, bhantai, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tai, tapassi, ahu samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? atha khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau yāvatakau ahausi bhagavatā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, tapassi! yathā taṃ sutavatā sāvakaina sammadaiva satthusāsanaṃ ājānantaina aivamaiva dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina samaṇassa gautamassa byākataṃ| kiñhi saubhati chavau manaudaṇḍau imassa aivaṃ auḷārikassa kāyadaṇḍassa upanidhāya! atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau’’ti|

59. aivaṃ  vuttai, upāli gahapati nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhantai dīghatapassī tapassī (sī॰ pī॰)! yathā taṃ sutavatā sāvakaina sammadaiva satthusāsanaṃ ājānantaina aivamaivaṃ bhadantaina tapassinā samaṇassa gautamassa byākataṃ| kiñhi saubhati chavau manaudaṇḍau imassa aivaṃ auḷārikassa kāyadaṇḍassa upanidhāya! atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau| handa  cāhaṃ, bhantai, gacchāmi samaṇassa gautamassa imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāmi| sacai mai samaṇau gautamau tathā patiṭṭhahissati yathā bhadantaina tapassinā patiṭṭhāpitaṃ; saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau dīghalaumikaṃ aiḷakaṃ laumaisu gahaitvā ākaḍḍhaiyya parikaḍḍhaiyya samparikaḍḍhaiyya, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ ākaḍḍhissāmi parikaḍḍhissāmi samparikaḍḍhissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma balavā sauṇḍikākammakārau mahantaṃ sauṇḍikākilañ‍jaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai pakkhipitvā kaṇṇai gahaitvā ākaḍḍhaiyya parikaḍḍhaiyya samparikaḍḍhaiyya, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ ākaḍḍhissāmi parikaḍḍhissāmi samparikaḍḍhissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma balavā sauṇḍikādhuttau vālaṃ thālaṃ (ka॰) kaṇṇai gahaitvā audhunaiyya niddhunaiyya nipphauṭaiyya nicchādaiyya (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), nic‍cauṭaiyya (ka॰), nippauṭhaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ audhunissāmi  niddhunissāmi nipphauṭaissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma kuñ‍jarau saṭṭhihāyanau gambhīraṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā sāṇadhauvikaṃ nāma kīḷitajātaṃ kīḷati, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ sāṇadhauvikaṃ mañ‍ñai kīḷitajātaṃ kīḷissāmi| handa cāhaṃ, bhantai, gacchāmi samaṇassa gautamassa imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ vādaṃ āraupaihi| ahaṃ vā hi, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaiyyaṃ, dīghatapassī vā nigaṇṭhau, tvaṃ vā’’ti|

60. aivaṃ vuttai, dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau maitaṃ, bhantai, ruc‍cati yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaiyya| samaṇau hi, bhantai, gautamau māyāvī āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitī’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, tapassi, anavakāsau yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyya| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ samaṇau gautamau upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyya| gaccha, tvaṃ, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ vādaṃ āraupaihi| ahaṃ vā hi, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaiyyaṃ, dīghatapassī vā nigaṇṭhau, tvaṃ vā’’ti| dutiyampi khau dīghatapassī…pai॰… tatiyampi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau maitaṃ, bhantai, ruc‍cati yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ  āraupaiyya| samaṇau hi, bhantai, gautamau māyāvī āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitī’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, tapassi, anavakāsau yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ  upagacchaiyya| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ samaṇau gautamau upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyya| gaccha tvaṃ, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa imasmiṃ kathāvatthusmiṃ vādaṃ āraupaihi| ahaṃ vā hi, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaiyyaṃ, dīghatapassī vā nigaṇṭhau, tvaṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau upāli gahapati nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā  yaina pāvārikambavanaṃ yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau upāli gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamā nu khvidha, bhantai, dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau’’ti?

‘‘āgamā khvidha, gahapati, dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau’’ti|

‘‘ahu khau pana tai, bhantai, dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti?

‘‘ahu khau mai, gahapati, dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tai, bhantai, ahu dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti?

atha khau bhagavā yāvatakau ahausi dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ upālissa gahapatissa āraucaisi|

61. aivaṃ vuttai, upāli gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhantai tapassī! yathā taṃ sutavatā sāvakaina sammadaiva satthusāsanaṃ ājānantaina aivamaivaṃ dīghatapassinā nigaṇṭhaina bhagavatau byākataṃ| kiñhi saubhati chavau manaudaṇḍau imassa aivaṃ auḷārikassa kāyadaṇḍassa upanidhāya? atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa  kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, gahapati, sac‍cai patiṭṭhāya mantaiyyāsi siyā nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘sac‍cai ahaṃ, bhantai, patiṭṭhāya mantaissāmi; hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

62. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, idhassa nigaṇṭhau ābādhikau dukkhitau  bāḷhagilānau sītaudakapaṭikkhittau uṇhaudakapaṭisaivī| sau sītaudakaṃ alabhamānau kālaṅkaraiyya| imassa pana, gahapati, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau katthūpapattiṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti?

‘‘atthi, bhantai, manausattā nāma daivā tattha sau upapaj‍jati’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’?

‘‘asu hi, bhantai, manaupaṭibaddhau kālaṅkarautī’’ti|

‘‘manasi karauhi, gahapati gahapati gahapati manasi karauhi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), gahapati manasi karauhi (ka॰), gahapati gahapati (pī॰), manasi karitvā khau, gahapati, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| bhāsitā khau pana tai, gahapati, aisā vācā – ‘sac‍cai ahaṃ, bhantai, patiṭṭhāya mantaissāmi, hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’’ti| ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha, atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau’’ti|

63. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, idhassa nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau sabbavārivāritau sabbavāriyuttau sabbavāridhutau sabbavāriphuṭau| sau abhik‍kamantau paṭik‍kamantau bahū khuddakai pāṇai saṅghātaṃ āpādaiti| imassa pana, gahapati, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau kaṃ vipākaṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti?

‘‘asañ‍caitanikaṃ, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nau mahāsāvaj‍jaṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti|

‘‘sacai  pana, gahapati, caitaitī’’ti?

‘‘mahāsāvaj‍jaṃ, bhantai, hautī’’ti|

‘‘caitanaṃ pana, gahapati, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau kismiṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti?

‘‘manaudaṇḍasmiṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘manasi karauhi, gahapati, manasi karitvā khau, gahapati, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| bhāsitā khau pana tai, gahapati, aisā vācā – ‘sac‍cai ahaṃ, bhantai, patiṭṭhāya mantaissāmi; hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’’ti| ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha, atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau’’ti|

64. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, ayaṃ nāḷandā iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ākiṇṇamanussā’’ti?
