‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai, ayaṃ nāḷandā iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ākiṇṇamanussā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, idha purisau āgacchaiyya ukkhittāsikau| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ yāvatikā imissā nāḷandāya pāṇā tai aikaina khaṇaina aikaina muhuttaina aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, pahauti nu khau sau purisau yāvatikā imissā nāḷandāya pāṇā tai aikaina khaṇaina aikaina muhuttaina  aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ kātu’’nti?

‘‘dasapi, bhantai, purisā, vīsampi, bhantai, purisā, tiṃsampi, bhantai, purisā, cattārīsampi, bhantai, purisā, pañ‍ñāsampi, bhantai, purisā nappahaunti yāvatikā imissā nāḷandāya pāṇā tai aikaina khaṇaina aikaina muhuttaina aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ kātuṃ| kiñhi saubhati aikau chavau purisau’’ti!

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, idha āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā iddhimā caitauvasippattau| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imaṃ nāḷandaṃ aikaina manaupadausaina bhasmaṃ karissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, pahauti nu khau sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā iddhimā caitauvasippattau imaṃ nāḷandaṃ aikaina manaupadausaina bhasmaṃ kātu’’nti?

‘‘dasapi, bhantai, nāḷandā, vīsampi nāḷandā, tiṃsampi nāḷandā, cattārīsampi nāḷandā, pañ‍ñāsampi nāḷandā pahauti sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā iddhimā caitauvasippattau aikaina manaupadausaina bhasmaṃ kātuṃ| kiñhi saubhati aikā chavā nāḷandā’’ti!

‘‘manasi karauhi, gahapati, manasi karitvā khau, gahapati, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| bhāsitā khau pana tai, gahapati, aisā vācā – ‘sac‍cai ahaṃ, bhantai, patiṭṭhāya mantaissāmi; hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’’ti|

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha, atha khau kāyadaṇḍauva mahāsāvaj‍jatarau pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, nau tathā vacīdaṇḍau, nau tathā manaudaṇḍau’’ti|

65. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, sutaṃ tai daṇḍakīrañ‍ñaṃ daṇḍakārañ‍ñaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kāliṅgārañ‍ñaṃ majjhārañ‍ñaṃ maijjhārañ‍ñaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mātaṅgārañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñabhūta’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai, sutaṃ mai daṇḍakīrañ‍ñaṃ kāliṅgārañ‍ñaṃ majjhārañ‍ñaṃ mātaṅgārañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñabhūta’’nti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, kinti tai sutaṃ kaina taṃ daṇḍakīrañ‍ñaṃ kāliṅgārañ‍ñaṃ majjhārañ‍ñaṃ mātaṅgārañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñabhūta’’nti?

‘‘sutaṃ  maitaṃ, bhantai, isīnaṃ manaupadausaina taṃ daṇḍakīrañ‍ñaṃ kāliṅgārañ‍ñaṃ majjhārañ‍ñaṃ mātaṅgārañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñabhūta’’nti|

‘‘manasi karauhi, gahapati, manasi karitvā khau, gahapati, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| bhāsitā khau pana tai, gahapati, aisā vācā – ‘sac‍cai ahaṃ, bhantai, patiṭṭhāya mantaissāmi; hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’’ti|

66. ‘‘purimainaivāhaṃ, bhantai, aupammaina bhagavatau attamanau abhiraddhau| api cāhaṃ imāni bhagavatau vicitrāni pañhapaṭibhānāni sautukāmau, aivāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ pac‍canīkaṃ kātabbaṃ amañ‍ñissaṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca  bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

67. ‘‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, gahapati, karauhi, anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ  ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, gahapati, karauhi, anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| mañhi, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā sāvakaṃ labhitvā kaivalakappaṃ nāḷandaṃ paṭākaṃ pariharaiyyuṃ – ‘upāli amhākaṃ gahapati sāvakattaṃ upagatau’ti| atha ca pana maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, gahapati, karauhi, anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, dutiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

68. ‘‘dīgharattaṃ khau tai, gahapati, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ yaina naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau  abhiraddhau yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau tai, gahapati, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ yaina naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’ti| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – ‘mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ  din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti| atha ca pana maṃ bhagavā nigaṇṭhaisupi dānai samādapaiti| api ca, bhantai, mayamaittha kālaṃ jānissāma| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, tatiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

69. atha  khau bhagavā upālissa gahapatissa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ ānupubbīkathaṃ (sī॰), ānupubbikathaṃ (pī॰), anupubbikathaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi upāliṃ gahapatiṃ kal‍lacittaṃ  muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva upālissa gahapatissa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti| atha khau upāli gahapati diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma, bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, gahapati, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

70. atha khau upāli gahapati bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina sakaṃ nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā dauvārikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aj‍jataggai, samma dauvārika, āvarāmi dvāraṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ nigaṇṭhīnaṃ, anāvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ bhagavatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| sacai kauci nigaṇṭhau āgacchati tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘tiṭṭha, bhantai, mā pāvisi| aj‍jataggai upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau| āvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ nigaṇṭhīnaṃ, anāvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ bhagavatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| sacai tai, bhantai, piṇḍakaina atthau, aitthaiva tiṭṭha, aitthaiva tai āharissantī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau dauvārikau upālissa gahapatissa pac‍cassausi|

71. assausi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau – ‘‘upāli kira gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau’’ti| atha khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, upāli kira gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, tapassi, anavakāsau yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyya| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ samaṇau gautamau upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai  …pai॰… upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘handāhaṃ, bhantai, gacchāmi yāva jānāmi yadi vā upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau yadi vā nau’’ti| ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, tapassi, jānāhi yadi vā upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau yadi vā nau’’ti|

72. atha khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau yaina upālissa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau dauvārikau dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna dīghatapassiṃ nigaṇṭhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭha, bhantai, mā pāvisi| aj‍jataggai upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau| āvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ nigaṇṭhīnaṃ, anāvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ bhagavatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| sacai tai, bhantai, piṇḍakaina atthau, aitthaiva tiṭṭha, aitthaiva tai āharissantī’’ti| ‘‘na mai, āvusau, piṇḍakaina atthau’’ti vatvā tatau paṭinivattitvā yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃyaiva khau, bhantai, yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau| aitaṃ khau tai ahaṃ, bhantai, nālatthaṃ na khau mai, bhantai, ruc‍cati yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaiyya| samaṇau hi, bhantai, gautamau māyāvī āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitīti| āvaṭṭau khau tai, bhantai, upāli gahapati samaṇaina gautamaina āvaṭṭaniyā māyāyā’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, tapassi, anavakāsau  yaṃ upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyya| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ samaṇau gautamau upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃyaiva, bhantai…pai॰…  upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau dīghatapassī nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃyaiva khau, bhantai…pai॰…  upālissa gahapatissa sāvakattaṃ upagacchaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘handa cāhaṃ, tapassi, gacchāmi yāva cāhaṃ sāmaṃyaiva jānāmi yadi vā upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau yadi vā nau’’ti|

atha khau nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ yaina upālissa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau dauvārikau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭha, bhantai, mā pāvisi| aj‍jataggai upāli gahapati samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakattaṃ upagatau| āvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ nigaṇṭhīnaṃ, anāvaṭaṃ dvāraṃ bhagavatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| sacai tai, bhantai, piṇḍakaina atthau, aitthaiva tiṭṭha, aitthaiva tai āharissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma dauvārika, yaina upāli gahapati tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā upāliṃ gahapatiṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau; sau tai dassanakāmau’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau dauvārikau nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa paṭissutvā yaina upāli gahapati tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā upāliṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ  bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau; sau tai dassanakāmau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma dauvārika, majjhimāya dvārasālāya āsanāni pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau dauvārikau upālissa gahapatissa paṭissutvā majjhimāya dvārasālāya āsanāni pañ‍ñapaitvā yaina upāli gahapati tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā upāliṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍ñattāni khau, bhantai, majjhimāya dvārasālāya āsanāni| yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

73. atha khau upāli gahapati yaina majjhimā  dvārasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā yaṃ tattha āsanaṃ aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca tattha sāmaṃ nisīditvā dauvārikaṃ āmantaisi  – ‘‘taina hi, samma dauvārika, yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘upāli, bhantai, gahapati aivamāha – pavisa kira, bhantai, sacai ākaṅkhasī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau dauvārikau upālissa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘upāli, bhantai, gahapati aivamāha – ‘pavisa kira, bhantai, sacai ākaṅkhasī’’’ti| atha  khau nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ yaina majjhimā dvārasālā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau upāli gahapati – yaṃ sudaṃ pubbai yatau passati nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ disvāna tatau pac‍cuggantvā yaṃ tattha āsanaṃ aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca taṃ uttarāsaṅgaina  sammaj‍jitvā pamaj‍jitvā (sī॰ pī॰) pariggahaitvā nisīdāpaiti sau – dāni yaṃ tattha āsanaṃ aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca tattha sāmaṃ nisīditvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃvij‍janti khau, bhantai, āsanāni; sacai ākaṅkhasi, nisīdā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau upāliṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ummattausi tvaṃ, gahapati, dattausi tvaṃ, gahapati! ‘gacchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’ti gantvā mahatāsi vādasaṅghāṭaina paṭimuk‍kau āgatau| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, purisau aṇḍahārakau gantvā ubbhataihi aṇḍaihi āgacchaiyya, saiyyathā vā pana gahapati purisau akkhikahārakau gantvā ubbhataihi akkhīhi āgacchaiyya; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, gahapati, ‘gacchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’ti gantvā mahatāsi vādasaṅghāṭaina paṭimuk‍kau āgatau| āvaṭṭausi khau tvaṃ, gahapati, samaṇaina gautamaina āvaṭṭaniyā māyāyā’’ti|

74. ‘‘bhaddikā, bhantai, āvaṭṭanī māyā; kalyāṇī, bhantai, āvaṭṭanī māyā; piyā mai, bhantai, ñātisālauhitā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ; piyānampi mai assa ñātisālauhitānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya; sabbai caipi, bhantai, khattiyā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ; sabbaisānampissa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya  sukhāya; sabbai caipi, bhantai, brāhmaṇā…pai॰… vaissā…pai॰… suddā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ; sabbaisānampissa suddānaṃ  dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya; sadaivakau caipi, bhantai, laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ; sadaivakassapissa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā  pajāya sadaivamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyāti| taina hi, bhantai, upamaṃ tai karissāmi| upamāya pidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti|

75. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa jiṇṇassa vuḍḍhassa mahal‍lakassa daharā māṇavikā pajāpatī ahausi gabbhinī upavijañ‍ñā| atha khau, bhantai, sā māṇavikā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ kiṇitvā ānaihi, yau mai kumārakassa kīḷāpanakau bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, sau brāhmaṇau taṃ māṇavikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘āgamaihi tāva, bhauti, yāva vijāyati| sacai tvaṃ, bhauti, kumārakaṃ vijāyissasi, tassā tai ahaṃ āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ kiṇitvā ānaissāmi, yau tai kumārakassa kīḷāpanakau bhavissati| sacai pana tvaṃ, bhauti, kumārikaṃ vijāyissasi, tassā tai ahaṃ āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpikaṃ kiṇitvā ānaissāmi, yā tai kumārikāya kīḷāpanikā bhavissatī’ti| dutiyampi khau, bhantai, sā māṇavikā…pai॰…  tatiyampi khau, bhantai, sā māṇavikā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ kiṇitvā ānaihi, yau mai kumārakassa kīḷāpanakau bhavissatī’ti| atha khau, bhantai, sau brāhmaṇau tassā māṇavikāya sārattau paṭibaddhacittau āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ kiṇitvā ānaitvā taṃ māṇavikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘ayaṃ tai, bhauti, āpaṇā mak‍kaṭacchāpakau  kiṇitvā ānītau, yau tai kumārakassa kīḷāpanakau bhavissatī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sā māṇavikā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ ādāya yaina rattapāṇi rajataputtau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā rattapāṇiṃ rajakaputtaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – icchāmahaṃ, samma rattapāṇi, imaṃ mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ pītāvalaipanaṃ nāma raṅgajātaṃ rajitaṃ ākauṭitapac‍cākauṭitaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭha’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhantai, sau brāhmaṇau tassā māṇavikāya sārattau paṭibaddhacittau taṃ mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ ādāya yaina rattapāṇi  rajakaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rattapāṇiṃ rajakaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘icchāmahaṃ, samma rattapāṇi, imaṃ mak‍kaṭacchāpakaṃ pītāvalaipanaṃ nāma raṅgajātaṃ rajitaṃ ākauṭitapac‍cākauṭitaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, rattapāṇi rajakaputtau taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘ayaṃ khau tai, mak‍kaṭacchāpakau raṅgakkhamau hi khau, nau ākauṭanakkhamau, nau vimaj‍janakkhamau’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, bālānaṃ nigaṇṭhānaṃ vādau raṅgakkhamau  hi khau bālānaṃ nau paṇḍitānaṃ, nau anuyaugakkhamau, nau vimaj‍janakkhamau| atha khau, bhantai, sau brāhmaṇau aparaina samayaina navaṃ dussayugaṃ ādāya yaina rattapāṇi rajakaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rattapāṇiṃ rajakaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘icchāmahaṃ, samma rattapāṇi, imaṃ navaṃ dussayugaṃ pītāvalaipanaṃ nāma raṅgajātaṃ rajitaṃ ākauṭitapac‍cākauṭitaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, rattapāṇi rajakaputtau taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘idaṃ khau tai, bhantai, navaṃ dussayugaṃ raṅgakkhamañ‍caiva ākauṭanakkhamañ‍ca vimaj‍janakkhamañ‍cā’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, tassa bhagavatau vādau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa raṅgakkhamau caiva paṇḍitānaṃ nau bālānaṃ, anuyaugakkhamau ca vimaj‍janakkhamau cā’’ti|

‘‘sarājikā khau, gahapati, parisā aivaṃ jānāti – ‘upāli gahapati nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa sāvakau’ti| kassa taṃ, gahapati, sāvakaṃ dhāraimā’’ti? aivaṃ  vuttai, upāli gahapati uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ  paṇāmaitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, suṇauhi yassāhaṃ sāvakau’’ti –

76.

 ‘‘dhīrassa vigatamauhassa, pabhin‍nakhīlassa vijitavijayassa|

anīghassa susamacittassa, vuddhasīlassa sādhupañ‍ñassa|

vaisamantarassa vaissantarassa (sī॰ pī॰) vimalassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘akathaṃkathissa tusitassa, vantalaukāmisassa muditassa|

katasamaṇassa manujassa, antimasārīrassa narassa|

anaupamassa virajassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘asaṃsayassa kusalassa, vainayikassa sārathivarassa|

anuttarassa ruciradhammassa, nik‍kaṅkhassa pabhāsakassa pabhāsakarassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

mānacchidassa vīrassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘nisabhassa  appamaiyyassa, gambhīrassa maunapattassa|

khaimaṅkarassa vaidassa, dhammaṭṭhassa saṃvutattassa|

saṅgātigassa muttassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘nāgassa  pantasainassa, khīṇasaṃyaujanassa muttassa|

paṭimantakassa paṭimantassa (ka॰) dhaunassa, pan‍nadhajassa vītarāgassa|

dantassa nippapañ‍cassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘isisattamassa akuhassa, taivij‍jassa brahmapattassa|

nhātakassa nahātakassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) padakassa, passaddhassa viditavaidassa|

purindadassa sak‍kassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘ariyassa bhāvitattassa, pattipattassa vaiyyākaraṇassa|

satimatau vipassissa, anabhinatassa nau apanatassa|

anaijassa vasippattassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘samuggatassa sammaggatassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) jhāyissa, ananugatantarassa suddhassa|

asitassa hitassa appahīnassa (sī॰ pī॰), appabhītassa (syā॰), pavivittassa aggappattassa|

tiṇṇassa tārayantassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘santassa bhūripañ‍ñassa, mahāpañ‍ñassa vītalaubhassa|

tathāgatassa sugatassa, appaṭipuggalassa asamassa|

visāradassa nipuṇassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmi||

‘‘taṇhacchidassa buddhassa, vītadhūmassa anupalittassa|

āhunaiyyassa yakkhassa, uttamapuggalassa atulassa|

mahatau yasaggapattassa, bhagavatau tassa sāvakauhamasmī’’ti||

77. ‘‘kadā  sañ‍ñūḷhā pana tai, gahapati, imai samaṇassa gautamassa vaṇṇā’’ti? ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nānāpupphānaṃ mahāpuppharāsi, tamainaṃ dakkhau mālākārau vā mālākārantaivāsī vā vicittaṃ mālaṃ ganthaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, sau bhagavā anaikavaṇṇau anaikasatavaṇṇau| kau hi, bhantai, vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ na karissatī’’ti? atha khau nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa bhagavatau sak‍kāraṃ asahamānassa tatthaiva uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggacchīti uggañchi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

upālisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kuk‍kuravatikasuttaṃ

78. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kauliyaisu viharati haliddavasanaṃ nāma kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau puṇṇau ca kauliyaputtau gauvatikau acailau ca sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| acailau pana sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā kuk‍kurauva palikuj‍jitvā palikuṇṭhitvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), paliguṇṭhitvā (ka॰) aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau duk‍karakārakau chamānikkhittaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati| tassa taṃ kuk‍kuravataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, puṇṇa, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| dutiyampi khau puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau duk‍karakārakau chamānikkhittaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati| tassa taṃ kuk‍kuravataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

79. ‘‘addhā khau tai ahaṃ, puṇṇa, na labhāmi| alaṃ, puṇṇa, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchīti; api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| idha, puṇṇa, aikac‍cau kuk‍kuravataṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kurasīlaṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kuracittaṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kurākappaṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ| sau kuk‍kuravataṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kurasīlaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kuracittaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, kuk‍kurākappaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā kuk‍kurānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| sacai khau panassa aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, sāssa sāyaṃ (ka॰) hauti micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhissa micchādiṭṭhikassa (sī॰) khau ahaṃ, puṇṇa, dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi  – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| iti khau, puṇṇa, sampaj‍jamānaṃ  kuk‍kuravataṃ kuk‍kurānaṃ sahabyataṃ upanaiti, vipaj‍jamānaṃ niraya’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau paraudi, assūni pavattaisi|

atha khau bhagavā puṇṇaṃ kauliyaputtaṃ gauvatikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitaṃ  khau tai ahaṃ, puṇṇa, nālatthaṃ| alaṃ, puṇṇa, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, aitaṃ raudāmi yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha; api ca mai idaṃ, bhantai, kuk‍kuravataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| ayaṃ, bhantai, puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau| tassa taṃ gauvataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, sainiya, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| dutiyampi khau acailau sainiyau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau| tassa taṃ gauvataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

80. ‘‘addhā khau tai ahaṃ, sainiya, na labhāmi| alaṃ, sainiya, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchīti; api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| idha, sainiya, aikac‍cau gauvataṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gausīlaṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gaucittaṃ bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gavākappaṃ gvākappaṃ (ka॰) bhāvaiti paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ| sau gauvataṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gausīlaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gaucittaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ, gavākappaṃ bhāvaitvā paripuṇṇaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gun‍naṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| sacai khau  panassa aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhissa khau ahaṃ, sainiya, dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| iti khau, sainiya, sampaj‍jamānaṃ gauvataṃ gun‍naṃ sahabyataṃ upanaiti, vipaj‍jamānaṃ niraya’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau paraudi, assūni pavattaisi|

atha khau bhagavā acailaṃ sainiyaṃ kuk‍kuravatikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitaṃ khau tai ahaṃ, sainiya, nālatthaṃ| alaṃ, sainiya, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ  aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, aitaṃ raudāmi yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha; api ca mai idaṃ, bhantai, gauvataṃ dīgharattaṃ samattaṃ samādin‍naṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati; pahauti bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathā ahaṃ caivimaṃ gauvataṃ pajahaiyyaṃ, ayañ‍caiva acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau taṃ kuk‍kuravataṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, puṇṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

81. ‘‘cattārimāni, puṇṇa, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, puṇṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, puṇṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ, kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, puṇṇa, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhaṃ sabyāpajjhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti aikantadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi sattā nairayikā| iti khau, puṇṇa, bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hauti; yaṃ karauti taina upapaj‍jati, upapan‍namainaṃ phassā phusanti| aivaṃpāhaṃ, puṇṇa, ‘kammadāyādā sattā’ti vadāmi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, puṇṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, puṇṇa, aikac‍cau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ  samānaṃ abyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau abyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau  samānau abyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti aikantasukhaṃ, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| iti khau, puṇṇa, bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hauti; yaṃ karauti taina upapaj‍jati, upapan‍namainaṃ phassā phusanti| aivaṃpāhaṃ, puṇṇa, ‘kammadāyādā sattā’ti vadāmi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, puṇṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, puṇṇa, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhāpi abyābajjhāpi phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihipi abyābajjhaihipi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vaidanaṃ vaidaiti vaukiṇṇasukhadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| iti khau, puṇṇa, bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hauti; yaṃ karauti taina upapaj‍jati| upapan‍namainaṃ phassā phusanti| aivaṃpāhaṃ, puṇṇa, ‘kammadāyādā sattā’ti vadāmi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, puṇṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, puṇṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ, kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? tatra, puṇṇa, yamidaṃ  kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ yampidaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ yampidaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, puṇṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ, kammakkhayāya saṃvattatīti| imāni khau, puṇṇa, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti|

82. aivaṃ vuttai, puṇṇau kauliyaputtau gauvatikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ  saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| acailau  pana sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai…pai॰… pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘yau khau, sainiya, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ sau cattārau māsai parivasati| catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| api ca maittha puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhantā pabbaj‍jaṃ ākaṅkhantā upasampadaṃ tai cattārau māsai parivasanti catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya, ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi| catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu, upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti| alattha khau acailau sainiyau kuk‍kuravatikau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā sainiyau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau  ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā sainiyau arahataṃ ahausīti|

kuk‍kuravatikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. abhayarājakumārasuttaṃ

83. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau abhayau rājakumārau yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, rājakumāra, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ  āraupaihi| aivaṃ tai kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchissati – ‘abhayaina rājakumāraina samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ mahiddhikassa aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa vādau āraupitau’’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ panāhaṃ, bhantai, samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ mahiddhikassa aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’’ti? ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, rājakumāra, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘bhāsaiyya nu khau, bhantai, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā’ti? sacai tai samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarauti – ‘bhāsaiyya, rājakumāra, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā’ti, tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘atha kiñ‍carahi tai, bhantai, puthuj‍janaina nānākaraṇaṃ? puthuj‍janaupi hi taṃ vācaṃ bhāsaiyya yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā’ti| sacai  pana tai samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarauti – ‘na, rājakumāra, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ bhāsaiyya yā sā vācā paraisaṃ  appiyā amanāpā’ti, tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘atha kiñ‍carahi tai, bhantai, daivadattau byākatau – ‘‘āpāyikau daivadattau, nairayikau daivadattau, kappaṭṭhau daivadattau, ataikicchau daivadattau’’ti? tāya ca pana tai vācāya daivadattau kupitau ahausi anattamanau’ti| imaṃ khau tai, rājakumāra, samaṇau gautamau ubhataukauṭikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau naiva sakkhiti uggilituṃ na sakkhiti augilituṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma purisassa ayausiṅghāṭakaṃ kaṇṭhai vilaggaṃ, sau naiva sak‍kuṇaiyya uggilituṃ na sak‍kuṇaiyya augilituṃ; aivamaiva khau tai, rājakumāra, samaṇau gautamau imaṃ ubhataukauṭikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau naiva sakkhiti uggilituṃ na sakkhiti augilitu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau abhayau rājakumārau nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

84. aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nassa khau abhayassa rājakumārassa sūriyaṃ suriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ul‍laukaitvā aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau aj‍ja bhagavatau vādaṃ āraupaituṃ| svai dānāhaṃ sakai nivaisanai bhagavatau vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’’ti bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya attacatutthau bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha  khau abhayau rājakumārau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina abhayassa rājakumārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau abhayau rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau abhayau rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

85. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau abhayau rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhāsaiyya nu khau, bhantai, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā’’ti? ‘‘na khvaittha, rājakumāra, aikaṃsainā’’ti| ‘‘aittha, bhantai, anassuṃ nigaṇṭhā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, rājakumāra, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘aittha, bhantai, anassuṃ nigaṇṭhā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau maṃ, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau aitadavauca – ‘aihi tvaṃ, rājakumāra, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaihi| aivaṃ tai kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchissati – abhayaina rājakumāraina samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ mahiddhikassa aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa vādau āraupitau’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā kathaṃ panāhaṃ, bhantai, samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ mahiddhikassa aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’ti? ‘aihi tvaṃ, rājakumāra, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – bhāsaiyya nu khau, bhantai, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpāti? sacai tai samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarauti – bhāsaiyya, rājakumāra, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpāti, tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – atha kiñ‍carahi tai, bhantai, puthuj‍janaina nānākaraṇaṃ? puthuj‍janaupi hi taṃ vācaṃ bhāsaiyya  yā sā vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpāti| sacai pana tai samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarauti – na, rājakumāra, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ bhāsaiyya yā sā  vācā paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpāti, tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – atha kiñ‍carahi tai, bhantai, daivadattau byākatau – āpāyikau daivadattau, nairayikau daivadattau, kappaṭṭhau daivadattau, ataikicchau daivadattauti? tāya ca pana tai vācāya daivadattau kupitau ahausi anattamanauti| imaṃ khau tai, rājakumāra, samaṇau gautamau ubhataukauṭikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau  naiva sakkhiti uggilituṃ na sakkhiti augilituṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma purisassa ayausiṅghāṭakaṃ kaṇṭhai vilaggaṃ, sau naiva sak‍kuṇaiyya uggilituṃ na sak‍kuṇaiyya augilituṃ; aivamaiva khau tai, rājakumāra, samaṇau gautamau imaṃ ubhataukauṭikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau naiva sakkhiti uggilituṃ na sakkhiti augilitu’’’nti|

86. taina khau pana samayaina daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau abhayassa rājakumārassa aṅkai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ  mañ‍ñasi, rājakumāra, sacāyaṃ kumārau tuyhaṃ vā pamādamanvāya dhātiyā vā pamādamanvāya kaṭṭhaṃ vā kaṭhalaṃ kathalaṃ (ka॰) vā mukhai āharaiyya, kinti naṃ karaiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘āharaiyyassāhaṃ, bhantai| sacai, bhantai, na sak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ ādikainaiva āhattuṃ āharituṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vāmaina hatthaina sīsaṃ pariggahaitvā paggahaitvā (sī॰) dakkhiṇaina hatthaina vaṅkaṅguliṃ karitvā salauhitampi āharaiyyaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? atthi mai, bhantai, kumārai anukampā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, rājakumāra, yaṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| yampi tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| yañ‍ca khau tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāya| yaṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ  anatthasaṃhitaṃ sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| yampi tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| yañ‍ca tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ sā  ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāya| taṃ kissa haitu? atthi, rājakumāra, tathāgatassa sattaisu anukampā’’ti|

87. ‘‘yaimai, bhantai, khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi samaṇapaṇḍitāpi pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā tathāgataṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchanti, pubbaiva nu khau, aitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau caitasau parivitak‍kitaṃ hauti ‘yai maṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ pucchissanti taisāhaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissāmī’ti, udāhu ṭhānasauvaitaṃ tathāgataṃ paṭibhātī’’ti?

‘‘taina hi, rājakumāra, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi, yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rājakumāra, kusalau tvaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgāna’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai, kusalau ahaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgāna’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rājakumāra, yai taṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ nāmidaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅga’nti? pubbaiva nu khau tai aitaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kitaṃ  assa ‘yai maṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ pucchissanti taisāhaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissāmī’ti, udāhu ṭhānasauvaitaṃ paṭibhāsaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘ahañhi, bhantai, rathikau sañ‍ñātau kusalau rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ| sabbāni mai rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgāni suviditāni| ṭhānasauvaitaṃ maṃ paṭibhāsaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, rājakumāra, yai tai khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi samaṇapaṇḍitāpi pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā tathāgataṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchanti, ṭhānasauvaitaṃ tathāgataṃ paṭibhāti| taṃ kissa haitu? sā hi, rājakumāra, tathāgatassa dhammadhātu suppaṭividdhā yassā dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā ṭhānasauvaitaṃ tathāgataṃ paṭibhātī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, abhayau rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

abhayarājakumārasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bahuvaidanīyasuttaṃ

88. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai udāyi, vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘tissau khau, thapati gahapati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā  khau, thapati, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā; dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, gahapati, dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā; tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, thapati, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| dutiyampi khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā; dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, thapati, dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā; tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, thapati, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā, dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| naiva khau sakkhi āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ na panāsakkhi pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ|

89. assausi  khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmatau udāyissa pañ‍cakaṅgaina thapatinā saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau ahausi āyasmatau udāyissa pañ‍cakaṅgaina thapatinā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santañ‍ñaiva khau, ānanda, pariyāyaṃ pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati udāyissa nābbhanumaudi, santañ‍ñaiva  ca pana pariyāyaṃ udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa nābbhanumaudi| dvaipānanda, vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, tissaupi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, pañ‍capi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, chapi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, aṭṭhārasapi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, chattiṃsapi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina, aṭṭhasatampi vaidanā vuttā mayā pariyāyaina| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau khau, ānanda, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, ānanda, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ na samanujānissanti na samanumañ‍ñissanti na samanumaudissanti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharissanti| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau khau, ānanda, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, ānanda, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ samanujānissanti samanumañ‍ñissanti samanumaudissanti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharissanti’’|

90. ‘‘pañ‍ca khau imai, ānanda, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā  piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, ānanda, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, ānanda, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aitaparamaṃ sattā sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti, idamassa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ  sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aitaparamaṃ sattā sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti, idamassa nānujānāmi| taṃ  kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā, paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ  atthaṅgamā, nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau  khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya…pai॰…| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘yau khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aitaparamaṃ sattā sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti, idamassa nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

91. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ  – ‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samaṇau gautamau āha; tañ‍ca sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| tayidaṃ kiṃsu, tayidaṃ kathaṃsū’ti? aivaṃvādinau, ānanda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘na khau, āvusau, bhagavā sukhaṃyaiva vaidanaṃ sandhāya sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti; api ca, āvusau, yattha yattha sukhaṃ upalabbhati yahiṃ yahiṃ taṃ taṃ tathāgatau sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

bahuvaidanīyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. apaṇṇakasuttaṃ

92. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina sālā nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| assausuṃ khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā  bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ sālaṃ  anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti| atha khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu; sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai  bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

93. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau sālaiyyakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi pana vau, gahapatayau, kauci manāpau satthā yasmiṃ vau ākāravatī saddhā paṭiladdhā’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau nau, bhantai, kauci manāpau satthā yasmiṃ nau ākāravatī saddhā paṭiladdhā’’ti| ‘‘manāpaṃ vau, gahapatayau, satthāraṃ alabhantaihi ayaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau samādāya vattitabbau| apaṇṇakau hi, gahapatayau, dhammau samattau samādin‍nau, sau vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| katamau ca, gahapatayau, apaṇṇakau dhammau’’?

94. ‘‘santi, gahapatayau, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ; natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ sukaṭaduk‍kaṭānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau; natthi mātā, natthi pitā; natthi sattā aupapātikā; natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā samaggatā (ka॰) sammā paṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| taisaṃyaiva khau, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ  aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā  ujuvipac‍canīkavādā| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ; atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau; atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau; atthi mātā, atthi pitā; atthi sattā  aupapātikā; atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammā paṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, gahapatayau – ‘nanumai samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

95. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā abhinibbaj‍jaitvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), abhinibbij‍jitvā (ka॰) yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau akusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā passanti akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘natthi parau laukau’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘natthi parau laukau’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti micchāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘natthi parau laukau’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti micchāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘natthi parau laukau’ti āha; yai tai arahantau paralaukavidunau taisamayaṃ pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘natthi parau laukau’ti paraṃ  sañ‍ñāpaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti (ka॰); sāssa hauti asaddhammasañ‍ñatti assaddhammapañ‍ñatti (ka॰)| tāya ca pana asaddhammasañ‍ñattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa susīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, dussīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvācā ariyānaṃ pac‍canīkatā asaddhammasañ‍ñatti attuk‍kaṃsanā paravambhanā| aivamassimai aivaṃ’si’mai’ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau natthi parau laukau aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā sautthimattānaṃ karissati; sacai khau atthi parau laukau aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu parau laukau, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau – dussīlau purisapuggalau micchādiṭṭhi natthikavādau’ti| sacai khau atthaiva parau laukau, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaliggahau – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau dussamattau samādin‍nau, aikaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati kusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

96. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau akusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā yamidaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? passanti hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘atthi parau laukau’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘atthi parau laukau’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti sammāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘atthi parau laukau’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti sammāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘atthi parau laukau’ti āha; yai tai arahantau paralaukavidunau taisamayaṃ na pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana paraṃ laukaṃ ‘atthi parau laukau’ti paraṃ  sañ‍ñāpaiti; sāssa hauti saddhammasañ‍ñatti| tāya ca pana saddhammasañ‍ñattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa dussīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, susīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā ariyānaṃ apac‍canīkatā saddhammasañ‍ñatti anattuk‍kaṃsanā  aparavambhanā| aivamassimai anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau atthi parau laukau, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu parau laukau, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau – sīlavā purisapuggalau sammādiṭṭhi atthikavādau’ti| sacai khau atthaiva parau laukau, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaṭaggahau – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau susamattau samādin‍nau, ubhayaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati akusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

97. ‘‘santi, gahapatayau, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau pāṇamatimāpayatau (sī॰ pī॰), pāṇamatipātāpayatau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pāṇamatipāpayatau (ka॰), adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā  bhaṇatau; karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau, chindantau chaidāpaintau, pacantau pācaintau; natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau; natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina sac‍cavācaina (ka॰) natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| taisaṃyaiva khau, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ujuvipac‍canīkavādā  tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau  saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau; karautau karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau, chindantau chaidāpaintau, pacantau pācaintau; atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau  yajāpaintau; atthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina  damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina atthi puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, gahapatayau, nanumai samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

98. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau; karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau…pai॰… dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā yamidaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau akusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā passanti akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai  ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘natthi kiriyā’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘natthi kiriyā’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti  micchāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘natthi kiriyā’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti micchāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘natthi kiriyā’ti āha, yai tai arahantau kiriyavādā taisamayaṃ pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘natthi kiriyā’ti paraṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti; sāssa hauti asaddhammasañ‍ñatti| tāya ca pana asaddhammasañ‍ñattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa susīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, dussīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca  micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvācā ariyānaṃ pac‍canīkatā asaddhammasañ‍ñatti attuk‍kaṃsanā paravambhanā| aivamassimai anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau natthi kiriyā, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā sautthimattānaṃ karissati; sacai khau atthi kiriyā aivamayaṃ  bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu kiriyā, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau – dussīlau purisapuggalau  micchādiṭṭhi akiriyavādau’ti| sacai khau atthaiva kiriyā, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaliggahau – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau dussamattau samādin‍nau, aikaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati kusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

99. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau; karautau karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau, chindantau  chaidāpaintau, pacantau pācaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina atthi puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau akusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā yamidaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? passanti hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘atthi kiriyā’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘atthi kiriyā’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti sammāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘atthi kiriyā’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti sammāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘atthi kiriyā’ti āha; yai tai arahantau kiriyavādā taisamayaṃ na pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana kiriyaṃ ‘atthi kiriyā’ti paraṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti; sāssa  hauti saddhammasañ‍ñatti| tāya  ca pana saddhammasañ‍ñattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa dussīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, susīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā ariyānaṃ apac‍canīkatā saddhammasañ‍ñatti anattuk‍kaṃsanā aparavambhanā| aivamassimai anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau atthi kiriyā, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu  kiriyā, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau – sīlavā purisapuggalau sammādiṭṭhi kiriyavādau’ti| sacai khau atthaiva kiriyā, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaṭaggahau – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau susamattau samādin‍nau, ubhayaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati akusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

100. ‘‘santi, gahapatayau, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| natthi balaṃ, natthi vīriyaṃ viriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), natthi purisathāmau, natthi purisaparak‍kamau; sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṃgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti| taisaṃyaiva khau, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ujuvipac‍canīkavādā| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘atthi haitu, atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| atthi haitu, atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| atthi balaṃ, atthi vīriyaṃ, atthi  purisathāmau, atthi purisaparak‍kamau; na sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā atthi purisaparak‍kamau, sabbai sattā… savasā sabalā savīriyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) niyatisaṃgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, gahapatayau, nanumai  samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’ti? ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’|

101. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| natthi balaṃ, natthi vīriyaṃ, natthi purisathāmau, natthi purisaparak‍kamau; sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṃgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā yamidaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau akusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā passanti akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘natthi  haitū’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘natthi haitū’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti micchāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘natthi haitū’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti micchāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘natthi haitū’ti āha; yai tai arahantau haituvādā taisamayaṃ pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘natthi haitū’ti paraṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti; sāssa hauti asaddhammasañ‍ñatti| tāya ca pana asaddhammasañ‍ñattiyā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa susīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, dussīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvācā ariyānaṃ pac‍canīkatā asaddhammasañ‍ñatti attānuk‍kaṃsanā paravambhanā| aivamassimai anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau natthi haitu, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sautthimattānaṃ karissati; sacai khau atthi haitu, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu haitu, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau – dussīlau purisapuggalau micchādiṭṭhi ahaitukavādau’ti| sacai khau atthaiva haitu, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaliggahau  – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ gārayhau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ  maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau dussamattau samādin‍nau, aikaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati kusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

102. ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi haitu, atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| atthi haitu, atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| atthi balaṃ, atthi vīriyaṃ, atthi purisathāmau, atthi purisaparak‍kamau; na sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṃgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti taisamaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ? yamidaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai tayau  akusalai dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā yamidaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ – imai tayau kusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? passanti hi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘atthi haitū’ tissa diṭṭhi hauti; sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘atthi haitū’ti saṅkappaiti; svāssa hauti sammāsaṅkappau| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘atthi haitū’ti vācaṃ bhāsati; sāssa hauti sammāvācā| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ  ‘atthi haitū’ti āha, yai tai arahantau haituvādā taisamayaṃ na pac‍canīkaṃ karauti| santaṃyaiva khau pana haituṃ ‘atthi haitū’ti paraṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti; sāssa hauti saddhammasañ‍ñatti| tāya ca pana saddhammasañ‍ñattiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| iti pubbaiva khau panassa dussīlyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, susīlyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ayañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā ariyānaṃ apac‍canīkatā saddhammasañ‍ñatti anattuk‍kaṃsanā aparavambhanā| aivamassimai anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sacai khau atthi haitu, aivamayaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| kāmaṃ khau pana māhu haitu, hautu naisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ; atha ca panāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisapuggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau – sīlavā purisapuggalau sammādiṭṭhi haituvādau’ti| sacai khau atthi haitu, aivaṃ imassa bhautau purisapuggalassa ubhayattha kaṭaggahau  – yañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati| aivamassāyaṃ apaṇṇakau dhammau susamattau samādin‍nau, ubhayaṃsaṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati, riñ‍cati akusalaṃ ṭhānaṃ|

103. ‘‘santi, gahapatayau, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau  – ‘natthi sabbasau āruppā’ti| taisaṃyaiva khau, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ujuvipac‍canīkavādā| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘atthi sabbasau āruppā’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, gahapatayau, nanumai samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau āruppā’ti, idaṃ mai adiṭṭhaṃ; yaipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi sabbasau āruppā’ti, idaṃ mai aviditaṃ| ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau pana ajānantau apassantau aikaṃsaina ādāya vauharaiyyaṃ – idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ñanti, na maitaṃ assa patirūpaṃ| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau āruppā’ti, sacai taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati – yai tai daivā rūpinau manaumayā, apaṇṇakaṃ mai tatrūpapatti bhavissati| yai pana tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi sabbasau āruppā’ti, sacai taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati – yai tai daivā arūpinau sañ‍ñāmayā, apaṇṇakaṃ mai tatrūpapatti bhavissati| dissanti khau pana rūpādhikaraṇaṃ rūpakāraṇā (ka॰) daṇḍādāna-satthādāna-kalaha-viggaha-vivāda-tuvaṃtuvaṃ-paisuñ‍ña-musāvādā| ‘natthi khau panaitaṃ sabbasau arūpai’’’ti| sau iti  paṭisaṅkhāya rūpānaṃyaiva nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

104. ‘‘santi, gahapatayau, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti| taisaṃyaiva khau, gahapatayau, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ujuvipac‍canīkavādā| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘atthi sabbasau  bhavaniraudhau’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, gahapatayau, nanumai samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tatra, gahapatayau, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, idaṃ mai adiṭṭhaṃ; yaipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā  aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, idaṃ mai aviditaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana ajānantau apassantau aikaṃsaina ādāya vauharaiyyaṃ – idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ñanti, na maitaṃ assa patirūpaṃ| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, sacai taisaṃ bhavataṃ  samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati – yai tai daivā arūpinau sañ‍ñāmayā apaṇṇakaṃ mai tatrūpapatti bhavissati| yai pana tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, sacai taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati – yaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyissāmi| yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, taisamayaṃ diṭṭhi sārāgāya sarāgāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) santikai, saṃyaugāya santikai, abhinandanāya santikai, ajjhausānāya santikai, upādānāya santikai| yai pana tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘atthi sabbasau bhavaniraudhau’ti, taisamayaṃ diṭṭhi asārāgāya santikai, asaṃyaugāya santikai, anabhinandanāya santikai, anajjhausānāya santikai, anupādānāya santikai’’’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya bhavānaṃyaiva nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

105. ‘‘cattāraumai, gahapatayau, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; sau anattantapau  aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati|

106. ‘‘katamau ca, gahapatayau, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… vitthārau ma॰ ni॰ 2.6-7 kandarakasuttai iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatayau, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

‘‘katamau  ca, gahapatayau, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau aurabbhikau hauti sūkarikau…pai॰… yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kurūrakammantā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatayau, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

‘‘katamau ca, gahapatayau, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau puggalau rājā vā hauti khattiyau muddhāvasittau…pai॰… taipi daṇḍataj‍jitā bhayataj‍jitā assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni karaunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatayau, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

‘‘katamau ca, gahapatayau, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati? idha, gahapatayau, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau  upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai  pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ  cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| sau aivaṃ samāhitai  cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatayau, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

apaṇṇakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

gahapativaggau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kandaranāgarasaikhavatau  ca, pautaliyau puna jīvakabhac‍cau|

upālidamathau kuk‍kuraabhayau, bahuvaidanīyāpaṇṇakatau dasamau||


2. bhikkhuvaggau

1. ambalaṭṭhikarāhulauvādasuttaṃ

107.  aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā rāhulau ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ viharati| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina ambalaṭṭhikā yaināyasmā rāhulau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaisi, udakañ‍ca pādānaṃ| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaisi| āyasmāpi khau rāhulau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

108. atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ udakāvasaisaṃ udakādhānai ṭhapaitvā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, rāhula, imaṃ parittaṃ udakāvasaisaṃ udakādhānai ṭhapita’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ parittakaṃ khau, rāhula, taisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ yaisaṃ natthi sampajānamusāvādai laj‍jā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ udakāvasaisaṃ chaḍḍaitvā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, rāhula, parittaṃ udakāvasaisaṃ chaḍḍita’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ chaḍḍitaṃ khau, rāhula, taisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ yaisaṃ natthi sampajānamusāvādai laj‍jā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā  taṃ udakādhānaṃ nik‍kuj‍jitvā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, rāhula, imaṃ udakādhānaṃ nik‍kuj‍jita’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ khau, rāhula, taisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ yaisaṃ natthi sampajānamusāvādai laj‍jā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ udakādhānaṃ uk‍kuj‍jitvā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, rāhula, imaṃ udakādhānaṃ rittaṃ tuccha’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ rittaṃ tucchaṃ khau, rāhula, taisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ yaisaṃ natthi  sampajānamusāvādai laj‍jāti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā ubbūḷhavā (sī॰ pī॰) abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau saṅgāmagatau purimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, pacchimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, purimainapi kāyaina kammaṃ karauti, pacchimainapi kāyaina kammaṃ karauti, sīsainapi kammaṃ karauti, kaṇṇaihipi  kammaṃ karauti, dantaihipi kammaṃ karauti, naṅguṭṭhainapi kammaṃ  karauti; rakkhataiva sauṇḍaṃ| tattha hatthārauhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau saṅgāmagatau purimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, pacchimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti…pai॰… naṅguṭṭhainapi kammaṃ karauti; rakkhataiva sauṇḍaṃ| aparic‍cattaṃ khau rañ‍ñau nāgassa jīvita’nti| yatau khau, rāhula, rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau saṅgāmagatau purimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, pacchimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti…pai॰… naṅguṭṭhainapi kammaṃ karauti, sauṇḍāyapi kammaṃ karauti, tattha hatthārauhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau saṅgāmagatau purimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, pacchimaihipi pādaihi kammaṃ karauti, purimainapi kāyaina kammaṃ karauti, pacchimainapi kāyaina kammaṃ karauti, sīsainapi kammaṃ karauti, kaṇṇaihipi kammaṃ karauti, dantaihipi kammaṃ karauti, naṅguṭṭhainapi kammaṃ karauti, sauṇḍāyapi kammaṃ karauti| paric‍cattaṃ khau rañ‍ñau nāgassa jīvitaṃ| natthi dāni kiñ‍ci rañ‍ñau nāgassa akaraṇīya’nti| aivamaiva khau, rāhula, yassa kassaci sampajānamusāvādai natthi laj‍jā, nāhaṃ tassa kiñ‍ci pāpaṃ akaraṇīyanti vadāmi| tasmātiha tai, rāhula, ‘hassāpi na musā bhaṇissāmī’ti – aivañhi tai, rāhula, sikkhitabbaṃ|
