109. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, kimatthiyau ādāsau’’ti? ‘‘pac‍cavaikkhaṇatthau, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāyaina kammaṃ kattabbaṃ, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā vācāya kammaṃ kattabbaṃ, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā manasā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ| yadaiva  tvaṃ, rāhula, kāyaina kammaṃ kattukāmau ahausi, tadaiva tai kāyakammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhundrayaṃ, dukkhudayaṃ (ka॰) dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, kāyaina kammaṃ sasak‍kaṃ na karaṇīyaṃ saṃsak‍kaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ (ka॰)| sacai  pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – kusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, kāyaina kammaṃ karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘karauntainapi tai, rāhula, kāyaina kammaṃ tadaiva tai kāyakammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, paṭisaṃharaiyyāsi tvaṃ, rāhula, aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – kusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, anupadaj‍jaiyyāsi tvaṃ, rāhula, aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ|

‘‘katvāpi tai, rāhula, kāyaina kammaṃ tadaiva tai kāyakammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati saṃvatti (pī॰), parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai khau tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ akāsiṃ, idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, kāyakammaṃ satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu  daisaitabbaṃ, vivaritabbaṃ, uttānīkātabbaṃ; daisaitvā vivaritvā uttānīkatvā āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ  āpaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ kāyaina kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai kāyakammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – kusalaṃ idaṃ kāyakammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, tainaiva tvaṃ, rāhula, pītipāmauj‍jaina viharaiyyāsi ahaurattānusikkhī kusalaisu dhammaisu|

110. ‘‘yadaiva tvaṃ, rāhula, vācāya kammaṃ kattukāmau ahausi, tadaiva tai vacīkammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, vācāya kammaṃ sasak‍kaṃ na karaṇīyaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – kusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, vācāya kammaṃ karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘karauntainapi, rāhula, vācāya kammaṃ tadaiva tai vacīkammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, paṭisaṃharaiyyāsi tvaṃ, rāhula, aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ  naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na  ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati  – kusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, anupadaj‍jaiyyāsi, tvaṃ rāhula, aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ|

‘‘katvāpi tai, rāhula, vācāya kammaṃ tadaiva tai vacīkammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati saṃvatti (sī॰ pī॰), parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai khau tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, vacīkammaṃ satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu daisaitabbaṃ, vivaritabbaṃ, uttānīkattabbaṃ; daisaitvā vivaritvā uttānīkatvā āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ vācāya kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai vacīkammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – kusalaṃ idaṃ vacīkammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, tainaiva tvaṃ, rāhula, pītipāmauj‍jaina viharaiyyāsi ahaurattānusikkhī kusalaisu dhammaisu|

111. ‘‘yadaiva tvaṃ, rāhula, manasā kammaṃ kattukāmau ahausi, tadaiva tai manaukammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, manasā kammaṃ sasak‍kaṃ na karaṇīyaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ  idaṃ manasā kammaṃ kattukāmau idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya, na parabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattaiyya, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattaiyya – kusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ  sukhudrayaṃ  sukhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ tai, rāhula, manasā kammaṃ karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘karauntainapi tai, rāhula, manasā kammaṃ tadaiva tai manaukammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, paṭisaṃharaiyyāsi tvaṃ, rāhula, aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ karaumi idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – kusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, anupadaj‍jaiyyāsi tvaṃ, rāhula, aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ|

‘‘katvāpi tai, rāhula, manasā kammaṃ tadaiva tai manaukammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ nu khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati saṃvatti (sī॰ pī॰), parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti? sacai khau tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ  khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ akāsiṃ idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – akusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipāka’nti, aivarūpaṃ pana aivarūpai (sī॰ pī॰), aivarūpai pana (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tai, rāhula, manaukammaṃ manaukammai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭīyitabbaṃ harāyitabbaṃ jigucchitabbaṃ; aṭṭīyitvā harāyitvā jigucchitvā āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai pana tvaṃ, rāhula, pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ idaṃ manasā kammaṃ akāsiṃ  idaṃ mai manaukammaṃ naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati – kusalaṃ idaṃ manaukammaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipāka’nti, tainaiva tvaṃ, rāhula, pītipāmauj‍jaina viharaiyyāsi ahaurattānusikkhī kusalaisu dhammaisu|

112. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, rāhula, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, vacīkammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, manaukammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, sabbai tai aivamaivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā vacīkammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā manaukammaṃ parisaudhaisuṃ| yaipi hi kaici, rāhula, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhaissanti, vacīkammaṃ parisaudhaissanti, manaukammaṃ parisaudhaissanti, sabbai tai aivamaivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhaissanti, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā vacīkammaṃ parisaudhaissanti, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā manaukammaṃ parisaudhaissanti| yaipi hi kaici, rāhula, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhainti, vacīkammaṃ parisaudhainti, manaukammaṃ parisaudhainti, sabbai tai aivamaivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhainti, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā vacīkammaṃ parisaudhainti, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā manaukammaṃ parisaudhainti| tasmātiha, rāhula, ‘pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāyakammaṃ parisaudhaissāmi, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā vacīkammaṃ parisaudhaissāmi, pac‍cavaikkhitvā pac‍cavaikkhitvā manaukammaṃ parisaudhaissāmī’ti – aivañhi tai, rāhula, sikkhitabba’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

ambalaṭṭhikarāhulauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. mahārāhulauvādasuttaṃ

113. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| āyasmāpi khau  rāhulau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| atha khau bhagavā apalaukaitvā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpaṃ – atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā – sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabba’’nti| ‘‘rūpamaiva nu khau, bhagavā, rūpamaiva nu khau, sugatā’’ti? ‘‘rūpampi, rāhula, vaidanāpi, rāhula, sañ‍ñāpi, rāhula, saṅkhārāpi, rāhula, viñ‍ñāṇampi, rāhulā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā rāhulau ‘‘kau naj‍ja kau nuj‍ja (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhagavatā sammukhā auvādaina auvaditau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissatī’’ti tatau paṭinivattitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| addasā khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvāna āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ānāpānassatiṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| ānāpānassati, rāhula, bhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti|

114. atha khau āyasmā rāhulau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā nu khau, bhantai, ānāpānassati, kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ, yaṃ  vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu paṭhavīdhātu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, pathavīdhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindati, pathavīdhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti’’|

115. ‘‘katamā  ca, rāhula, āpaudhātu? āpaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā āpaudhātu yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu āpaudhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āpaudhātuyā nibbindati, āpaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

116. ‘‘katamā ca, rāhula, taijaudhātu? taijaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – yaina ca santappati yaina ca jīrīyati yaina ca pariḍayhati yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā taijaudhātu yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu taijaudhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā taijaudhātuyā nibbindati, taijaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

117. ‘‘katamā  ca, rāhula, vāyaudhātu? vāyaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – uddhaṅgamā vātā, adhaugamā vātā, kucchisayā vātā, kauṭṭhāsayā kauṭṭhasayā (sī॰ pī॰) vātā, aṅgamaṅgānusārinau vātā, assāsau passāsau, iti yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu vāyaudhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindati, vāyaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

118. ‘‘katamā ca, rāhula, ākāsadhātu? ākāsadhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsaṃ ākāsagataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaṇṇacchiddaṃ nāsacchiddaṃ mukhadvāraṃ, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ ajjhauharati, yattha ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ santiṭṭhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ adhaubhāgaṃ adhaubhāgā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nikkhamati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsaṃ ākāsagataṃ, aghaṃ aghagataṃ, vivaraṃ vivaragataṃ, asamphuṭṭhaṃ, maṃsalauhitaihi upādin‍naṃ  ākāsagataṃ upādin‍naṃ (sī॰ pī॰) – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu yā ca bāhirā ākāsadhātu ākāsadhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā ākāsadhātuyā cittaṃ nibbindati, ākāsadhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

119. ‘‘pathavīsamaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| pathavīsamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, pathaviyā sucimpi nikkhipanti, asucimpi nikkhipanti, gūthagatampi nikkhipanti, muttagatampi nikkhipanti, khaiḷagatampi nikkhipanti, pubbagatampi nikkhipanti, lauhitagatampi nikkhipanti, na ca taina pathavī aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, rāhula, pathavīsamaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| pathavīsamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti|

‘‘āpausamaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| āpausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, āpasmiṃ sucimpi  dhauvanti, asucimpi dhauvanti, gūthagatampi dhauvanti, muttagatampi dhauvanti, khaiḷagatampi dhauvanti, pubbagatampi dhauvanti, lauhitagatampi dhauvanti, na ca  taina āpau aṭṭīyati  vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaiva khau  tvaṃ, rāhula, āpausamaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| āpausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti|

‘‘taijausamaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| taijausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, taijau sucimpi dahati, asucimpi dahati, gūthagatampi dahati, muttagatampi dahati, khaiḷagatampi dahati, pubbagatampi dahati, lauhitagatampi dahati, na ca taina taijau aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, rāhula, taijausamaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| taijausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti|

‘‘vāyausamaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| vāyausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, vāyau sucimpi upavāyati, asucimpi upavāyati, gūthagatampi upavāyati, muttagatampi upavāyati, khaiḷagatampi upavāyati, pubbagatampi upavāyati, lauhitagatampi upavāyati, na ca taina vāyau aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, rāhula, vāyausamaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| vāyausamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya  ṭhassanti|

‘‘ākāsasamaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| ākāsasamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti| saiyyathāpi, rāhula, ākāsau na katthaci patiṭṭhitau; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, rāhula, ākāsasamaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| ākāsasamañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau uppan‍nā manāpāmanāpā phassā cittaṃ na pariyādāya ṭhassanti|

120. ‘‘maittaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| maittañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yau byāpādau  sau pahīyissati| karuṇaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| karuṇañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yā vihaisā sā pahīyissati| muditaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| muditañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yā arati sā pahīyissati| upaikkhaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| upaikkhañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yau paṭighau sau pahīyissati| asubhaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| asubhañhi tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yau rāgau sau pahīyissati| anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| anic‍casañ‍ñañhi  tai, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvayatau yau asmimānau sau pahīyissati|

121. ‘‘ānāpānassatiṃ, rāhula, bhāvanaṃ bhāvaihi| ānāpānassati hi tai, rāhula, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, rāhula, ānāpānassati, kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, rāhula, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati satauva satau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) passasati|

‘‘dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘‘anic‍cānupassī  assasissāmī’ti  sikkhati; ‘anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘virāgānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘virāgānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, rāhula, ānāpānassati, aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| aivaṃ bhāvitāya, rāhula, ānāpānassatiyā, aivaṃ  bahulīkatāya yaipi tai carimakā assāsā taipi viditāva nirujjhanti nau aviditā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahārāhulauvādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. cūḷamālukyasuttaṃ

122. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmatau mālukyaputtassa māluṅkyaputtassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yānimāni diṭṭhigatāni bhagavatā abyākatāni ṭhapitāni paṭikkhittāni – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi, ‘asassatau laukau’tipi, ‘antavā laukau’tipi, ‘anantavā laukau’tipi, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi – tāni mai bhagavā na byākarauti| yāni mai bhagavā na byākarauti taṃ mai na ruc‍cati, taṃ mai nakkhamati| sauhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchissāmi| sacai mai bhagavā byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā – aivāhaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ  carissāmi; nau cai mai bhagavā byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā – aivāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’ti|

123. atha  khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

124. ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – yānimāni diṭṭhigatāni bhagavatā abyākatāni ṭhapitāni paṭikkhittāni – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi, ‘asassatau laukau’tipi…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi – tāni mai bhagavā na byākarauti| yāni mai bhagavā na byākarauti taṃ mai  na ruc‍cati, taṃ mai nakkhamati| sauhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchissāmi| sacai mai bhagavā byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰…  ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā – aivāhaṃ bhagavati, brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi| nau cai mai bhagavā byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā – aivāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmīti| sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti, ‘sassatau laukau’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu; sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘asassatau laukau’ti, ‘asassatau laukau’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu| nau cai bhagavā jānāti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ajānatau khau pana apassatau aitadaiva ujukaṃ hauti yadidaṃ – ‘na jānāmi, na passāmī’ti| sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘antavā laukau’ti, ‘anantavā laukau’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu; sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘anantavā laukau’ti, ‘anantavā laukau’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu| nau cai bhagavā jānāti – ‘antavā laukau’ti vā, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ajānatau khau pana apassatau aitadaiva  ujukaṃ hauti yadidaṃ – ‘na jānāmi, na passāmī’ti| sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti mai bhagavā byākarautu; sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti mai bhagavā byākarautu| nau cai bhagavā jānāti – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ajānatau khau pana apassatau aitadaiva ujukaṃ hauti yadidaṃ – ‘na  jānāmi, na passāmī’ti| sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti mai bhagavā  byākarautu; sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu| nau cai bhagavā jānāti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ajānatau khau pana apassatau aitadaiva ujukaṃ hauti yadidaṃ – ‘na jānāmi na passāmī’ti| sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu; sacai bhagavā jānāti – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti mai bhagavā byākarautu| nau cai bhagavā jānāti – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na  na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ajānatau khau pana apassatau aitadaiva ujukaṃ hauti yadidaṃ – ‘na jānāmi, na passāmī’’’ti|

125. ‘‘kiṃ nu kiṃ nu khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tāhaṃ, mālukyaputta, aivaṃ avacaṃ – ‘aihi tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, mayi brahmacariyaṃ cara, ahaṃ tai byākarissāmi – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tvaṃ vā pana maṃ aivaṃ avaca – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi, bhagavā mai byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti kira, mālukyaputta, naivāhaṃ taṃ vadāmi – aihi tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, mayi brahmacariyaṃ cara, ahaṃ tai byākarissāmi – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’ti; napi kira maṃ tvaṃ vadaisi – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi, bhagavā mai byākarissati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti| aivaṃ santai, maughapurisa, kau santau kaṃ pac‍cācikkhasi?

126. ‘‘yau khau, mālukyaputta, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi yāva mai bhagavā na byākarissati – ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti vā…pai॰…  ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vāti, abyākatamaiva  taṃ, mālukyaputta, tathāgataina assa, atha sau puggalau kālaṃ karaiyya| saiyyathāpi, mālukyaputta, purisau sal‍laina viddhau assa savisaina gāḷhapalaipanaina| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhapaiyyuṃ| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ  sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ purisaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, khattiyau vā brāhmaṇau vā vaissau vā suddau vā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ purisaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau iti vā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ purisaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, dīghau vā rassau vā majjhimau vā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ purisaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, kāḷau vā sāmau vā maṅguracchavī vā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ purisaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, amukasmiṃ gāmai  vā nigamai vā nagarai vā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ dhanuṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, yadi vā cāpau yadi vā kaudaṇḍau’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ jiyaṃ jānāmi yāyamhi viddhau, yadi vā ak‍kassa yadi vā saṇhassa saṇṭhassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yadi vā nhārussa yadi vā maruvāya yadi vā khīrapaṇṇinau’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ kaṇḍaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, yadi vā gacchaṃ yadi vā raupima’nti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ kaṇḍaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, yassa pattaihi vājitaṃ vākhittaṃ (ka॰) yadi vā gijjhassa yadi vā kaṅkassa yadi vā kulalassa yadi vā maurassa yadi vā sithilahanunau’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ kaṇḍaṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, yassa nhārunā parikkhittaṃ yadi vā gavassa yadi vā mahiṃsassa yadi vā bhairavassa rauruvassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yadi vā saimhārassā’ti; sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ sal‍laṃ āharissāmi yāva na taṃ sal‍laṃ jānāmi yainamhi viddhau, yadi vā sal‍laṃ yadi vā khurappaṃ yadi vā vaikaṇḍaṃ yadi vā nārācaṃ yadi vā vacchadantaṃ yadi vā karavīrapatta’nti  – añ‍ñātamaiva taṃ, mālukyaputta, taina purisaina assa, atha sau purisau kālaṃ karaiyya| aivamaiva khau, mālukyaputta, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na tāvāhaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi yāva mai bhagavā na byākarissati – ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti  vā…pai॰… ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vāti – abyākatamaiva taṃ, mālukyaputta, tathāgataina assa, atha sau puggalau kālaṅkaraiyya|

127. ‘‘‘sassatau laukau’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivaṃ ‘nau asassatau laukau’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivampi ‘nau sassatau laukau’ti vā, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā diṭṭhiyā sati atthaiva jāti, atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ, santi saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā; yaisāhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai nighātaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| ‘antavā laukau’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivaṃ ‘nau anantavā laukau’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivampi ‘nau antavā laukau’ti vā, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā diṭṭhiyā sati atthaiva jāti, atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ, santi saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā; yaisāhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai nighātaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau  abhavissāti, aivaṃ ‘nau añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivampi ‘nau taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā diṭṭhiyā sati atthaiva jāti…pai॰… nighātaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivaṃ ‘nau na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivampi ‘nau hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā diṭṭhiyā sati atthaiva jāti…pai॰…  yaisāhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai nighātaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivaṃ ‘nau naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau abhavissāti, aivampi ‘nau hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhiyā sati, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā diṭṭhiyā sati atthaiva jāti…pai॰… yaisāhaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai nighātaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi|

128. ‘‘tasmātiha, mālukyaputta, abyākatañ‍ca mai abyākatatau  dhāraitha; byākatañ‍ca mai byākatatau dhāraitha| kiñ‍ca, mālukyaputta, mayā abyākataṃ? ‘sassatau laukau’ti mālukyaputta, mayā abyākataṃ; ‘asassatau laukau’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘antavā laukau’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘anantavā laukau’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti  – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – mayā abyākataṃ; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti – mayā abyākataṃ| kasmā caitaṃ, mālukyaputta, mayā abyākataṃ? na haitaṃ, mālukyaputta, atthasaṃhitaṃ na ādibrahmacariyakaṃ na naitaṃ (sī॰) nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| tasmā taṃ mayā abyākataṃ| kiñ‍ca, mālukyaputta, mayā byākataṃ? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, mālukyaputta, mayā byākataṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti – mayā byākataṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti – mayā byākataṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti – mayā byākataṃ| kasmā caitaṃ, mālukyaputta, mayā byākataṃ? aitañhi, mālukyaputta, atthasaṃhitaṃ aitaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| tasmā taṃ mayā byākataṃ| tasmātiha, mālukyaputta, abyākatañ‍ca mai  abyākatatau dhāraitha; byākatañ‍ca mai byākatatau dhāraithā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷamālukyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. mahāmālukyasuttaṃ

129. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘dhāraitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, mayā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, dhāraisi mayā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aurambhāgiyaṃ  saṃyaujanaṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; vicikicchaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; kāmacchandaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; byāpādaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti|

‘‘kassa khau nāma tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, imāni aivaṃ pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni daisitāni dhāraisi? nanu, mālukyaputta, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā iminā taruṇūpamaina upārambhaina upārambhissanti? daharassa hi, mālukyaputta, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa sak‍kāyautipi na hauti, kutau  panassa uppaj‍jissati sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi? anusaitvaivassa anusaiti tvaivassa (sī॰ pī॰) sak‍kāyadiṭṭhānusayau| daharassa hi, mālukyaputta, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa dhammātipi na hauti, kutau panassa uppaj‍jissati dhammaisu vicikicchā? anusaitvaivassa vicikicchānusayau| daharassa hi, mālukyaputta, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa sīlātipi na hauti, kutau panassa uppaj‍jissati sīlaisu sīlabbataparāmāsau? anusaitvaivassa sīlabbataparāmāsānusayau| daharassa hi, mālukyaputta, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa kāmātipi na hauti, kutau panassa uppaj‍jissati kāmaisu kāmacchandau? anusaitvaivassa kāmarāgānusayau| daharassa hi, mālukyaputta, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa sattātipi na hauti, kutau panassa uppaj‍jissati sattaisu byāpādau? anusaitvaivassa byāpādānusayau| nanu, mālukyaputta, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā iminā taruṇūpamaina upārambhaina upārambhissantī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau, aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni daisaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hānanda, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā  ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

130. ‘‘idhānanda, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhipariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiparaitaina; uppan‍nāya ca sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi thāmagatā appaṭivinītā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ| vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina; uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sā vicikicchā thāmagatā appaṭivinītā aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ| sīlabbataparāmāsapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati sīlabbataparāmāsaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca sīlabbataparāmāsassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sau sīlabbataparāmāsau thāmagatau appaṭivinītau aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ| kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sau kāmarāgau thāmagatau appaṭivinītau aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ| byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sau byāpādau thāmagatau appaṭivinītau aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ|

131. ‘‘sutavā ca khau, ānanda, ariyasāvakau  ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau  ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na sak‍kāyadiṭṭhipariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiparaitaina; uppan‍nāya ca sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa sā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi sānusayā pahīyati| na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na vicikicchāparaitaina; uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa sā vicikicchā sānusayā pahīyati| na sīlabbataparāmāsapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na sīlabbataparāmāsaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca sīlabbataparāmāsassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa sau sīlabbataparāmāsau sānusayau pahīyati| na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na kāmarāgaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa sau kāmarāgau sānusayau pahīyati| na byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na byāpādaparaitaina; uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa sau byāpādau sānusayau pahīyati|

132. ‘‘yau, ānanda, maggau yā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya taṃ maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ anāgamma pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni ñassati vā dakkhati vā pajahissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau  sāravatau tacaṃ acchaitvā phaigguṃ acchaitvā sāracchaidau bhavissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yau maggau yā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya taṃ maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ anāgamma pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni ñassati vā dakkhati vā pajahissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau ca khau, ānanda, maggau yā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya taṃ  maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ āgamma pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni ñassati vā dakkhati vā pajahissati vāti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau tacaṃ chaitvā phaigguṃ chaitvā sāracchaidau bhavissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yau maggau yā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya taṃ maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ āgamma pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni ñassati vā dakkhati vā pajahissati vāti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, gaṅgā nadī pūrā udakassa samatittikā  kākapaiyyā| atha dubbalakau purisau āgacchaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imissā gaṅgāya nadiyā tiriyaṃ bāhāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gacchissāmī’ti gacchāmīti (sī॰ pī॰); sau na sak‍kuṇaiyya gaṅgāya nadiyā tiriyaṃ bāhāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gantuṃ| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci yassa kassaci (sabbattha) sak‍kāyaniraudhāya dhammai daisiyamānai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati; saiyyathāpi  sau dubbalakau purisau aivamaitai daṭṭhabbā| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, gaṅgā nadī pūrā udakassa samatittikā kākapaiyyā| atha balavā purisau āgacchaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imissā gaṅgāya nadiyā tiriyaṃ bāhāya  sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gacchissāmī’ti; sau sak‍kuṇaiyya gaṅgāya nadiyā tiriyaṃ bāhāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gantuṃ| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci sak‍kāyaniraudhāya dhammai daisiyamānai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati; saiyyathāpi sau balavā purisau aivamaitai daṭṭhabbā|

133. ‘‘katamau cānanda, maggau, katamā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya? idhānanda, bhikkhu upadhivivaikā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānā sabbasau kāyaduṭṭhul‍lānaṃ paṭippassaddhiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti paṭipāpaiti (syā॰), patiṭṭhāpaiti (ka॰)| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā  cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau  taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti; nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī, anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, ānanda, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰…  dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ… anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, ānanda, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ  sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ…pai॰…  anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, ānanda, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ…pai॰…  anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, ānanda, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ…pai॰…  anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, ānanda, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti|

‘‘aisau cai, bhantai, maggau aisā paṭipadā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya, atha kiñ‍carahi idhaikac‍cai bhikkhū caitauvimuttinau aikac‍cai bhikkhū pañ‍ñāvimuttinau’’ti? ‘‘aittha khau panaisāhaṃ aittha khau taisāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), ānanda, indriyavaimattataṃ vadāmī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahāmālukyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. bhaddālisuttaṃ

134. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aikāsanabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi; aikāsanabhaujanaṃ khau, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhuñ‍jamānau appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāmi  appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha, tumhaipi, bhikkhavai, aikāsanabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jatha; aikāsanabhaujanaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, na ussahāmi aikāsanabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jituṃ; aikāsanabhaujanañhi mai, bhantai, bhuñ‍jatau siyā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, siyā vippaṭisārau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, bhaddāli, yattha nimantitau assasi tattha aikadaisaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā aikadaisaṃ nīharitvāpi bhuñ‍jaiyyāsi| aivampi khau  tvaṃ, bhaddāli, bhuñ‍jamānau aikāsanau yāpaissasī’’ti bhuñ‍jamānau yāpaissasīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| ‘‘aivampi khau ahaṃ, bhantai, na ussahāmi bhuñ‍jituṃ; aivampi hi mai, bhantai, bhuñ‍jatau siyā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, siyā vippaṭisārau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavatā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisi| atha  khau āyasmā bhaddāli sabbaṃ taṃ taimāsaṃ na bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ adāsi, yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī|

135. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatīti| atha khau āyasmā bhaddāli yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ bhaddāliṃ tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idaṃ khau, āvusau bhaddāli, bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karīyati karaṇīyaṃ (ka॰)| niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissati| iṅghāvusau bhaddāli, aitaṃ dausakaṃ sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, mā tai pacchā duk‍karataraṃ ahausī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā  bhaddāli taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ bhagavatā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha  tvaṃ, bhaddāli, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ  yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ mayā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘bhagavā khau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati, bhagavāpi maṃ jānissati – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘sambahulā khau  bhikkhu sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃ upagatā, taipi maṃ jānissanti – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘sambahulā khau bhikkhuniyau sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃ upagatā, tāpi maṃ jānissanti – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘sambahulā khau upāsakā sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti, taipi maṃ jānissanti – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘sambahulā khau upāsikā sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti, tāpi maṃ jānissanti – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausi| samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi – ‘sambahulā khau nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃ upagatā, taipi maṃ jānissanti – bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau thairañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau tai, bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausī’’ti|

‘‘ac‍cayau  maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ bhagavatā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, bhaddāli, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ mayā sikkhāpadai  pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisi’’|

136. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhaddāli, idhassa bhikkhu ubhataubhāgavimuttau, tamahaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aihi mai tvaṃ, bhikkhu, paṅkai saṅkamau hauhī’ti, api nu khau sau saṅkamaiyya vā añ‍ñaina vā kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiyya, ‘nau’ti vā vadaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhaddāli, idhassa bhikkhu pañ‍ñāvimuttau… kāyasakkhi… diṭṭhippattau… saddhāvimuttau… dhammānusārī… saddhānusārī, tamahaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aihi mai tvaṃ, bhikkhu, paṅkai saṅkamau hauhī’ti, api nu khau sau saṅkamaiyya vā añ‍ñaina vā kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiyya, ‘nau’ti vā vadaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ  mañ‍ñasi, bhaddāli, api nu tvaṃ, bhaddāli, tasmiṃ samayai ubhataubhāgavimuttau vā hausi pañ‍ñāvimuttau  vā kāyasakkhi vā diṭṭhippattau vā saddhāvimuttau vā dhammānusārī vā saddhānusārī vā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘nanu tvaṃ, bhaddāli, tasmiṃ samayai rittau tucchau aparaddhau’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai| ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ bhagavatā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, bhaddāli, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ mayā sikkhāpadai pañ‍ñāpiyamānai bhikkhusaṅghai sikkhaṃ samādiyamānai anussāhaṃ pavaidaisi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, bhaddāli, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhihaisā, bhaddāli, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati’’|

137. ‘‘idha, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajaiyyaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ  palālapuñ‍jaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikaraiyya’nti| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ  kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau satthāpi upavadati, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī upavadanti, daivatāpi upavadanti, attāpi attānaṃ upavadati| sau satthārāpi upavaditau, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñūhi sabrahmacārīhi upavaditau, daivatāhipi upavaditau, attanāpi attānaṃ upavaditau na uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārissa|

138. ‘‘idha pana, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārī hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajaiyyaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ  palālapuñ‍jaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikaraiyya’nti| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| tassa tathāvūpakaṭṭhassa viharatau satthāpi na upavadati, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī na upavadanti, daivatāpi na upavadanti, attāpi attānaṃ na upavadati| sau satthārāpi anupavaditau, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñūhi sabrahmacārīhi anupavaditau, daivatāhipi anupavaditau, attanāpi attānaṃ anupavaditau uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarauti| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca  akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

139. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati, satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi  taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai  vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissa|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ  āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi taṃ, bhaddāli, hauti yathā taṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya paripūrakārissā’’ti|

140. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍caṃ bhikkhuṃ pasayha pasayha pavayha pavayha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kāraṇaṃ karaunti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍caṃ bhikkhuṃ nau tathā pasayha pasayha kāraṇaṃ karauntī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu abhiṇhāpattikau hauti āpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, na sammā vattati, na laumaṃ pātaiti, na naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti nāha| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu  abhiṇhāpattikau āpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, na sammā vattati, na laumaṃ pātaiti, na naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti  taṃ karaumī’ti nāha| sādhu vatāyasmantau imassa bhikkhunau tathā tathā upaparikkhatha yathāssidaṃ yathayidaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) adhikaraṇaṃ na khippamaiva vūpasamaiyyāti| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhunau bhikkhū  tathā tathā upaparikkhanti yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ na khippamaiva vūpasammati|

141. ‘‘idha pana, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu abhiṇhāpattikau hauti āpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau nāñ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ na apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau  hauti taṃ karaumī’ti āha| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiṇhāpattikau āpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau nāñ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ na apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti āha| sādhu vatāyasmantau, imassa bhikkhunau tathā tathā upaparikkhatha yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ khippamaiva vūpasamaiyyāti| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhunau bhikkhū tathā tathā upaparikkhanti yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ khippamaiva vūpasammati|

142. ‘‘idha, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu adhic‍cāpattikau hauti anāpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, na sammā vattati, na laumaṃ pātaiti, na naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti nāha| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu adhic‍cāpattikau anāpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, na sammā vattati, na laumaṃ pātaiti, na naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti nāha| sādhu vatāyasmantau, imassa bhikkhunau tathā tathā upaparikkhatha yathāssidaṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ na khippamaiva vūpasamaiyyāti| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhunau bhikkhū tathā tathā upaparikkhanti yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ  na khippamaiva vūpasammati|

143. ‘‘idha pana, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu adhic‍cāpattikau hauti anāpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau nāñ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti āha| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu adhic‍cāpattikau anāpattibahulau| sau bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau nāñ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, ‘yaina saṅghau  attamanau hauti taṃ karaumī’ti āha| sādhu vatāyasmantau, imassa bhikkhunau tathā tathā upaparikkhatha yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ khippamaiva vūpasamaiyyāti| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhaddāli, bhikkhunau bhikkhū tathā tathā upaparikkhanti yathāssidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ khippamaiva vūpasammati|

144. ‘‘idha, bhaddāli, aikac‍cau bhikkhu saddhāmattakaina vahati paimamattakaina| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saddhāmattakaina vahati paimamattakaina| sacai mayaṃ imaṃ bhikkhuṃ pasayha pasayha kāraṇaṃ karissāma – mā yampissa taṃ saddhāmattakaṃ paimamattakaṃ tamhāpi parihāyī’ti| saiyyathāpi, bhaddāli, purisassa aikaṃ cakkhuṃ, tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā taṃ aikaṃ cakkhuṃ rakkhaiyyuṃ – ‘mā yampissa taṃ aikaṃ cakkhuṃ tamhāpi parihāyī’ti; aivamaiva khau, bhaddāli, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu saddhāmattakaina vahati paimamattakaina| tatra, bhaddāli, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saddhāmattakaina vahati paimamattakaina| sacai mayaṃ imaṃ bhikkhuṃ pasayha pasayha kāraṇaṃ karissāma – mā yampissa taṃ saddhāmattakaṃ paimamattakaṃ tamhāpi parihāyī’ti| ayaṃ khau, bhaddāli, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍caṃ bhikkhuṃ pasayha pasayha kāraṇaṃ karaunti| ayaṃ pana, bhaddāli, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍caṃ bhikkhuṃ nau tathā pasayha pasayha kāraṇaṃ karauntī’’ti|

145. ‘‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina pubbai appatarāni  caiva sikkhāpadāni ahaisuṃ bahutarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahiṃsu? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina aitarahi bahutarāni caiva sikkhāpadāni haunti appatarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahantī’ti? ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhaddāli, hauti sattaisu hāyamānaisu, saddhammai  antaradhāyamānai, bahutarāni caiva sikkhāpadāni haunti appatarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahantīti| na tāva, bhaddāli, satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti yāva na idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti| yatau ca khau, bhaddāli, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, bhaddāli, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti yāva na saṅghau mahattaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhaddāli, saṅghau mahattaṃ pattau hauti, atha idhaikac‍cai  āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti| atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, bhaddāli, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti yāva na saṅghau lābhaggaṃ pattau hauti, yasaggaṃ pattau hauti, bāhusac‍caṃ pattau hauti, rattañ‍ñutaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhaddāli, saṅghau rattañ‍ñutaṃ pattau hauti, atha idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya|

146. ‘‘appakā khau tumhai, bhaddāli, taina samayaina ahuvattha yadā vau ahaṃ ājānīyasusūpamaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaisiṃ| taṃ sarasi sarasi tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), sarasi taṃ (?) bhaddālī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tatra, bhaddāli, kaṃ haituṃ pac‍caisī’’ti?

‘‘sau hi nūnāhaṃ, bhantai, dīgharattaṃ satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī ahausi’’nti|

‘‘na khau, bhaddāli, aisaiva haitu, aisa pac‍cayau| api ca mai tvaṃ, bhaddāli, dīgharattaṃ caitasā  caitauparic‍ca viditau – ‘na cāyaṃ maughapurisau mayā dhammai daisiyamānai aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasau sabbaṃ caitasau (ka॰) saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇātī’ti| api ca tai ahaṃ, bhaddāli, ājānīyasusūpamaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

147. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhaddāli, dakkhau assadamakau bhadraṃ assājānīyaṃ labhitvā paṭhamainaiva mukhādhānai kāraṇaṃ kāraiti| tassa mukhādhānai kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa hauntiyaiva visūkāyitāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici, yathā taṃ akāritapubbaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa| sau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ ṭhānai parinibbāyati| yatau khau, bhaddāli, bhadrau assājānīyau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ ṭhānai parinibbutau hauti, tamainaṃ assadamakau uttari kāraṇaṃ kāraiti yugādhānai| tassa yugādhānai kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa hauntiyaiva visūkāyitāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici, yathā taṃ akāritapubbaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa| sau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ  ṭhānai parinibbāyati| yatau khau, bhaddāli, bhadrau assājānīyau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ ṭhānai parinibbutau hauti, tamainaṃ assadamakau uttari kāraṇaṃ kāraiti anuk‍kamai maṇḍalai khurakāsai khurakāyai (sī॰ pī॰) dhāvai davattai ravatthai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) rājaguṇai rājavaṃsai uttamai javai uttamai hayai uttamai sākhalyai| tassa uttamai javai uttamai hayai uttamai sākhalyai kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa hauntiyaiva visūkāyitāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici, yathā taṃ akāritapubbaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāriyamānassa| sau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ ṭhānai parinibbāyati| yatau khau, bhaddāli, bhadrau assājānīyau abhiṇhakāraṇā anupubbakāraṇā tasmiṃ ṭhānai parinibbutau hauti, tamainaṃ assadamakau uttari vaṇṇiyañ‍ca pāṇiyañ‍ca valiyañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰), baliyañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰) anuppavaicchati| imaihi khau, bhaddāli, dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhaddāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi dasahi? idha, bhaddāli, bhikkhu asaikhāya sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsaṅkappaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya  sammāvācāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammākammantaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāvāyāmaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāsatiyā  saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsamādhinā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāvimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhaddāli, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā bhaddāli bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

bhaddālisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. laṭukikaupamasuttaṃ

148. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅguttarāpaisu viharati āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅguttarāpānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āpaṇaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| āpaṇai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yainañ‍ñatarau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau udāyī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āpaṇaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| āpaṇai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina sau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau udāyissa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivamīdisai aviñ‍ñāṇakappakaraṇai vata nau bhagavā dukkhadhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā sukhadhammānaṃ upahattā; bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upahattā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā udāyī sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

149. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā dukkhadhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā sukhadhammānaṃ upahattā; bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upahattā’ti| mayañhi, bhantai, pubbai sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai| ahu khau sau, bhantai, samayau yaṃ bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘iṅgha tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ divāvikālabhaujanaṃ pajahathā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, ahudaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ, ahudaiva ahu (sī॰ pī॰) daumanassaṃ – ‘yampi nau saddhā gahapatikā divā vikālai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ dainti tassapi nau bhagavā pahānamāha, tassapi nau sugatau paṭinissaggamāhā’ti| tai  mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati paimañ‍ca gāravañ‍ca hiriñ‍ca auttappañ‍ca sampassamānā  aivaṃ taṃ divāvikālabhaujanaṃ pajahimhā| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jāma pātau ca| ahu khau sau, bhantai, samayau yaṃ bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘iṅgha tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ rattiṃvikālabhaujanaṃ pajahathā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, ahudaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ ahudaiva daumanassaṃ – ‘yampi nau imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ bhattānaṃ paṇītasaṅkhātataraṃ tassapi nau bhagavā pahānamāha, tassapi nau sugatau paṭinissaggamāhā’ti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau purisau divā sūpaiyyaṃ labhitvā aivamāha – ‘handa ca imaṃ nikkhipatha, sāyaṃ sabbaiva samaggā  bhuñ‍jissāmā’ti| yā kāci, bhantai, saṅkhatiyau sabbā tā rattiṃ, appā divā| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati paimañ‍ca gāravañ‍ca hiriñ‍ca auttappañ‍ca sampassamānā aivaṃ taṃ rattiṃvikālabhaujanaṃ pajahimhā| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ piṇḍāya carantā candanikampi pavisanti, auligal‍laipi papatanti, kaṇṭakāvāṭampi kaṇṭakavattampi (sī॰ pī॰), kaṇṭakarājimpi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ārauhanti, suttampi gāviṃ ārauhanti, māṇavaihipi samāgacchanti katakammaihipi akatakammaihipi, mātugāmaupi tai taina (ka॰) asaddhammaina nimantaiti| bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ piṇḍāya carāmi| addasā khau maṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarā itthī vij‍jantarikāya bhājanaṃ dhauvantī| disvā maṃ bhītā vissaramakāsi – ‘abhummai abbhummai (sī॰ pī॰) pisācau vata ma’nti! aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ itthiṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘nāhaṃ, bhagini, pisācau; bhikkhu piṇḍāya  ṭhitau’ti| ‘bhikkhussa ātumārī, bhikkhussa mātumārī ṭhitau’ti| bhikkhussa ātumātumārī (ka॰)! varaṃ tai, bhikkhu, tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina kucchi parikantau, na tvaiva varaṃ yaṃ na tvaiva yā (sī॰ pī॰) rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ kucchihaitu piṇḍāya carasī’ti carasāti (sī॰ pī॰)| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, tadanussaratau aivaṃ hauti – ‘bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā dukkhadhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā sukhadhammānaṃ upahattā; bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ apahattā, bahūnaṃ vata nau bhagavā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upahattā’’’ti|

150. ‘‘aivamaiva panudāyi, idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā  vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| tai tañ‍caiva nappajahanti, mayi  ca appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau – saiyyathāpi, udāyi, laṭukikā sakuṇikā pūtilatāya bandhanaina baddhā tatthaiva vadhaṃ vā bandhaṃ vā maraṇaṃ vā āgamaiti| yau nu khau, udāyi, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaina sā laṭukikā sakuṇikā pūtilatāya bandhanaina baddhā tatthaiva vadhaṃ vā bandhaṃ vā maraṇaṃ vā āgamaiti, tañhi tassā abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhana’nti; sammā nu khau sau, udāyi, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| yaina sā, bhantai, laṭukikā sakuṇikā pūtilatāya bandhanaina baddhā tatthaiva vadhaṃ vā bandhaṃ vā maraṇaṃ vā āgamaiti, tañhi tassā balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, udāyi, idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti? tai tañ‍caiva nappajahanti, mayi ca appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’’|

151. ‘‘idha  panudāyi, aikac‍cai kulaputtā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā  vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa pahātabbassa yassa nau bhagavā pahānamāha, yassa nau sugatau paṭinissaggamāhā’ti? tai tañ‍caiva pajahanti, mayi ca na appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā tai taṃ pahāya appaussuk‍kā pan‍nalaumā paradattavuttā paradavuttā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) migabhūtaina caitasā viharanti| taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhanaṃ – saiyyathāpi, udāyi, rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau daḷhaihi varattaihi bandhanaihi baddhau īsakaṃyaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaitvā tāni bandhanāni saṃchinditvā saṃpadālaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati| yau nu khau, udāyi, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaihi sau rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau daḷhaihi varattaihi bandhanaihi baddhau īsakaṃyaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaitvā tāni bandhanāni saṃchinditvā saṃpadālaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati, tañhi tassa balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ  bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’ti; sammā nu khau sau, udāyi, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| yaihi sau, bhantai, rañ‍ñau nāgau īsādantau urūḷhavā abhijātau saṅgāmāvacarau daḷhaihi varattaihi bandhanaihi baddhau īsakaṃyaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaitvā tāni bandhanāni saṃchinditvā  saṃpadālaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati, tañhi tassa abalaṃ bandhanaṃ…pai॰… asārakaṃ bandhana’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, udāyi, idhaikac‍cai kulaputtā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa pahātabbassa yassa nau bhagavā pahānamāha, yassa nau sugatau paṭinissaggamāhā’ti? tai tañ‍caiva pajahanti, mayi ca na appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā tai taṃ  pahāya appaussuk‍kā pan‍nalaumā paradattavuttā migabhūtaina caitasā viharanti| taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhanaṃ’’|

152. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau daliddau assakau anāḷhiyau; tassa’ssa aikaṃ agārakaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ kākātidāyiṃ kākātiḍāyiṃ (?) naparamarūpaṃ, aikā khaṭaupikā kaḷaupikā (ka॰) auluggaviluggā naparamarūpā, aikissā kumbhiyā  dhañ‍ñasamavāpakaṃ naparamarūpaṃ, aikā jāyikā naparamarūpā| sau ārāmagataṃ bhikkhuṃ passaiyya sudhautahatthapādaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuttāviṃ sītāya chāyāya nisin‍naṃ adhicittai yuttaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ‘sukhaṃ vata, bhau, sāmañ‍ñaṃ, āraugyaṃ vata, bhau, sāmañ‍ñaṃ! sau vatassaṃ sau vatassa (ka॰) yauhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau na sak‍kuṇaiyya aikaṃ agārakaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ kākātidāyiṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ khaṭaupikaṃ  auluggaviluggaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikissā kumbhiyā dhañ‍ñasamavāpakaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ jāyikaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| yau nu khau, udāyi, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaihi sau purisau bandhanaihi baddhau na sak‍kauti aikaṃ agārakaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ kākātidāyiṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ khaṭaupikaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikissā kumbhiyā dhañ‍ñasamavāpakaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ jāyikaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ  auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ; tañhi tassa abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhana’nti; sammā nu khau sau, udāyi, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| yaihi sau, bhantai, purisau bandhanaihi baddhau, na sak‍kauti aikaṃ agārakaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ kākātidāyiṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ khaṭaupikaṃ auluggaviluggaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikissā kumbhiyā dhañ‍ñasamavāpakaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya, aikaṃ jāyikaṃ naparamarūpaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ; tañhi tassa balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, udāyi, idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa  adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti? tai tañ‍caiva nappajahanti, mayi ca appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’’|

153. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, udāyi, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā aḍḍhau mahaddhanau  mahābhaugau, naikānaṃ nikkhagaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ dhañ‍ñagaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ khaittagaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ vatthugaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ bhariyagaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ dāsagaṇānaṃ cayau, naikānaṃ dāsigaṇānaṃ cayau; sau ārāmagataṃ bhikkhuṃ passaiyya sudhautahatthapādaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuttāviṃ sītāya chāyāya nisin‍naṃ adhicittai yuttaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ‘sukhaṃ vata, bhau, sāmañ‍ñaṃ, āraugyaṃ vata, bhau, sāmañ‍ñaṃ! sau vatassaṃ yauhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau sak‍kuṇaiyya naikāni nikkhagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dhañ‍ñagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni khaittagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni vatthugaṇāni pahāya, naikāni bhariyagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsigaṇāni pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| yau nu khau, udāyi, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaihi sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā bandhanaihi baddhau, sak‍kauti naikāni nikkhagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dhañ‍ñagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni  khaittagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni vatthugaṇāni pahāya, naikāni bhariyagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsigaṇāni pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ, tañhi tassa balavaṃ bandhanaṃ, daḷhaṃ bandhanaṃ, thiraṃ bandhanaṃ, apūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, thūlau, kaliṅgarau’ti; sammā nu khau sau, udāyi, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| yaihi sau, bhantai, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā bandhanaihi baddhau, sak‍kauti naikāni nikkhagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dhañ‍ñagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni  khaittagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni vatthugaṇāni pahāya, naikāni bhariyagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsagaṇāni pahāya, naikāni dāsigaṇāni pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ; tañhi tassa abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhana’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, udāyi, idhaikac‍cai kulaputtā ‘idaṃ pajahathā’ti mayā vuc‍camānā tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ panimassa appamattakassa auramattakassa pahātabbassa yassa nau bhagavā pahānamāha yassa, nau sugatau paṭinissaggamāhā’ti? tai tañ‍caiva pajahanti, mayi ca na appac‍cayaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| yai ca bhikkhū sikkhākāmā tai taṃ pahāya  appaussuk‍kā pan‍nalaumā paradattavuttā migabhūtaina caitasā viharanti| taisaṃ taṃ, udāyi, hauti abalaṃ bandhanaṃ, dubbalaṃ bandhanaṃ, pūtikaṃ bandhanaṃ, asārakaṃ bandhanaṃ’’|

154. ‘‘cattāraumai, udāyi, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai  cattārau? idhudāyi, aikac‍cau puggalau upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍nau hauti upadhipaṭinissaggāya| tamainaṃ upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍naṃ upadhipaṭinissaggāya upadhipaṭisaṃyuttā sarasaṅkappā samudācaranti| sau tai adhivāsaiti, nappajahati, na vinaudaiti, na byantīkarauti, na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| imaṃ khau ahaṃ, udāyi, puggalaṃ ‘saṃyuttau’ti vadāmi nau ‘visaṃyuttau’| taṃ kissa haitu? indriyavaimattatā hi mai, udāyi, imasmiṃ puggalai viditā|

‘‘idha panudāyi, aikac‍cau puggalau upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍nau hauti upadhipaṭinissaggāya| tamainaṃ upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍naṃ upadhipaṭinissaggāya upadhipaṭisaṃyuttā sarasaṅkappā samudācaranti| sau tai nādhivāsaiti, pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| imampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, puggalaṃ ‘saṃyuttau’ti vadāmi nau ‘visaṃyuttau’| taṃ kissa haitu? indriyavaimattatā hi mai, udāyi, imasmiṃ puggalai viditā|
