‘‘idha panudāyi, aikac‍cau puggalau upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍nau hauti upadhipaṭinissaggāya| tamainaṃ upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍naṃ upadhipaṭinissaggāya kadāci karahaci satisammausā upadhipaṭisaṃyuttā sarasaṅkappā samudācaranti; dandhau, udāyi, satuppādau| atha khau naṃ khippamaiva pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau divasaṃsantattai divasasantattai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ayaukaṭāhai dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni nipātaiyya; dandhau, udāyi, udakaphusitānaṃ nipātau| atha khau naṃ khippamaiva parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, idhaikac‍cau  puggalau upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍nau hauti upadhipaṭinissaggāya| tamainaṃ upadhipahānāya paṭipan‍naṃ upadhipaṭinissaggāya kadāci karahaci satisammausā upadhipaṭisaṃyuttā sarasaṅkappā samudācaranti; dandhau, udāyi, satuppādau| atha khau naṃ khippamaiva pajahati, vinaudaiti, byantīkarauti, anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| imampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, puggalaṃ ‘saṃyuttau’ti vadāmi nau ‘visaṃyuttau’| taṃ  kissa haitu? indriyavaimattatā hi mai, udāyi, imasmiṃ puggalai viditā|

‘‘idha panudāyi, aikac‍cau puggalau ‘upadhi dukkhassa mūla’nti – iti viditvā nirupadhi hauti, upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau| imaṃ khau ahaṃ, udāyi, puggalaṃ ‘visaṃyuttau’ti vadāmi nau ‘saṃyuttau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? indriyavaimattatā hi mai, udāyi, imasmiṃ puggalai viditā| imai khau, udāyi, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

155. ‘‘pañ‍ca khau imai, udāyi, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, udāyi, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, udāyi, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ miḷhasukhaṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) puthuj‍janasukhaṃ anariyasukhaṃ, na saivitabbaṃ, na bhāvaitabbaṃ, na bahulīkātabbaṃ; ‘bhāyitabbaṃ  aitassa sukhassā’ti vadāmi|

156. ‘‘idhudāyi, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pītiyā ca virāgā… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sukhassa ca pahānā… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhasukhaṃ, āsaivitabbaṃ, bhāvaitabbaṃ, bahulīkātabbaṃ; ‘na bhāyitabbaṃ aitassa sukhassā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘idhudāyi, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; idaṃ khau ahaṃ, udāyi, iñ‍jitasmiṃ vadāmi| kiñ‍ca tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ? yadaiva tattha vitak‍kavicārā aniruddhā haunti idaṃ tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ| idhudāyi, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, iñ‍jitasmiṃ vadāmi| kiñ‍ca tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ? yadaiva tattha pītisukhaṃ aniruddhaṃ hauti idaṃ tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ| idhudāyi, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, iñ‍jitasmiṃ vadāmi| kiñ‍ca tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ? yadaiva tattha upaikkhāsukhaṃ  aniruddhaṃ hauti idaṃ tattha iñ‍jitasmiṃ| idhudāyi, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; idaṃ khau ahaṃ, udāyi, aniñ‍jitasmiṃ vadāmi|

‘‘idhudāyi, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; idaṃ khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti  vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca  tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; idampi khau ahaṃ, udāyi, ‘anala’nti vadāmi, ‘pajahathā’ti vadāmi, ‘samatik‍kamathā’ti vadāmi| kau ca tassa samatik‍kamau? idhudāyi, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ tassa samatik‍kamau; iti khau ahaṃ, udāyi, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassapi pahānaṃ vadāmi| passasi nau tvaṃ, udāyi, taṃ saṃyaujanaṃ aṇuṃ vā thūlaṃ vā yassāhaṃ nau pahānaṃ vadāmī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā udāyī bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

laṭukikaupamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. cātumasuttaṃ

157. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā cātumāyaṃ viharati āmalakīvanai| taina khau pana samayaina sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhāni pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni cātumaṃ anuppattāni haunti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā ahaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kai panaitai, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā, kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘aitāni, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhāni pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni cātumaṃ  anuppattāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| tai āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, mama vacanaina tai bhikkhū āmantaihi – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū  āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai  bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā, kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘imāni, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhāni pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni cātumaṃ anuppattāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| taimai āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā’’ti| ‘‘gacchatha, bhikkhavai, paṇāmaimi vau, na vau mama santikai vatthabba’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya pak‍kamiṃsu|

158. taina khau pana samayaina cātumaiyyakā sakyā santhāgārai sandhāgārai (ka॰) san‍nipatitā haunti kainacidaiva  karaṇīyaina| addasaṃsu khau cātumaiyyakā sakyā tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai; disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘handa, kahaṃ pana tumhai āyasmantau gacchathā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhusaṅghau paṇāmitau’’ti| ‘‘tainahāyasmantau muhuttaṃ nisīdatha, appaiva nāma mayaṃ sak‍kuṇaiyyāma bhagavantaṃ pasādaitu’’nti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū cātumaiyyakānaṃ sakyānaṃ pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau cātumaiyyakā sakyā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau cātumaiyyakā sakyā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhinandatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ; abhivadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bhagavatā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau anuggahitau, aivamaiva bhagavā aitarahi anuggaṇhātu bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| santaittha, bhantai, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ| taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bījānaṃ taruṇānaṃ udakaṃ alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, santaittha bhikkhū  navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, vacchassa taruṇassa mātaraṃ apassantassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| abhinandatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ; abhivadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bhagavatā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau anuggahitau; aivamaiva bhagavā aitarahi anuggaṇhātu bhikkhusaṅgha’’nti|

159. atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – brahmalaukai  antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhinandatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ; abhivadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bhagavatā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau anuggahitau; aivamaiva bhagavā aitarahi anuggaṇhātu bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| santaittha, bhantai, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ  dassanāya alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bījānaṃ taruṇānaṃ udakaṃ alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi bhantai, vacchassa taruṇassa mātaraṃ apassantassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, santaittha bhikkhū navā  acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, siyā vipariṇāmau| abhinandatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ; abhivadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bhagavatā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau  anuggahitau; aivamaiva bhagavā aitarahi anuggaṇhātu bhikkhusaṅgha’’nti|

160. asakkhiṃsu khau cātumaiyyakā ca sakyā brahmā ca sahampati bhagavantaṃ pasādaituṃ bījūpamaina ca taruṇūpamaina ca| atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, gaṇhatha pattacīvaraṃ| pasāditau bhagavā cātumaiyyakaihi ca sakyaihi brahmunā ca sahampatinā bījūpamaina ca taruṇūpamaina cā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pattacīvaramādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kinti tai, sāriputta, ahausi mayā bhikkhusaṅghai paṇāmitai’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, ahausi – ‘bhagavatā bhikkhusaṅghau paṇāmitau| appaussuk‍kau dāni bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharissati, mayampi dāni appaussuk‍kā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāramanuyuttā viharissāmā’’’ti| ‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, sāriputta, āgamaihi tvaṃ, sāriputta, diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāra’’nti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kinti tai, mauggal‍lāna, ahausi mayā bhikkhusaṅghai paṇāmitai’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, ahausi – ‘bhagavatā bhikkhusaṅghau paṇāmitau| appaussuk‍kau dāni bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharissati, ahañ‍ca dāni āyasmā ca sāriputtau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mauggal‍lāna! ahaṃ vā hi, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharaiyyaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā vā’’ti|

161. atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, bhayāni udakaurauhantai pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| katamāni cattāri? ūmibhayaṃ ummībhayaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), kumbhīlabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ – imāni, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayāni udakaurauhantai pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cattārimāni bhayāni  idhaikac‍cai puggalai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitai pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| katamāni  cattāri? ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhīlabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ|

162. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ūmibhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| tamainaṃ tathā pabbajitaṃ samānaṃ sabrahmacārī auvadanti, anusāsanti – ‘aivaṃ tai abhik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai paṭik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai ālaukitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai vilaukitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai samiñ‍jitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai pasāritabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai saṅghāṭipattacīvaraṃ dhāraitabba’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā añ‍ñai auvadāma, anusāsāma auvadāmapi anusāsāmapi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| imai panamhākaṃ puttamattā mañ‍ñai, nattamattā mañ‍ñai, amhai aivaṃ (ka॰) auvaditabbaṃ  anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’ti| sau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ūmibhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| ‘ūmibhaya’nti khau, bhikkhavai, kaudhupāyāsassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

163. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kumbhīlabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| tamainaṃ tathā pabbajitaṃ samānaṃ sabrahmacārī auvadanti anusāsanti – ‘idaṃ tai khāditabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na khāditabbaṃ; idaṃ tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ; idaṃ tai sāyitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na sāyitabbaṃ; idaṃ tai pātabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na pātabbaṃ; kappiyaṃ tai khāditabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na khāditabbaṃ; kappiyaṃ tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ; kappiyaṃ tai sāyitabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na sāyitabbaṃ; kappiyaṃ tai pātabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na pātabbaṃ; kālai tai khāditabbaṃ, vikālai tai na khāditabbaṃ; kālai tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, vikālai tai  na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ; kālai tai sāyitabbaṃ, vikālai tai na sāyitabbaṃ; kālai tai pātabbaṃ, vikālai tai na pātabba’nti| tassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā yaṃ icchāma taṃ khādāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ khādāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ bhuñ‍jāma, yaṃ  na icchāma na taṃ bhuñ‍jāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ sāyāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ sāyāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ pivāma pipāma (sī॰ pī॰), yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ pivāma; kappiyampi khādāma, akappiyampi khādāma; kappiyampi bhuñ‍jāma, akappiyampi bhuñ‍jāma; kappiyampi sāyāma, akappiyampi sāyāma; kappiyampi pivāma, akappiyampi pivāma; kālaipi khādāma, vikālaipi khādāma; kālaipi bhuñ‍jāma vikālaipi bhuñ‍jāma; kālaipi sāyāma, vikālaipi sāyāma; kālaipi pivāma, vikālaipi pivāma| yampi nau saddhā gahapatikā divā vikālai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ dainti tatthapimai mukhāvaraṇaṃ mañ‍ñai karauntī’ti| sau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kumbhīlabhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| ‘kumbhīlabhaya’nti khau, bhikkhavai, audarikattassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

164. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati| arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya  vācāya anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi sau tattha passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ paricāriyamānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārimhā| saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai saṃvij‍janti khau kulai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhaugā| sak‍kā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātu’nti| sau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āvaṭṭabhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| ‘āvaṭṭabhaya’nti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

165. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, susukābhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati| arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsaina anuddhastaina (sī॰ pī॰) cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, susukābhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| ‘susukābhaya’nti khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayāni, idhaikac‍cai puggalai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitai pāṭikaṅkhitabbānī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

cātumasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. naḷakapānasuttaṃ

166. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati naḷakapānai palāsavanai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā kulaputtā bhagavantaṃ uddissa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā haunti – āyasmā ca anuruddhau, āyasmā ca bhaddiyau nandiyau (sī॰ pī॰) vinayai ca ma॰ ni॰ 1 cūḷagausiṅgai ca, āyasmā ca kimilau kimbilau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), āyasmā ca bhagu, āyasmā ca kauṇḍañ‍ñau kuṇḍadhānau (sī॰ pī॰), āyasmā ca raivatau, āyasmā ca ānandau, añ‍ñai ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā kulaputtā| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau  abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tai kulaputtai  ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā mamaṃ uddissa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, kac‍ci tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū abhiratā brahmacariyai’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| dutiyampi khau bhagavā tai kulaputtai ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā mamaṃ uddissa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, kac‍ci tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū abhiratā brahmacariyai’’ti? dutiyampi khau tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| tatiyampi khau bhagavā tai kulaputtai ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā mamaṃ uddissa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, kac‍ci tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū abhiratā brahmacariyai’’ti? tatiyampi khau tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

167. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ tai kulaputtai pucchaiyya’’nti! atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kac‍ci tumhai, anuruddhā, abhiratā brahmacariyai’’ti? ‘‘taggha mayaṃ, bhantai, abhiratā brahmacariyai’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitaṃ khau, anuruddhā, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhai abhiramaiyyātha brahmacariyai| yaina tumhai anuruddhā, bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā susukāḷakaisā kāmai paribhuñ‍jaiyyātha taina tumhai, anuruddhā, bhadrainapi yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā susukāḷakaisā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| tai ca khau pana tumhai, anuruddhā, naiva rājābhinītā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, na caurābhinītā agārasmā  anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, na iṇaṭṭā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, na bhayaṭṭā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, nājīvikāpakatā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| api ca khaumhi autiṇṇau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithāti – nanu tumhai, anuruddhā, aivaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ  pabbajitaina ca pana, anuruddhā, kulaputtaina kimassa karaṇīyaṃ? vivaikaṃ, anuruddhā, kāmaihi vivaikaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ nādhigacchati añ‍ñaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ ca (ka॰) tatau santataraṃ, tassa abhijjhāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, byāpādaupi cittaṃ  pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, thīnamiddhampi thīnamiddhampi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍campi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, vicikicchāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, aratīpi  cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, tandīpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| vivaikaṃ, anuruddhā, kāmaihi vivaikaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ nādhigacchati añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ’’|

‘‘vivaikaṃ, anuruddhā, kāmaihi vivaikaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ adhigacchati añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ, tassa abhijjhāpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, byāpādaupi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, thīnamiddhampi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍campi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, vicikicchāpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, aratīpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, tandīpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| vivaikaṃ, anuruddhā, kāmaihi vivaikaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ adhigacchati añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ|

168. ‘‘kinti vau, anuruddhā, mayi hauti – ‘yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, appahīnā tai tathāgatassa; tasmā tathāgatau saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaitī’’’ti? ‘‘na khau  nau, bhantai, bhagavati aivaṃ hauti – ‘yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, appahīnā tai tathāgatassa; tasmā tathāgatau saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaitī’ti| aivaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavati hauti – ‘yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā  sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, pahīnā tai tathāgatassa; tasmā tathāgatau saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaitī’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! tathāgatassa, anuruddhā, yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, pahīnā tai ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| saiyyathāpi, anuruddhā, tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau punavirūḷhiyā; aivamaiva khau, anuruddhā, tathāgatassa yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, pahīnā tai ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā; tasmā tathāgatau saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anuruddhā, kaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau tathāgatau sāvakai abbhatītai kālaṅkatai upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau; asu amutra upapan‍nau’’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, anuruddhā, tathāgatau janakuhanatthaṃ na janalapanatthaṃ na lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsatthaṃ na ‘iti maṃ janau jānātū’ti sāvakai abbhatītai kālaṅkatai upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’ti| santi ca khau, anuruddhā, kulaputtā saddhā uḷāravaidā uḷārapāmauj‍jā| tai taṃ sutvā tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharanti| taisaṃ taṃ, anuruddhā, hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya’’|

169. ‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); sau bhagavatā byākatau – añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahī’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃpañ‍ñau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃvihārī sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhunau phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’ti| sau  khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā  – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñau… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhunau phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñau… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ  upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhunau phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñau… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi  itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhunau phāsuvihārau hauti|

170. ‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhunī suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahī’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃpañ‍ñā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃvihārinī sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhuniyā phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhunī suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinī anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārinī… aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi  khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhuniyā phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhunī suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminī sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārinī… aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhuniyā phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, bhikkhunī suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārinī… aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī  tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, bhikkhuniyā phāsuvihārau hauti|

171. ‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsakau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau upāsakau kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā  laukā’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau  sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃpañ‍ñau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃvihārī sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, upāsakassa phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsakau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau upāsakau kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau… aivaṃpañ‍ñau… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, upāsakassa phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsakau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau upāsakau kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| sau khau panassa āyasmā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau vā hauti anussavassutau vā – ‘aivaṃsīlau sau āyasmā ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammau…pai॰…  aivaṃpañ‍ñau… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau sau āyasmā ahausi itipī’ti| sau tassa saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantau tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā upāsakassa phāsuvihārau hauti|

172. ‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsikā suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā upāsikā kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinī anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārinī… aivaṃvimuttā  sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya cittaṃ  upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, upāsikāya phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsikā suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā upāsikā kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminī sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārinī… aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya  cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, upāsikāya phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘idhānuruddhā, upāsikā suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmā upāsikā kālaṅkatā; sā bhagavatā byākatā – tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| sā khau panassā bhaginī sāmaṃ diṭṭhā vā hauti anussavassutā vā – ‘aivaṃsīlā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃdhammā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃpañ‍ñā sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃvihārinī sā bhaginī ahausi itipi, aivaṃvimuttā sā bhaginī ahausi itipī’ti| sā tassā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarantī tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati| aivampi khau, anuruddhā, upāsikāya phāsuvihārau hauti|

‘‘iti  khau, anuruddhā, tathāgatau na janakuhanatthaṃ na janalapanatthaṃ na lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsatthaṃ na ‘iti maṃ janau jānātū’ti sāvakai abbhatītai kālaṅkatai upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’ti| santi ca khau, anuruddhā, kulaputtā saddhā uḷāravaidā uḷārapāmauj‍jā| tai taṃ sutvā tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharanti| taisaṃ taṃ, anuruddhā, hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

naḷakapānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. gauliyānisuttaṃ

173. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina gauliyāni gulissāni (sī॰ pī॰), gaulissāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāma bhikkhu ārañ‍ñikau ārañ‍ñakau (sabbattha) padasamācārau padarasamācārau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saṅghamajjhai ausaṭau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau gauliyāniṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina sabrahmacārīsu sagāravaina bhavitabbaṃ sappatissaina| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau sabrahmacārīsu agāravau hauti appatissau, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina, yau ayamāyasmā sabrahmacārīsu agāravau hauti appatissau’ti – tassa appatissautissa (sī॰ pī॰) bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina sabrahmacārīsu sagāravaina bhavitabbaṃ sappatissaina|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ – ‘iti thairai ca bhikkhū nānupakhaj‍ja nisīdissāmi navai ca bhikkhū na āsanaina paṭibāhissāmī’ti| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau  bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau na āsanakusalau hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina, yau ayamāyasmā āsanakusalau na hautī’ti yau ayamāyasmā ābhisamācārikampi dhammaṃ na jānātīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina ābhisamācārikaupi dhammau jānitabbau| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau ābhisamācārikampi dhammaṃ na jānāti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā ābhisamācārikampi dhammaṃ  na jānātī’ti  – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina ābhisamācārikaupi dhammau jānitabbau ayaṃ ābhisamācārikatatiyavārau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu na dissati|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina nātikālaina gāmau pavisitabbau nātidivā na divā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) paṭik‍kamitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau atikālaina gāmaṃ pavisati atidivā paṭik‍kamati, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā atikālaina gāmaṃ pavisati atidivā paṭik‍kamatī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina nātikālaina gāmau pavisitabbau, nātidivā paṭik‍kamitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina na  puraibhattaṃ  pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘ayaṃ nūnimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina viharatau vikālacariyā bahulīkatā, tamainaṃ saṅghagatampi samudācaratī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina na puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina anuddhataina bhavitabbaṃ acapalaina| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau uddhatau hauti capalau, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘idaṃ nūnimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina viharatau uddhac‍caṃ cāpalyaṃ bahulīkataṃ, tamainaṃ saṅghagatampi samudācaratī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina anuddhataina bhavitabbaṃ acapalaina|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina amukharaina bhavitabbaṃ avikiṇṇavācaina| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau mukharau hauti  vikiṇṇavācau, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā mukharau vikiṇṇavācau’ti – tassa bhavanti  vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina amukharaina bhavitabbaṃ avikiṇṇavācaina|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina suvacaina subbacaina (sī॰ ka॰) bhavitabbaṃ kalyāṇamittaina| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu saṅghagatau saṅghai viharantau dubbacau hauti pāpamittau, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā dubbacau pāpamittau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā saṅghagataina saṅghai viharantaina suvacaina bhavitabbaṃ kalyāṇamittaina|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā indriyaisu guttadvāraina bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu indriyaisu aguttadvārau hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā  indriyaisu aguttadvārau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā indriyaisu guttadvāraina bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunā bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunā  bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā jāgariyaṃ anuyuttaina bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttau hauti, tassa  bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā jāgariyaṃ anuyuttaina bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā āraddhavīriyaina bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu kusītau hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā kusītau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā āraddhavīriyaina bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā upaṭṭhitassatinā bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu muṭṭhassatī hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā muṭṭhassatī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā upaṭṭhitassatinā bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā samāhitaina bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu asamāhitau hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina  yau ayamāyasmā asamāhitau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā samāhitaina bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā pañ‍ñavatā bhavitabbaṃ| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu duppañ‍ñau hauti, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā duppañ‍ñau’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā pañ‍ñavatā bhavitabbaṃ|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā abhidhammai abhivinayai yaugau karaṇīyau| santāvusau, ārañ‍ñikaṃ bhikkhuṃ abhidhammai abhivinayai pañhaṃ pucchitārau| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu abhidhammai abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyati, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā abhidhammai abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyatī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā  ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā abhidhammai abhivinayai yaugau karaṇīyau|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tattha yaugau karaṇīyau| santāvusau, ārañ‍ñikaṃ bhikkhuṃ yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tattha pañhaṃ pucchitārau| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyati, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyatī’ti – tassa  bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tattha yaugau karaṇīyau|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināvusau, bhikkhunā uttari manussadhammai yaugau karaṇīyau| santāvusau, ārañ‍ñikaṃ bhikkhuṃ uttari manussadhammai pañhaṃ pucchitārau| sacai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu uttari manussadhammai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyati, tassa bhavanti vattārau| ‘kiṃ panimassāyasmatau ārañ‍ñikassa aikassārañ‍ñai sairivihāraina yau ayamāyasmā yassatthāya pabbajitau tamatthaṃ na jānātī’ti – tassa bhavanti vattārau| tasmā ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā uttari manussadhammai yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ārañ‍ñikainaiva nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā imai dhammā samādāya vattitabbā udāhu gāmantavihārināpī’’ti? ‘‘ārañ‍ñikaināpi khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunā imai dhammā samādāya vattitabbā pagaiva gāmantavihārinā’’ti|

gauliyānisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kīṭāgirisuttaṃ

174. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kāsīsu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā rattibhaujanaṃ (ka॰) bhuñ‍jāmi| añ‍ñatra khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānau appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāmi appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha, tumhaipi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jatha| añ‍ñatra khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau bhagavā kāsīsu anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kīṭāgiri nāma kāsīnaṃ nigamau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā kīṭāgirismiṃ viharati kāsīnaṃ nigamai|

175. taina khau pana samayaina assajipunabbasukā nāma bhikkhū kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsikā haunti| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘bhagavā khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jati bhikkhusaṅghau ca| añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānanti appātaṅkatañ‍ca  lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha, tumhaipi, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jatha| añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, tumhaipi rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayaṃ khau, āvusau, sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai| tai mayaṃ sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jamānā pātau ca divā ca vikālai appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāma appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| tai mayaṃ kiṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvissāma? sāyañ‍caiva mayaṃ bhuñ‍jissāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai’’ti|

yatau  khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū sañ‍ñāpaituṃ, atha yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū aitadavaucumha – ‘bhagavā khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jati bhikkhusaṅghau ca; añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānanti appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha, tumhaipi, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jatha| añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, tumhaipi rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū amhai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayaṃ khau, āvusau, sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai| tai mayaṃ sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jamānā pātau ca divā ca vikālai appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāma appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| tai mayaṃ kiṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvissāma? sāyañ‍caiva mayaṃ bhuñ‍jissāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai’ti| yatau khau mayaṃ, bhantai, nāsakkhimha assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū sañ‍ñāpaituṃ, atha mayaṃ aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaimā’’ti|

176. atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū āmantaihi – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, sambahulā bhikkhū tumhai upasaṅkamitvā  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘bhagavā khau, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jati bhikkhusaṅghau ca| añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānanti appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha, tumhaipi, āvusau, añ‍ñatraiva rattibhaujanā bhuñ‍jatha| añ‍ñatra khau panāvusau, tumhaipi rattibhaujanā  bhuñ‍jamānā  appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai  kira kiṃ nu (ka॰), bhikkhavai, tumhai tai bhikkhū aivaṃ avacuttha – ‘mayaṃ khau panāvusau, sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai| tai mayaṃ sāyañ‍caiva bhuñ‍jamānā pātau ca divā ca vikālai appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāma appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| tai mayaṃ kiṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvissāma? sāyañ‍caiva mayaṃ bhuñ‍jissāma pātau ca divā ca vikālai’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

177. ‘‘kiṃ nu mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tassa akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanu mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha idhaikac‍cassa yaṃ aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, idha panaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, idha panaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, idha panaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

178. ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā  abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadāmi| mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ  aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ  sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā  dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadāmi|

179. ‘‘mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadāmi| mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadāmi|

180. ‘‘mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya  – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ pajahathā’ti vadāmi’’| mayā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātaṃ abhavissa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya  – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivāhaṃ ajānantau ‘aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadaiyyaṃ; api nu mai aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, patirūpaṃ abhavissā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasmā ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ  viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya – ‘idhaikac‍cassa aivarūpaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, tasmāhaṃ ‘aivarūpaṃ  adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti vadāmi|

181. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbaisaṃyaiva bhikkhūnaṃ ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbaisaṃyaiva bhikkhūnaṃ ‘na appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, tathārūpānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ ‘na appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? kataṃ taisaṃ appamādaina| abhabbā tai pamaj‍jituṃ| yai ca khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saikkhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, tathārūpānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāmimai āyasmantau anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānā kalyāṇamittai bhajamānā indriyāni saman‍nānayamānā – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyunti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

182. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai satta? ubhataubhāgavimuttau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau, kāyasakkhi, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau, dhammānusārī, saddhānusārī|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ubhataubhāgavimuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā phassitvā (sī॰ pī॰) viharati pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā  āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau ubhataubhāgavimuttau imassa khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘na appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? kataṃ tassa appamādaina| abhabbau sau pamaj‍jituṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pañ‍ñāvimuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai na kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau  pañ‍ñāvimuttau| imassapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘na appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? kataṃ tassa appamādaina| abhabbau sau pamaj‍jituṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyasakkhi? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā  tai kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā aikac‍cai āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyasakkhi| imassa khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhippattau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai na kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā aikac‍cai āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti, tathāgatappavaiditā cassa dhammā pañ‍ñāya vaudiṭṭhā haunti vaucaritā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhippattau| imassapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa  haitu? appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau saddhāvimuttau| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai na kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā aikac‍cai āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti, tathāgatai cassa saddhā niviṭṭhā hauti mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau saddhāvimuttau| imassapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni  paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau dhammānusārī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai na kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā aikac‍cai āsavā parikkhīṇā disvā āsavā aparikkhīṇā (sī॰ pī॰) haunti, tathāgatappavaiditā cassa  dhammā pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti, api cassa imai dhammā haunti, saiyyathidaṃ – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau dhammānusārī| imassapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau saddhānusārī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai na kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā aikac‍cai āsavā parikkhīṇā disvā āsavā aparikkhīṇā (sī॰ pī॰) haunti, tathāgatai cassa saddhāmattaṃ hauti paimamattaṃ, api cassa imai dhammā haunti, saiyyathidaṃ – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau saddhānusārī| imassapi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau  kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāti! imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau ‘appamādaina karaṇīya’nti vadāmi|

183. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādikainaiva añ‍ñārādhanaṃ vadāmi; api ca, bhikkhavai, anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā  añ‍ñārādhanā hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā añ‍ñārādhanā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, saddhājātau upasaṅkamati, upasaṅkamantau payirupāsati, payirupāsantau sautaṃ audahati, auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti, dhatānaṃ dhātānaṃ (ka॰) dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, atthaṃ upaparikkhatau dhammā nijjhānaṃ khamanti, dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā sati chandau jāyati, chandajātau ussahati, ussāhaitvā tulaiti, tulayitvā padahati, pahitattau samānau kāyaina caiva paramasac‍caṃ sacchikarauti, pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ ativijjha passati| sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, saddhā nāhausi; tampi nāma, bhikkhavai, upasaṅkamanaṃ nāhausi; sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, payirupāsanā nāhausi; tampi nāma, bhikkhavai, sautāvadhānaṃ nāhausi; tampi nāma, bhikkhavai, dhammassavanaṃ nāhausi; sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, dhammadhāraṇā nāhausi; sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, atthūpaparikkhā nāhausi; sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, dhammanijjhānakkhanti  nāhausi; saupi nāma, bhikkhavai, chandau nāhausi; saupi nāma, bhikkhavai, ussāhau nāhausi; sāpi nāma, bhikkhavai, tulanā nāhausi; tampi nāma, bhikkhavai, padhānaṃ nāhausi| vippaṭipan‍nāttha, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipan‍nāttha, bhikkhavai| kīva dūraivimai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā apak‍kantā imamhā dhammavinayā|

184. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, catuppadaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ yassuddiṭṭhassa viñ‍ñū purisau nacirassaiva pañ‍ñāyatthaṃ ājānaiyya| uddisissāmi vau uddiṭṭhassāpi (ka॰), bhikkhavai, ājānissatha mai ta’’nti? ‘‘kai ca mayaṃ, bhantai, kai ca dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti? yaupi sau, bhikkhavai, satthā āmisagaru āmisadāyādau āmisaihi saṃsaṭṭhau viharati tassa pāyaṃ aivarūpī paṇaupaṇaviyā na upaiti – ‘aivañ‍ca nau assa atha naṃ karaiyyāma, na ca nau aivamassa na naṃ karaiyyāmā’ti, kiṃ pana, bhikkhavai, yaṃ tathāgatau sabbasau āmisaihi visaṃsaṭṭhau viharati| saddhassa, bhikkhavai, sāvakassa satthusāsanai pariyaugāhiya pariyaugāya (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), pariyaugayha (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vattatau ayamanudhammau hauti – ‘satthā bhagavā, sāvakauhamasmi; jānāti bhagavā, nāhaṃ jānāmī’ti| saddhassa, bhikkhavai, sāvakassa satthusāsanai pariyaugāhiya vattatau ruḷhanīyaṃ rumhaniyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) satthusāsanaṃ hauti aujavantaṃ| saddhassa, bhikkhavai, sāvakassa satthusāsanai pariyaugāhiya  vattatau ayamanudhammau hauti – ‘kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru ca aṭṭhi ca avasissatu, sarīrai upasussatu upasussatu sarīrai (sī॰), sarīrai avasussatu (ka॰) maṃsalauhitaṃ, yaṃ  taṃ purisathāmaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina pattabbaṃ na taṃ apāpuṇitvā vīriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ santhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bhavissatī’ti| saddhassa, bhikkhavai, sāvakassa satthusāsanai pariyaugāhiya vattatau dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kīṭāgirisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

bhikkhuvaggau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kuñ‍jara-rāhula-sassatalaukau, mālukyaputtau ca bhaddāli-nāmau|

khudda-dijātha-sahampatiyācaṃ, nāḷaka-rañ‍ñikiṭāgirināmau||


3. paribbājakavaggau

1. taivij‍javacchasuttaṃ

185. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina vacchagauttau paribbājakau aikapuṇḍarīkai paribbājakārāmai paṭivasati| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina aikapuṇḍarīkau paribbājakārāmau yaina vacchagauttau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā yaina aikapuṇḍarīkau paribbājakārāmau yaina vacchagauttau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā| svāgataṃ sāgataṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| vacchagauttaupi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ  nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, aparisai+saṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti, caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti, caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ  ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘yai tai, vaccha, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti, caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti, na mai tai vuttavādinau, abbhācikkhanti ca pana maṃ asatā abhūtainā’’ti|

186. ‘‘kathaṃ  byākaramānā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau assāma, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyāma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘‘taivij‍jau samaṇau gautamau’ti khau, vaccha, byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ  abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya| ahañhi, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| ahañhi, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi| ahañhi, vaccha, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi|

‘‘‘taivij‍jau samaṇau gautamau’ti  khau, vaccha, byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau gautama, kauci gihī gihisaṃyaujanaṃ appahāya kāyassa bhaidā dukkhassantakarau’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, vaccha, kauci gihī gihisaṃyaujanaṃ appahāya kāyassa bhaidā dukkhassantakarau’’ti|

‘‘atthi  pana, bhau gautama, kauci gihī gihisaṃyaujanaṃ appahāya kāyassa bhaidā saggūpagau’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na  tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yai gihī gihisaṃyaujanaṃ appahāya kāyassa bhaidā saggūpagā’’ti ‘‘atthi khau vaccha kauci gihī gihisaṃyaujanaṃ appahāya kāyassa bhaidā saggūpagauti’’| (ka॰)|

‘‘atthi  nu khau, bhau gautama, kauci ājīvakau ājīvikau (ka॰) kāyassa bhaidā dukkhassantakarau’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, vaccha, kauci ājīvakau kāyassa bhaidā dukkhassantakarau’’ti|

‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, kauci ājīvakau kāyassa bhaidā saggūpagau’’ti? ‘‘itau khau sau, vaccha, aikanavutau kappau itau kau vaccha aikanavutai kappai (ka॰) yamahaṃ anussarāmi, nābhijānāmi kañ‍ci ājīvakaṃ saggūpagaṃ añ‍ñatra aikaina; saupāsi kammavādī kiriyavādī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ santai, bhau gautama, suñ‍ñaṃ aduṃ titthāyatanaṃ antamasau saggūpagainapī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, vaccha, suñ‍ñaṃ aduṃ titthāyatanaṃ antamasau saggūpagainapī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

taivij‍javacchasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aggivacchasuttaṃ

187. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘antavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘antavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘anantavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘anantavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva  sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi  bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

188. ‘‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau gautama, sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na  khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi maughamañ‍ñantīti vadaisi (sī॰), maughamañ‍ñanti iti vadaisi (?)| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, antavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – antavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, anantavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – anantavā laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti – aivaṃdiṭṭhi bhavaṃ gautamau’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau  ahaṃ, vaccha, aivaṃdiṭṭhi – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti vadaisi|

‘‘kiṃ pana bhau gautamau ādīnavaṃ sampassamānau aivaṃ imāni sabbasau diṭṭhigatāni anupagatau’’ti?

189. ‘‘‘sassatau laukau’ti khau, vaccha, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhikantāraṃ (sī॰ pī॰) diṭṭhivisūkaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ sadukkhaṃ savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, na nibbidāya na  virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| ‘asassatau laukau’ti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘antavā laukau’ti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘anantavā laukau’ti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, vaccha  …pai॰… ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, vaccha…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, vaccha, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ sadukkhaṃ savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| imaṃ khau ahaṃ, vaccha, ādīnavaṃ sampassamānau aivaṃ imāni sabbasau diṭṭhigatāni anupagatau’’ti|

‘‘atthi pana bhautau gautamassa kiñ‍ci diṭṭhigata’’nti? ‘‘diṭṭhigatanti khau, vaccha, apanītamaitaṃ tathāgatassa| diṭṭhañhaitaṃ, vaccha, tathāgataina – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā, iti vaidanāya samudayau, iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā, iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau, iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau; iti saṅkhārā, iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau, iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau; iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| tasmā tathāgatau sabbamañ‍ñitānaṃ sabbamathitānaṃ sabbaahaṃkāramamaṃkāramānānusayānaṃ khayā virāgā  niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā anupādā vimuttauti vadāmī’’ti|

190. ‘‘aivaṃ  vimuttacittau pana, bhau gautama, bhikkhu kuhiṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaiti’’| ‘‘taina hi, bhau gautama, na upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘na upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaiti’’| ‘‘taina hi, bhau gautama, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaiti’’| ‘‘taina hi, bhau gautama, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaiti’’|

‘‘‘aivaṃ  vimuttacittau pana, bhau gautama, bhikkhu kuhiṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaitī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, bhau gautama, na upapaj‍jatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaitī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, bhau gautama, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaitī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, bhau gautama, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘naiva upapaj‍jati na na  upapaj‍jatīti khau, vaccha, na upaitī’ti vadaisi| aitthāhaṃ, bhau gautama, añ‍ñāṇamāpādiṃ, aittha sammauhamāpādiṃ| yāpi mai aisā bhautau gautamassa purimaina kathāsal‍lāpaina ahu pasādamattā  sāpi mai aitarahi antarahitā’’ti| ‘‘alañhi tai, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇāya, alaṃ sammauhāya| gambhīrau hāyaṃ, vaccha, dhammau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| sau tayā duj‍jānau añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrayaugaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina (dī॰ ni॰ 1.420) añ‍ñatrācariyakaina’’ añ‍ñatthācariyakaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

191. ‘‘taina hi, vaccha, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi; yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vaccha, sacai tai puratau aggi jalaiyya, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ – ‘ayaṃ mai puratau aggi jalatī’’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhau gautama, puratau aggi jalaiyya, jānaiyyāhaṃ – ‘ayaṃ mai puratau aggi jalatī’’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana taṃ, vaccha, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘yau tai ayaṃ puratau aggi jalati ayaṃ aggi kiṃ paṭic‍ca jalatī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, vaccha, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘yau tai ayaṃ puratau aggi jalati ayaṃ aggi kiṃ paṭic‍ca jalatī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ  – ‘yau mai ayaṃ puratau aggi jalati ayaṃ aggi tiṇakaṭṭhupādānaṃ paṭic‍ca jalatī’’’ti|

‘‘sacai tai, vaccha, puratau sau aggi nibbāyaiyya, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ – ‘ayaṃ mai puratau aggi nibbutau’’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhau gautama, puratau sau aggi nibbāyaiyya, jānaiyyāhaṃ – ‘ayaṃ mai puratau aggi nibbutau’’’ti|

‘‘sacai  pana taṃ, vaccha, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘yau tai ayaṃ puratau aggi nibbutau sau aggi itau katamaṃ  disaṃ gatau – puratthimaṃ vā dakkhiṇaṃ vā pacchimaṃ vā uttaraṃ vā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, vaccha, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘na upaiti, bhau gautama, yañhi sau, bhau gautama, aggi tiṇakaṭṭhupādānaṃ paṭic‍ca ajali jalati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tassa ca pariyādānā añ‍ñassa ca anupahārā anāhārau nibbutau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

192. ‘‘aivamaiva khau, vaccha, yaina rūpaina tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya taṃ rūpaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| rūpasaṅkhayavimuttau rūpasaṅkhāvimuttau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivaṃ vaidanāsaṅkhayādīsupi khau, vaccha, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāḷhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, upapaj‍jati  ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti|

‘‘yāya vaidanāya tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya sā vaidanā tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| vaidanāsaṅkhayavimuttau khau, vaccha, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāḷhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti|

‘‘yāya sañ‍ñāya tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya sā sañ‍ñā tathāgatassa pahīnā  ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| sañ‍ñāsaṅkhayavimuttau khau, vaccha, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāḷhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| upapaj‍jatīti  na upaiti, na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti|

‘‘yaihi saṅkhāraihi tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya tai saṅkhārā tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| saṅkhārasaṅkhayavimuttau khau, vaccha, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāḷhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti|

‘‘yaina viñ‍ñāṇaina tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇasaṅkhayavimuttau khau, vaccha, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāḷhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, upapaj‍jati ca na ca upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti, naiva upapaj‍jati na na upapaj‍jatīti na upaiti’’|

aivaṃ vuttai, vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai  mahāsālarukkhau| tassa anic‍catā sākhāpalāsā paluj‍jaiyyuṃ sākhāpalāsaṃ paluj‍jaiyya, tacapapaṭikā paluj‍jaiyyuṃ, phaiggū paluj‍jaiyyuṃ phaiggu paluj‍jaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); sau aparaina samayaina apagatasākhāpalāsau apagatatacapapaṭikau apagataphaiggukau suddhau assa, sārai patiṭṭhitau; aivamaiva bhautau gautamassa pāvacanaṃ apagatasākhāpalāsaṃ apagatatacapapaṭikaṃ apagataphaiggukaṃ suddhaṃ, sārai patiṭṭhitaṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰…  upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

aggivacchasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahāvacchasuttaṃ

193. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dīgharattāhaṃ bhautā gautamaina sahakathī| sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau saṃkhittaina kusalākusalaṃ daisaitū’’ti| ‘‘saṃkhittainapi khau tai ahaṃ, vaccha, kusalākusalaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, vitthārainapi khau tai ahaṃ, vaccha, kusalākusalaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; api ca tai ahaṃ, vaccha, saṃkhittaina kusalākusalaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

194. ‘‘laubhau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, alaubhau kusalaṃ; dausau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, adausau kusalaṃ; mauhau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, amauhau kusalaṃ| iti khau, vaccha, imai tayau dhammā akusalā, tayau dhammā kusalā|
