‘‘pāṇātipātau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; adin‍nādānaṃ khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; kāmaisumicchācārau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; musāvādau  khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; pisuṇā vācā khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; pharusā vācā khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; samphappalāpau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ; abhijjhā khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, anabhijjhā kusalaṃ; byāpādau khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ, abyāpādau kusalaṃ; micchādiṭṭhi khau, vaccha, akusalaṃ sammādiṭṭhi kusalaṃ| iti khau, vaccha, imai dasa dhammā akusalā, dasa dhammā kusalā|

‘‘yatau  khau, vaccha, bhikkhunau taṇhā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, sau hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’ti|

195. ‘‘tiṭṭhatu bhavaṃ gautamau| atthi pana tai bhautau gautamassa aikabhikkhupi sāvakau yau āsavānaṃ khayā sāvakau āsavānaṃ khayā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yai bhikkhū mama sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu bhavaṃ gautamau, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhū| atthi pana bhautau gautamassa aikā bhikkhunīpi sāvikā yā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja  viharatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yā bhikkhuniyau mama sāvikā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu bhavaṃ gautamau, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhū, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhuniyau| atthi pana bhautau gautamassa aikupāsakaupi sāvakau gihī audātavasanau brahmacārī yau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yai upāsakā mama sāvakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā  aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’’ti|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu  bhavaṃ gautamau, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhū, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhuniyau, tiṭṭhantu upāsakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau| atthi pana bhautau gautamassa aikupāsakaupi sāvakau gihī audātavasanau kāmabhaugī sāsanakarau auvādappaṭikarau yau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai viharatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yai upāsakā mama sāvakā gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau sāsanakarā auvādappaṭikarā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharantī’’ti|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu  bhavaṃ gautamau, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhū, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhuniyau, tiṭṭhantu upāsakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau, tiṭṭhantu upāsakā gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau| atthi pana bhautau gautamassa aikupāsikāpi sāvikā gihinī audātavasanā brahmacārinī yā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinī anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yā upāsikā mama sāvikā gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyiniyau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’’ti|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu bhavaṃ gautamau, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhū, tiṭṭhantu bhikkhuniyau, tiṭṭhantu upāsakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau, tiṭṭhantu upāsakā gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau, tiṭṭhantu upāsikā gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau| atthi pana bhautau gautamassa aikupāsikāpi sāvikā gihinī audātavasanā kāmabhauginī sāsanakarā auvādappaṭikarā yā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, vaccha, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai  satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yā upāsikā mama sāvikā gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau sāsanakarā auvādappaṭikarā tiṇṇavicchikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharantī’’ti|

196. ‘‘sacai  hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavaṃyaiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, nau ca khau bhikkhū ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau bhikkhū ca ārādhakā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

‘‘sacai hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, nau ca khau bhikkhuniyau ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau, bhikkhū  ca ārādhakā, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

‘‘sacai hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu, nau ca khau upāsakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā, upāsakā ca gihī  audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

‘‘sacai hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, nau ca khau upāsakā gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

‘‘sacai  hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, nau ca khau upāsikā gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau  ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā, upāsikā ca gihiniyau audātavasanā  brahmacāriniyau ārādhikā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

‘‘sacai hi, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau abhavissa, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā abhavissaṃsu, upāsikā ca gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu, nau ca khau upāsikā gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau ārādhikā abhavissaṃsu; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ abhavissa tainaṅgaina| yasmā ca khau, bhau gautama, imaṃ dhammaṃ bhavañ‍caiva gautamau ārādhakau, bhikkhū ca ārādhakā, bhikkhuniyau ca ārādhikā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau ārādhakā, upāsakā ca gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau ārādhakā, upāsikā ca gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau ārādhikā, upāsikā ca gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau ārādhikā; aivamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ tainaṅgaina|

197. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, gaṅgā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā samuddaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati, aivamaivāyaṃ bhautau gautamassa parisā sagahaṭṭhapabbajitā nibbānanin‍nā nibbānapauṇā nibbānapabbhārā nibbānaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰…  aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘yau khau, vaccha, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati  pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, sau cattārau māsai parivasati| catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya; api ca maittha puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhantā pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhantā upasampadaṃ cattārau māsai parivasanti, catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya; ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi| catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti| alattha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ alattha upasampadaṃ|

acirūpasampan‍nau  khau panāyasmā vacchagauttau addhamāsūpasampan‍nau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā vacchagauttau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāvatakaṃ, bhantai, saikhaina ñāṇaina saikhāya  vij‍jāya pattabbaṃ, anuppattaṃ taṃ mayā; uttari ca mai uttariṃ mai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaitū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, vaccha, dvai dhammai uttari bhāvaihi – samathañ‍ca vipassanañ‍ca| imai khau tai, vaccha, dvai dhammā uttari bhāvitā – samathau ca vipassanā ca – anaikadhātupaṭivaidhāya saṃvattissanti|

198. ‘‘sau tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva yāvadai (pī॰) ākaṅkhissasi – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau assaṃ; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasaiyyaṃ, parimaj‍jaiyyaṃ; yāvabrahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi – ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā  visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya  ubhau saddai suṇaiyyaṃ – dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai cā’ti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi – ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānaiyyaṃ – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ  cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ vītadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ vītamauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ vikkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ mahaggataṃ  cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ amahaggataṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ sauttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ anuttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ asamāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi – ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi; anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – amutrāsiṃ  aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti; iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau  tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi – ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai  duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyyaṃ – imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nāti; iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai  passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai  yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau tvaṃ, vaccha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi – ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇissasi, sati satiāyatanai’’ti|

199. atha khau āyasmā vacchagauttau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā vacchagauttau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā vacchagauttau arahataṃ ahausi|

200. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ dassanāya gacchanti| addasā khau āyasmā vacchagauttau tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca  – ‘‘handa! kahaṃ pana tumhai āyasmantau gacchathā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavantaṃ khau mayaṃ, āvusau, dassanāya gacchāmā’’ti| ‘‘tainahāyasmantau mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatha, aivañ‍ca vadaitha – ‘vacchagauttau, bhantai, bhikkhu bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti – pariciṇṇau mai bhagavā, pariciṇṇau mai sugatau’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau vacchagauttassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, vacchagauttau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘pariciṇṇau mai bhagavā, pariciṇṇau mai sugatau’’’ti| ‘‘pubbaiva mai, bhikkhavai, vacchagauttau bhikkhu caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘taivij‍jau vacchagauttau  bhikkhu mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’ti| daivatāpi mai aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘taivij‍jau, bhantai, vacchagauttau bhikkhu mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāvacchasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. dīghanakhasuttaṃ

201. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai sūkarakhatāyaṃ| atha khau dīghanakhau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau dīghanakhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’’’ti| ‘‘yāpi khau tai aisā, aggivaissana, diṭṭhi – ‘sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti, aisāpi tai diṭṭhi nakkhamatī’’ti? ‘‘aisā cai aisāpi (ka॰) mai, bhau gautama, diṭṭhi khamaiyya, taṃpassa tādisamaiva, taṃpassa  tādisamaivā’’ti| ‘‘atau khau tai, aggivaissana, bahū hi bahutarā laukasmiṃ yai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘taṃpassa tādisamaiva, taṃpassa tādisamaivā’ti| tai tañ‍caiva diṭṭhiṃ nappajahanti añ‍ñañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ upādiyanti| atau khau tai, aggivaissana, tanū hi tanutarā laukasmiṃ yai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘taṃpassa tādisamaiva, taṃpassa tādisamaivā’ti| tai tañ‍caiva diṭṭhiṃ pajahanti añ‍ñañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ na upādiyanti| santaggivaissana, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai khamatī’ti; santaggivaissana, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti; santaggivaissana, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikac‍caṃ mai khamati, aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti| tatraggivaissana, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai khamatī’ti taisamayaṃ diṭṭhi sārāgāya santikai, sañ‍ñaugāya santikai, abhinandanāya santikai ajjhausānāya santikai  upādānāya santikai; tatraggivaissana yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti taisamayaṃ diṭṭhi asārāgāya santikai, asañ‍ñaugāya santikai, anabhinandanāya santikai, anajjhausānāya santikai, anupādānāya santikai’’ti|

202. aivaṃ vuttai, dīghanakhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uk‍kaṃsaiti uk‍kaṃsati (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) mai bhavaṃ gautamau diṭṭhigataṃ, samuk‍kaṃsaiti sampahaṃsati (ka॰) mai bhavaṃ gautamau diṭṭhigata’’nti| ‘‘tatraggivaissana, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikac‍caṃ mai khamati, aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti| yā  hi taisaṃ khamati sāyaṃ diṭṭhi sārāgāya santikai, sañ‍ñaugāya santikai, abhinandanāya santikai, ajjhausānāya santikai, upādānāya santikai; yā hi taisaṃ nakkhamati sāyaṃ diṭṭhi asārāgāya santikai, asañ‍ñaugāya santikai, anabhinandanāya santikai, anajjhausānāya santikai, anupādānāya santikai| tatraggivaissana, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai khamatī’ti tattha viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati  – ‘yā khau mai ayaṃ diṭṭhi – sabbaṃ mai khamatīti, imañ‍cai ahaṃ diṭṭhiṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharaiyyaṃ – idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti; dvīhi mai assa viggahau – yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī  aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatīti, yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – aikac‍caṃ mai khamati, aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatīti – imaihi assa dvīhi viggahau| iti viggahai sati vivādau, vivādai sati vighātau, vighātai sati vihaisā’| iti sau viggahañ‍ca vivādañ‍ca vighātañ‍ca vihaisañ‍ca attani sampassamānau tañ‍caiva diṭṭhiṃ pajahati añ‍ñañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ na upādiyati| aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ paṭinissaggau hauti|

203. ‘‘tatraggivaissana, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti tattha viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yā khau mai ayaṃ diṭṭhi – sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti, imañ‍cai ahaṃ diṭṭhiṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharaiyyaṃ – idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti; dvīhi mai assa viggahau – yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi  – sabbaṃ mai khamatīti, yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – aikac‍caṃ mai khamati aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatīti – imaihi assa dvīhi viggahau| iti viggahai sati vivādau, vivādai sati vighātau, vighātai sati vihaisā’| iti sau viggahañ‍ca vivādañ‍ca  vighātañ‍ca vihaisañ‍ca attani sampassamānau tañ‍caiva diṭṭhiṃ pajahati añ‍ñañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ na upādiyati| aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ paṭinissaggau hauti|

204. ‘‘tatraggivaissana, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikac‍caṃ mai khamati, aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti tattha viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yā khau mai  ayaṃ diṭṭhi – aikac‍caṃ mai khamati, aikac‍caṃ mai nakkhamatīti, imañ‍cai ahaṃ diṭṭhiṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharaiyyaṃ – idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti; dvīhi mai assa viggahau – yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sabbaṃ mai khamatīti, yau cāyaṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sabbaṃ mai nakkhamatīti – imaihi assa dvīhi viggahau| iti viggahai sati vivādau, vivādai sati vighātau, vighātai sati vihaisā’| iti sau viggahañ‍ca vivādañ‍ca vighātañ‍ca vihaisañ‍ca attani sampassamānau tañ‍caiva diṭṭhiṃ pajahati añ‍ñañ‍ca diṭṭhiṃ na upādiyati| aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ paṭinissaggau hauti|

205. ‘‘ayaṃ  khau panaggivaissana, kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau cātummahābhūtikau (sī॰ syā॰) mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsupacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau, anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassitabbau| tassimaṃ kāyaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassatau yau kāyasmiṃ kāyachandau kāyasnaihau kāyanvayatā sā pahīyati|

‘‘tissau khau imā, aggivaissana, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| yasmiṃ, aggivaissana, samayai sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, na adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti; sukhaṃyaiva tasmiṃ samayai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| yasmiṃ, aggivaissana, samayai dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, na adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti; dukkhaṃyaiva tasmiṃ samayai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| yasmiṃ, aggivaissana, samayai adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, na dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti; adukkhamasukhaṃyaiva tasmiṃ samayai vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| sukhāpi khau, aggivaissana, vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā; dukkhāpi khau, aggivaissana, vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā; adukkhamasukhāpi khau, aggivaissana, vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| aivaṃ passaṃ, aggivaissana, sutavā ariyasāvakau sukhāyapi vaidanāya nibbindati, dukkhāyapi vaidanāya nibbindati, adukkhamasukhāyapi vaidanāya  nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ, vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ vimuttacittau khau, aggivaissana, bhikkhu na kainaci saṃvadati, na kainaci vivadati, yañ‍ca laukai vuttaṃ taina vauharati, aparāmasa’’nti|

206. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau piṭṭhitau  ṭhitau hauti bhagavantaṃ bījayamānau vījayamānau (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘taisaṃ taisaṃ kira nau bhagavā dhammānaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pahānamāha, taisaṃ taisaṃ kira nau sugatau dhammānaṃ abhiñ‍ñā paṭinissaggamāhā’’ti| iti hidaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa paṭisañ‍cikkhatau anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci| dīghanakhassa pana paribbājakassa virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| atha khau dīghanakhau paribbājakau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ  ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaiva khau bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina  dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

dīghanakhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. māgaṇḍiyasuttaṃ

207. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau, bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa agyāgārai tiṇasanthārakai tiṇasantharakai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kammāsadhammaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kammāsadhammaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina añ‍ñatarau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha  khau māgaṇḍiyau māgandiyau (sī॰ pī॰) paribbājakau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa agyāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa agyāgārai tiṇasanthārakaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| disvāna bhāradvājagauttaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa nvayaṃ bhautau bhāradvājassa agyāgārai tiṇasanthārakau pañ‍ñattau, samaṇasaiyyānurūpaṃ samaṇasaiyyārūpaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) mañ‍ñai’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bhau māgaṇḍiya, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi  satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| tassaisā bhautau gautamassa saiyyā pañ‍ñattā’’ti| ‘‘duddiṭṭhaṃ vata, bhau bhāradvāja, addasāma; duddiṭṭhaṃ vata, bhau bhāradvāja, addasāma! yai mayaṃ tassa bhautau gautamassa bhūnahunau bhūnahanassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saiyyaṃ addasāmā’’ti| ‘‘rakkhassaitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, vācaṃ; rakkhassaitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, vācaṃ| bahū hi tassa bhautau gautamassa khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi samaṇapaṇḍitāpi abhippasan‍nā vinītā ariyai ñāyai dhammai kusalai’’ti| ‘‘sammukhā caipi mayaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ passaiyyāma, sammukhāpi naṃ vadaiyyāma – ‘bhūnahu bhūnahanau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samaṇau gautamau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi nau suttai aucaratī’’ti| ‘‘sacai taṃ bhautau māgaṇḍiyassa agaru āraucaiyyāmi taṃ āraucaiyyamaitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), āraucaissāmi tassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samaṇassa gautamassā’’ti| ‘‘appaussuk‍kau bhavaṃ bhāradvājau vuttauva naṃ vadaiyyā’’ti|

208. assausi khau bhagavā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya bhāradvājagauttassa  brāhmaṇassa māgaṇḍiyaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa agyāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai tiṇasanthārakai| atha khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau bhāradvājagauttaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘ahu pana tai, bhāradvāja, māgaṇḍiyaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ  imaṃyaiva tiṇasanthārakaṃ ārabbha kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mayaṃ bhautau gautamassa āraucaitukāmā| atha ca pana bhavaṃ gautamau anakkhātaṃyaiva akkhāsī’’ti| ayañ‍ca hi ayañ‍ca hidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhagavatau bhāradvājagauttaina brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ antarākathā vippakatā hauti| atha khau māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa agyāgāraṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau māgaṇḍiyaṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

209. ‘‘cakkhuṃ  khau, māgaṇḍiya, rūpārāmaṃ rūparataṃ rūpasammuditaṃ| taṃ tathāgatassa dantaṃ guttaṃ rakkhitaṃ saṃvutaṃ, tassa ca saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| idaṃ nu tai aitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mai, bhau gautama, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi nau suttai aucaratī’’ti| ‘‘sautaṃ khau, māgaṇḍiya, saddārāmaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ  khau, māgaṇḍiya gandhārāmaṃ… jivhā khau, māgaṇḍiya, rasārāmā rasaratā rasasammuditā| sā tathāgatassa dantā guttā rakkhitā saṃvutā, tassā ca saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| idaṃ nu tai aitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mai, bhau gautama, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi nau suttai aucaratī’’ti| ‘‘kāyau khau, māgaṇḍiya, phauṭṭhabbārāmau phauṭṭhabbaratau…pai॰… manau khau, māgaṇḍiya, dhammārāmau dhammaratau dhammasammuditau| sau tathāgatassa dantau guttau rakkhitau saṃvutau, tassa ca saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| idaṃ nu tai aitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mai, bhau  gautama, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘bhūnahu samaṇau gautamau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhi nau suttai aucaratī’’ti|

210. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya – ‘idhaikac‍cau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi  rūpaihi paricāritapubbau assa iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sau aparaina samayaina rūpānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā rūpataṇhaṃ pahāya rūpapariḷāhaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau viharaiyya| imassa pana tai, māgaṇḍiya, kimassa vacanīya’’’nti? ‘‘na kiñ‍ci, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya – ‘idhaikac‍cau sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi  gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi paricāritapubbau assa iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sau aparaina samayaina phauṭṭhabbānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā phauṭṭhabbataṇhaṃ pahāya phauṭṭhabbapariḷāhaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau viharaiyya| imassa pana tai, māgaṇḍiya, kimassa vacanīya’’’nti? ‘‘na kiñ‍ci, bhau gautama’’|

211. ‘‘ahaṃ  khau pana, māgaṇḍiya, pubbai agāriyabhūtau samānau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraisiṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi| tassa mayhaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, tayau pāsādā ahaisuṃ – aikau vassikau, aikau haimantikau, aikau gimhikau| sau khau ahaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, vassikai pāsādai vassikai cattārau vassikai pāsādai cattārau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) māsai nippurisaihi tūriyaihi turiyaihi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paricārayamānau paricāriyamānau (sabbattha) na haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ aurauhāmi| sau aparaina samayaina kāmānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā kāmataṇhaṃ pahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau  ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau viharāmi| sau añ‍ñai sattai passāmi kāmaisu avītarāgai kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānai kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānai kāmai paṭisaivantai| sau taisaṃ na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? yāhayaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, rati, añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi – api dibbaṃ sukhaṃ  samadhigayha tiṭṭhati – tāya ratiyā ramamānau hīnassa na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi|

212. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiyya cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ| sau tattha nandanai vanai accharāsaṅghaparivutau dibbaihi pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiyya| sau passaiyya gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, api nu sau daivaputtau nandanai vanai accharāsaṅghaparivutau dibbaihi pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi  samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricārayamānau amussa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā pihaiyya, mānusakānaṃ vā pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ mānusakaihi vā kāmaihi āvaṭṭaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| taṃ kissa haitu? mānusakaihi, bhau gautama, kāmaihi dibbakāmā abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā cā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, pubbai agāriyabhūtau samānau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraisiṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi| sau aparaina samayaina kāmānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā kāmataṇhaṃ pahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau viharāmi| sau añ‍ñai sattai passāmi kāmaisu avītarāgai kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānai kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānai  kāmai paṭisaivantai, sau taisaṃ na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? yāhayaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, rati añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi  dhammaihi – api dibbaṃ sukhaṃ samadhigayha tiṭṭhati – tāya ratiyā ramamānau hīnassa na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi|

213. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, kuṭṭhī purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau kimīhi khaj‍jamānau nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānau aṅgārakāsuyā kāyaṃ paritāpaiyya| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau bhaisaj‍jaṃ karaiyya| sau taṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ āgamma kuṭṭhaihi parimuc‍caiyya, araugau assa sukhī sairī sayaṃvasī yaina kāmaṃ gamau| sau añ‍ñaṃ kuṭṭhiṃ purisaṃ passaiyya arugattaṃ pak‍kagattaṃ kimīhi khaj‍jamānaṃ nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānaṃ aṅgārakāsuyā kāyaṃ paritāpaintaṃ|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, api nu sau purisau amussa kuṭṭhissa purisassa pihaiyya aṅgārakāsuyā vā bhaisaj‍jaṃ paṭisaivanāya vā’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama| taṃ kissa haitu? raugai hi, bhau gautama, sati bhaisaj‍jaina karaṇīyaṃ hauti, raugai asati na bhaisaj‍jaina karaṇīyaṃ hautī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, pubbai agāriyabhūtau samānau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraisiṃ, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi| sau aparaina samayaina kāmānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā kāmataṇhaṃ pahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ  paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau viharāmi| sau añ‍ñai sattai passāmi kāmaisu avītarāgai kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānai kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānai kāmai paṭisaivantai| sau taisaṃ na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? yāhayaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, rati, añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi – api dibbaṃ sukhaṃ samadhigayha tiṭṭhati – tāya ratiyā ramamānau hīnassa na pihaimi, na tattha abhiramāmi|

214. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, kuṭṭhī purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau kimīhi khaj‍jamānau nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānau aṅgārakāsuyā kāyaṃ paritāpaiyya| tassa mittāmac‍cā  ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau bhaisaj‍jaṃ karaiyya| sau taṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ āgamma kuṭṭhaihi parimuc‍caiyya, araugau assa sukhī sairī sayaṃvasī yaina kāmaṃ gamau| tamainaṃ dvai balavantau purisā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuṃ upakaḍḍhaiyyuṃ|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, api nu sau purisau iti citicaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘asu hi, bhau gautama, aggi dukkhasamphassau caiva mahābhitāpau ca mahāpariḷāhau cā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, idānaiva nu khau sau aggi dukkhasamphassau caiva mahābhitāpau ca mahāpariḷāhau ca udāhu pubbaipi sau aggi dukkhasamphassau caiva mahābhitāpau ca mahāpariḷāhau cā’’ti? ‘‘idāni caiva, bhau gautama, sau aggi dukkhasamphassau caiva mahābhitāpau  ca mahāpariḷāhau ca, pubbaipi sau aggi dukkhasamphassau caiva mahābhitāpau ca mahāpariḷāhau ca| asu ca asu hi ca (sī॰ pī॰), bhau gautama, kuṭṭhī purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau kimīhi khaj‍jamānau nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānau upahatindriyau dukkhasamphassaiyaiva aggismiṃ sukhamiti viparītasañ‍ñaṃ pac‍calatthā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, māgaṇḍiya, atītampi addhānaṃ kāmā dukkhasamphassā caiva mahābhitāpā ca mahāpariḷāhā ca, anāgatampi addhānaṃ kāmā dukkhasamphassā caiva mahābhitāpā ca mahāpariḷāhā ca, aitarahipi pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ kāmā dukkhasamphassā caiva mahābhitāpā ca mahāpariḷāhā ca| imai ca, māgaṇḍiya, sattā kāmaisu avītarāgā kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānā kāmapariḷāhaina pariḍayhamānā upahatindriyā dukkhasamphassaisuyaiva kāmaisu sukhamiti viparītasañ‍ñaṃ pac‍calatthuṃ|

215. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, kuṭṭhī purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau kimīhi khaj‍jamānau nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānau aṅgārakāsuyā kāyaṃ paritāpaiti| yathā yathā khau, māgaṇḍiya, asu kuṭṭhī purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau kimīhi khaj‍jamānau nakhaihi vaṇamukhāni vippatacchamānau aṅgārakāsuyā kāyaṃ paritāpaiti tathā tathā’ssa tathā tathā tassaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tāni vaṇamukhāni asucitarāni  caiva haunti duggandhatarāni ca pūtikatarāni ca, hauti caiva kāci sātamattā assādamattā – yadidaṃ vaṇamukhānaṃ kaṇḍūvanahaitu; aivamaiva khau, māgaṇḍiya, sattā kāmaisu avītarāgā  kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānā kāmapariḷāhaina ca pariḍayhamānā kāmai paṭisaivanti| yathā yathā khau, māgaṇḍiya, sattā kāmaisu avītarāgā kāmataṇhāhi khaj‍jamānā kāmapariḷāhaina ca pariḍayhamānā kāmai paṭisaivanti tathā tathā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ kāmataṇhā caiva pavaḍḍhati, kāmapariḷāhaina ca pariḍayhanti, hauti caiva sātamattā assādamattā – yadidaṃ pañ‍cakāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, api nu tai diṭṭhau vā sutau vā rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricārayamānau kāmataṇhaṃ appahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ appaṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau vihāsi vā viharati vā viharissati vā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘sādhu, māgaṇḍiya! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricārayamānau kāmataṇhaṃ appahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ appaṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsau ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau vihāsi vā viharati vā viharissati vā| atha khau, māgaṇḍiya, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vigatapipāsā ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittā vihāsuṃ vā viharanti vā viharissanti vā sabbai tai kāmānaṃyaiva samudayañ‍ca  atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā kāmataṇhaṃ pahāya kāmapariḷāhaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā vigatapipāsā ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittā vihāsuṃ vā viharanti vā viharissanti vā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘āraugyaparamā lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukhaṃ|

aṭṭhaṅgikau ca maggānaṃ, khaimaṃ amatagāmina’’nti||

216. aivaṃ vuttai, māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ bhautā gautamaina – ‘āraugyaparamā  lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’nti| mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhau gautama, sutaṃ pubbakānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘āraugyaparamā lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’nti; tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, samaitī’’ti| ‘‘yaṃ pana tai aitaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sutaṃ pubbakānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ  ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘āraugyaparamā lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’nti, katamaṃ taṃ āraugyaṃ, katamaṃ taṃ nibbāna’’nti? aivaṃ vuttai, māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau sakānaiva sudaṃ gattāni pāṇinā anaumaj‍jati – ‘‘idantaṃ, bhau gautama, āraugyaṃ, idantaṃ nibbānaṃ| ahañhi, bhau gautama, aitarahi araugau sukhī, na maṃ kiñ‍ci ābādhatī’’ti|

217. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, jac‍candhau purisau; sau na passaiyya  kaṇhasuk‍kāni rūpāni, na passaiyya nīlakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya pītakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya lauhitakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya mañ‍jiṭṭhakāni mañ‍jaiṭṭhikāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mañ‍jaiṭṭhakāni (ka॰) rūpāni, na passaiyya samavisamaṃ, na passaiyya tārakarūpāni, na passaiyya candimasūriyai| sau suṇaiyya cakkhumatau bhāsamānassa – ‘chaikaṃ vata, bhau, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti! sau audātapariyaisanaṃ caraiyya| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau purisau tailamalikataina sāhuḷicīraina tailamasikataina sāhuḷacīvaraina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vañ‍caiyya – ‘idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti| sau taṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, paṭiggahaitvā pārupaiyya, pārupaitvā attamanau attamanavācaṃ nicchāraiyya – ‘chaikaṃ vata, bhau, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti!

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, api nu sau jac‍candhau purisau jānantau passantau amuṃ tailamalikataṃ sāhuḷicīraṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, paṭiggahaitvā pārupaiyya, pārupaitvā attamanau attamanavācaṃ nicchāraiyya – ‘chaikaṃ vata, bhau, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti udāhu cakkhumatau saddhāyā’’ti? ‘‘ajānantau hi, bhau gautama, apassantau sau jac‍candhau purisau amuṃ tailamalikataṃ sāhuḷicīraṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, paṭiggahaitvā pārupaiyya, pārupaitvā attamanau attamanavācaṃ nicchāraiyya – ‘chaikaṃ  vata, bhau, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti, cakkhumatau saddhāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, māgaṇḍiya, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā andhā acakkhukā ajānantā āraugyaṃ, apassantā nibbānaṃ, atha ca panimaṃ gāthaṃ bhāsanti – ‘āraugyaparamā lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’nti| pubbakaihaisā, māgaṇḍiya, arahantaihi sammāsambuddhaihi gāthā bhāsitā –

‘āraugyaparamā  lābhā, nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukhaṃ|

aṭṭhaṅgikau ca maggānaṃ, khaimaṃ amatagāmina’nti||

218. ‘‘sā aitarahi anupubbaina puthuj‍janagāthā puthuj‍janagatā (sī॰ pī॰)| ayaṃ khau pana, māgaṇḍiya, kāyau raugabhūtau gaṇḍabhūtau sal‍labhūtau aghabhūtau ābādhabhūtau, sau tvaṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ raugabhūtaṃ gaṇḍabhūtaṃ sal‍labhūtaṃ aghabhūtaṃ ābādhabhūtaṃ – ‘idantaṃ, bhau gautama, āraugyaṃ, idantaṃ nibbāna’nti vadaisi| tañhi tai, māgaṇḍiya, ariyaṃ cakkhuṃ natthi yaina tvaṃ ariyaina cakkhunā āraugyaṃ jānaiyyāsi, nibbānaṃ passaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ bhautau gautamassa! pahauti mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ āraugyaṃ jānaiyyaṃ, nibbānaṃ passaiyya’’nti|

219. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, jac‍candhau purisau; sau na passaiyya kaṇhasuk‍kāni rūpāni, na passaiyya nīlakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya pītakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya lauhitakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya mañ‍jiṭṭhakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya samavisamaṃ, na passaiyya tārakarūpāni, na passaiyya candimasūriyai| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau bhaisaj‍jaṃ karaiyya| sau taṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ āgamma na cakkhūni uppādaiyya, na  cakkhūni visaudhaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, māgaṇḍiya, nanu sau vaij‍jau yāvadaiva kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, māgaṇḍiya, ahañ‍cai tai dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ – ‘idantaṃ āraugyaṃ, idantaṃ nibbāna’nti, sau tvaṃ āraugyaṃ na jānaiyyāsi, nibbānaṃ na passaiyyāsi| sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ  pasan‍nau ahaṃ bhautau gautamassa| pahauti mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ āraugyaṃ jānaiyyaṃ, nibbānaṃ passaiyya’’nti|

220. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māgaṇḍiya, jac‍candhau purisau; sau na passaiyya kaṇhasuk‍kāni rūpāni, na passaiyya nīlakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya pītakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya lauhitakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya mañ‍jiṭṭhakāni rūpāni, na passaiyya samavisamaṃ, na passaiyya tārakarūpāni, na passaiyya candimasūriyai| sau suṇaiyya cakkhumatau bhāsamānassa – ‘chaikaṃ vata, bhau, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti! sau audātapariyaisanaṃ caraiyya| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau  purisau tailamalikataina sāhuḷicīraina vañ‍caiyya – ‘idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, audātaṃ vatthaṃ abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti| sau taṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, paṭiggahaitvā pārupaiyya| tassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā bhisak‍kaṃ sal‍lakattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| tassa sau bhisak‍kau sal‍lakattau bhaisaj‍jaṃ karaiyya – uddhaṃviraicanaṃ adhauviraicanaṃ añ‍janaṃ pac‍cañ‍janaṃ natthukammaṃ| sau taṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ  āgamma cakkhūni uppādaiyya, cakkhūni visaudhaiyya| tassa saha cakkhuppādā yau amusmiṃ tailamalikatai sāhuḷicīrai chandarāgau sau pahīyaitha| tañ‍ca naṃ purisaṃ amittataupi dahaiyya, pac‍catthikataupi dahaiyya, api ca jīvitā vauraupaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya – ‘dīgharattaṃ vata, bhau, ahaṃ iminā purisaina tailamalikataina sāhuḷicīraina nikatau vañ‍citau paluddhau – idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, audātaṃ vatthaṃ  abhirūpaṃ nimmalaṃ sucī’ti| aivamaiva khau, māgaṇḍiya, ahañ‍cai tai dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ – ‘idantaṃ āraugyaṃ, idantaṃ nibbāna’nti| sau tvaṃ āraugyaṃ jānaiyyāsi, nibbānaṃ passaiyyāsi| tassa tai saha cakkhuppādā yau pañ‍casupādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgau sau pahīyaitha; api ca tai aivamassa – ‘dīgharattaṃ vata, bhau, ahaṃ iminā cittaina nikatau vañ‍citau paluddhau paladdhau (sī॰ pī॰)| ahañhi rūpaṃyaiva upādiyamānau upādiyiṃ, vaidanaṃyaiva upādiyamānau upādiyiṃ, sañ‍ñaṃyaiva upādiyamānau upādiyiṃ, saṅkhāraiyaiva upādiyamānau upādiyiṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaṃyaiva upādiyamānau upādiyiṃ| tassa mai upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā  sambhavanti; aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ bhautau gautamassa! pahauti mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ imamhā āsanā anandhau vuṭṭhahaiyya’’nti|

221. ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sappurisai bhajaiyyāsi| yatau khau  tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sappurisai bhajissasi tatau tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, saddhammaṃ saussasi; yatau khau tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, saddhammaṃ saussasi tatau tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍jissasi; yatau khau tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍jissasi tatau tvaṃ, māgaṇḍiya, sāmaṃyaiva ñassasi, sāmaṃ dakkhissasi – imai raugā gaṇḍā sal‍lā; idha raugā gaṇḍā sal‍lā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| tassa mai upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti; aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti|

222. aivaṃ  vuttai, māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘yau khau, māgaṇḍiya, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, sau cattārau māsai parivasati; catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| api ca maittha puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhantā pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhantā upasampadaṃ cattārau māsai parivasanti, catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya; ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi, catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu, upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti| alattha khau māgaṇḍiyau paribbājakau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā māgaṇḍiyau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā māgaṇḍiyau arahataṃ ahausīti|

māgaṇḍiyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sandakasuttaṃ

223. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sandakau paribbājakau pilakkhaguhāyaṃ paṭivasati mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi paribbājakasataihi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmāvusau, yaina daivakatasaubbhau tainupasaṅkamissāma guhādassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina daivakatasaubbhau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina sandakau paribbājakau mahatiyā  paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ  itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| addasā khau sandakau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaisi  – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha; ayaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau āgacchati samaṇau ānandau| yāvatā khau pana samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā kausambiyaṃ paṭivasanti, ayaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau samaṇau ānandau| appasaddakāmā khau pana tai āyasmantau appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādinau; appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| atha khau tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

224. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina sandakau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sandakau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau bhavaṃ ānandau, svāgataṃ bhautau ānandassa| cirassaṃ khau bhavaṃ ānandau imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu bhavaṃ ānandau, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi khau āyasmā ānandau  pañ‍ñattai āsanai| sandakaupi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sandakaṃ paribbājakaṃ āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca – ‘‘kāyanuttha, sandaka, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘tiṭṭhataisā, bhau ānanda, kathā yāya mayaṃ aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā| naisā bhautau ānandassa kathā dul‍labhā bhavissati pacchāpi savanāya| sādhu vata bhavantaṃyaiva ānandaṃ paṭibhātu sakai ācariyakai dhammīkathā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, sandaka, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhau’’ti khau sandakau paribbājakau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca – ‘‘cattāraumai, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina abrahmacariyavāsā akkhātā cattāri ca anassāsikāni brahmacariyāni akkhātāni, yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca vasantau vā (sī॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi anārādhanapakkhai nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti| ‘‘katamai pana tai, bhau ānanda, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattārau abrahmacariyavāsā akkhātā, yattha viñ‍ñū  purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti?

225. ‘‘idha, sandaka, aikac‍cau satthā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi paraulaukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidainti| cātumahābhūtikau ayaṃ purisau yadā kālaṅkarauti, pathavī pathavīkāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, āpau āpaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, taijau taijaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, vāyau vāyaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, ākāsaṃ indriyāni saṅkamanti| āsandipañ‍camā purisā mataṃ ādāya gacchanti, yāvāḷāhanā padāni pañ‍ñāyanti| kāpautakāni aṭṭhīni bhavanti| bhassantā āhutiyau; dattupañ‍ñattaṃ yadidaṃ dānaṃ| taisaṃ tucchā musā vilāpau yai kaici atthikavādaṃ vadanti| bālai ca paṇḍitai ca kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’ti|

‘‘tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi paraulaukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidainti| cātumahābhūtikau ayaṃ purisau yadā kālaṅkarauti, pathavī pathavīkāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, āpau āpaukāyaṃ  anupaiti anupagacchati, taijau taijaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, vāyau vāyaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, ākāsaṃ indriyāni saṅkamanti| āsandipañ‍camā purisā mataṃ ādāya gacchanti, yāvāḷāhanā padāni pañ‍ñāyanti| kāpautakāni aṭṭhīni bhavanti| bhassantā āhutiyau; dattupañ‍ñattaṃ yadidaṃ dānaṃ| taisaṃ tucchā musā vilāpau yai kaici atthikavādaṃ vadanti| bālai ca paṇḍitai ca kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti  vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’ti| sacai imassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, akataina mai aittha kataṃ, avusitaina mai aittha vusitaṃ| ubhaupi mayaṃ aittha samasamā sāmañ‍ñaṃ pattā, yau cāhaṃ na vadāmi ‘ubhau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jissāma, vinassissāma, na bhavissāma paraṃ maraṇā’ti| atiraikaṃ khau panimassa bhautau satthunau naggiyaṃ muṇḍiyaṃ uk‍kuṭikappadhānaṃ kaisamassulaucanaṃ yauhaṃ puttasambādhasayanaṃ puttasambādhavasanaṃ (sī॰) ajjhāvasantau kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntau mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhāraintau jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantau iminā bhautā satthārā samasamagatikau bhavissāmi| abhisamparāyaṃ sauhaṃ kiṃ jānantau kiṃ passantau imasmiṃ satthari brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi? ‘sau abrahmacariyavāsau aya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati nibbij‍jāpak‍kamati (sī॰)| ayaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamau abrahmacariyavāsau akkhātau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ  na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

226. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘karautau kārayatau chindatau chaidāpayatau pacatau pācāpayatau saucayatau saucāpayatau kilamatau kilamāpayatau phandatau phandāpayatau pāṇamatipātayatau adin‍naṃ ādiyatau sandhiṃ chindatau nil‍laupaṃ haratau aikāgārikaṃ karautau paripanthai tiṭṭhatau paradāraṃ gacchatau musā bhaṇatau  karautau na  karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pacāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi  pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti|

‘‘tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – karautau kārayatau chindatau chaidāpayatau pacatau pācāpayatau saucatau saucāpayatau kilamatau kilamāpayatau phandatau phandāpayatau pāṇamatipātayatau adin‍naṃ ādiyatau sandhiṃ chindatau nil‍laupaṃ haratau aikāgārikaṃ karautau paripanthai tiṭṭhatau paradāraṃ gacchatau musā bhaṇatau karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pacāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ  gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| sacai imassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, akataina mai aittha kataṃ, avusitaina mai aittha vusitaṃ| ubhaupi mayaṃ aittha samasamā sāmañ‍ñaṃ pattā, yau cāhaṃ na vadāmi ‘ubhin‍naṃ kurutaṃ na karīyati pāpa’nti| atiraikaṃ khau panimassa bhautau satthunau naggiyaṃ muṇḍiyaṃ uk‍kuṭikappadhānaṃ kaisamassulaucanaṃ yauhaṃ puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantau kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntau mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhāraintau jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantau iminā bhautā satthārā samasamagatikau bhavissāmi| abhisamparāyaṃ sauhaṃ kiṃ jānantau kiṃ passantau imasmiṃ satthari brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi? ‘sau abrahmacariyavāsau aya’nti iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| ayaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dutiyau abrahmacariyavāsau akkhātau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

227. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā  saṃkilissanti; natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau  sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti; natthi balaṃ, natthi  vīriyaṃ, natthi purisathāmau, natthi purisaparak‍kamau; sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṅgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti|

‘‘tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya, ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā, ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| natthi balaṃ, natthi vīriyaṃ, natthi purisathāmau, natthi purisaparak‍kamau, sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṅgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti| sacai imassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, akataina mai aittha kataṃ, avusitaina mai aittha vusitaṃ| ubhaupi mayaṃ aittha samasamā sāmañ‍ñaṃ pattā, yau cāhaṃ na vadāmi ‘ubhau ahaitū appac‍cayā visujjhissāmā’ti| atiraikaṃ khau panimassa bhautau satthunau naggiyaṃ muṇḍiyaṃ uk‍kuṭikappadhānaṃ kaisamassulaucanaṃ yauhaṃ puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantau kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntau mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhāraintau jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantau iminā bhautā satthārā samasamagatikau bhavissāmi| abhisamparāyaṃ sauhaṃ kiṃ jānantau kiṃ passantau imasmiṃ satthari brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi? ‘sau abrahmacariyavāsau aya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā  brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| ayaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina tatiyau abrahmacariyavāsau akkhātau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

228. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā animmātā vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā, tai na iñ‍janti na vipariṇamanti na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| katamai satta? pathavīkāyau āpaukāyau taijaukāyau vāyaukāyau sukhai dukkhai jīvai sattamai – imai sattakāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā  animmātā  vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā| tai na iñ‍janti na vipariṇamanti na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti| nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| tattha natthi hantā vā ghātaitā vā sautā vā sāvaitā vā viñ‍ñātā vā viñ‍ñāpaitā vā| yaupi tiṇhaina satthaina sīsaṃ chindati, na kauci kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupaiti| sattan‍naṃtvaiva kāyānamantaraina satthaṃ vivaramanupatati| cuddasa khau panimāni yaunipamukhasatasahassāni saṭṭhi ca satāni cha ca satāni pañ‍ca ca kammunau satāni pañ‍ca ca kammāni tīṇi  ca kammāni, kammai ca aḍḍhakammai ca, dvaṭṭhipaṭipadā, dvaṭṭhantarakappā, chaḷābhijātiyau, aṭṭha purisabhūmiyau, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa ājīvakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa paribbājakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa  nāgāvāsasatai, vīsai indriyasatai, tiṃsai nirayasatai, chattiṃsa rajaudhātuyau, satta sañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta asañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta nigaṇṭhigabbhā, satta daivā, satta mānusā, satta paisācā, satta sarā, satta pavuṭā, satta papātā, satta papātasatāni, satta supinā, satta supinasatāni, cul‍lāsīti cūḷāsīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mahākappinau mahākappunau (sī॰ pī॰) satasahassāni, yāni bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| tattha natthi imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā aparipak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ paripācaissāmi, paripak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ phussa phussa byantiṃ karissāmīti| haivaṃ natthi dauṇamitai sukhadukkhai pariyantakatai saṃsārai, natthi hāyanavaḍḍhanai, natthi uk‍kaṃsāvakaṃsai| saiyyathāpi nāma suttaguḷai khittai nibbaiṭhiyamānamaiva palaiti, aivamaiva bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī’ti|

‘‘tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – sattimai kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā animmātā vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā| tai na iñ‍janti na vipariṇamanti na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti| nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| katamai satta? pathavīkāyau āpaukāyau taijaukāyau vāyaukāyau sukhai dukkhai jīvai sattamai – imai satta kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā animmātā vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā| tai na iñ‍janti na vipariṇamanti na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti| nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| tattha natthi hantā vā  ghātaitā vā sautā vā sāvaitā vā viñ‍ñātā vā viñ‍ñāpaitā vā| yaupi tiṇhaina satthaina sīsaṃ chindati, na kauci kañ‍ci jīvitā vauraupaiti| sattan‍naṃtvaiva kāyānamantaraina satthaṃ vivaramanupatati| cuddasa khau panimāni yaunipamukhasatasahassāni saṭṭhi ca satāni cha ca satāni pañ‍ca ca kammunau satāni pañ‍ca ca kammāni tīṇi ca kammāni, kammai ca aḍḍhakammai ca, dvaṭṭhipaṭipadā, dvaṭṭhantarakappā, chaḷābhijātiyau, aṭṭha purisabhūmiyau, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa ājīvakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa paribbājakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa nāgāvāsasatai, vīsai indriyasatai, tiṃsai nirayasatai, chattiṃsa rajaudhātuyau, satta sañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta asañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta nigaṇṭhigabbhā, satta daivā, satta mānusā, satta paisācā, satta sarā, satta pavuṭā, satta papātā, satta papātasatāni, satta supinā, satta supinasatāni, cul‍lāsīti mahākappinau satasahassāni, yāni bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| tattha natthi imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā aparipak‍kaṃ  vā kammaṃ paripācaissāmi, paripak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ phussa phussa byantiṃ karissāmīti, haivaṃ natthi dauṇamitai sukhadukkhai pariyantakatai saṃsārai, natthi hāyanavaḍḍhanai, natthi uk‍kaṃsāvakaṃsai| saiyyathāpi nāma suttaguḷai khittai nibbaiṭhiyamānamaiva palaiti, aivamaiva bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī’ti| sacai pana imassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, akataina mai aittha kataṃ, avusitaina mai aittha vusitaṃ| ubhaupi mayaṃ aittha samasamā sāmañ‍ñaṃ pattā, yau cāhaṃ na vadāmi| ‘ubhau sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmā’ti| atiraikaṃ khau panimassa bhautau satthunau naggiyaṃ muṇḍiyaṃ uk‍kuṭikappadhānaṃ kaisamassulaucanaṃ yauhaṃ puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantau kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntau mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhāraintau jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantau iminā bhautā satthārā samasamagatikau bhavissāmi| abhisamparāyaṃ sauhaṃ kiṃ jānantau kiṃ passantau imasmiṃ satthari brahmacariyaṃ carissāmi? ‘sau abrahmacariyavāsau aya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| ayaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina catutthau abrahmacariyavāsau akkhātau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘imai  khau tai, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattārau abrahmacariyavāsā  akkhātā yattha  viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau ānanda! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattārau abrahmacariyavāsāva samānā ‘abrahmacariyavāsā’ti akkhātā yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalanti| katamāni pana tāni, bhau ānanda, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattāri anassāsikāni brahmacariyāni akkhātāni yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti?

229. ‘‘idha, sandaka, aikac‍cau satthā sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| sau suñ‍ñampi agāraṃ pavisati, piṇḍampi na labhati, kuk‍kuraupi ḍaṃsati, caṇḍainapi hatthinā samāgacchati, caṇḍainapi assaina samāgacchati, caṇḍainapi gauṇaina samāgacchati, itthiyāpi purisassapi nāmampi gauttampi pucchati, gāmassapi nigamassapi nāmampi maggampi pucchati| sau ‘kimida’nti puṭṭhau samānau ‘suñ‍ñaṃ mai agāraṃ pavisitabbaṃ ahausi’, taina pāvisiṃ; ‘piṇḍampi aladdhabbaṃ ahausi’, taina nālatthaṃ; ‘kuk‍kuraina ḍaṃsitabbaṃ ahausi’, tainamhi taina (ka॰), taināsiṃ (?) daṭṭhau; ‘caṇḍaina hatthinā samāgantabbaṃ ahausi’, taina samāgamiṃ; ‘caṇḍaina assaina samāgantabbaṃ ahausi’, taina samāgamiṃ; ‘caṇḍaina gauṇaina samāgantabbaṃ ahausi’, taina samāgamiṃ; ‘itthiyāpi purisassapi nāmampi gauttampi pucchitabbaṃ ahausi’, taina pucchiṃ; ‘gāmassapi nigamassapi nāmampi maggampi pucchitabbaṃ ahausi’, taina pucchinti| tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti…pai॰…  ‘gāmassapi nigamassapi nāmampi maggampi pucchitabbaṃ ahausi, taina pucchi’nti| sau ‘anassāsikaṃ idaṃ brahmacariya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| idaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamaṃ anassāsikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ akkhātaṃ  yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

230. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā anussavikau hauti anussavasac‍cau| sau anussavaina itihitihaparamparāya piṭakasampadāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| anussavikassa khau pana, sandaka, satthunau anussavasac‍cassa sussutampi hauti dussutampi hauti tathāpi hauti añ‍ñathāpi hauti| tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā anussavikau anussavasac‍cau sau anussavaina itihitihaparamparāya piṭakasampadāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| anussavikassa khau pana satthunau anussavasac‍cassa sussutampi hauti dussutampi hauti tathāpi hauti añ‍ñathāpi hauti’| sau ‘anassāsikaṃ idaṃ brahmacariya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| idaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dutiyaṃ anassāsikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ akkhātaṃ yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

231. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā tak‍kī hauti vīmaṃsī| sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tak‍kissa khau pana, sandaka, satthunau vīmaṃsissa sutak‍kitampi hauti duttak‍kitampi hauti tathāpi hauti añ‍ñathāpi hauti| tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā tak‍kī vīmaṃsī| sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tak‍kissa khau pana satthunau vīmaṃsissa sutak‍kitampi hauti duttak‍kitampi hauti tathāpi hauti añ‍ñathāpi hauti’| sau ‘anassāsikaṃ idaṃ brahmacariya’nti – iti viditvā tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| idaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina tatiyaṃ anassāsikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ akkhātaṃ yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

232. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sandaka, idhaikac‍cau satthā mandau hauti maumūhau| sau mandattā maumūhattā tattha tattha tathā tathā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañhaṃ puṭṭhau  samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ – ‘aivantipi aivampi (sī॰ pī॰) mai nau, tathātipi tathāpi (sī॰ pī॰) mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi añ‍ñathāpi (sī॰ pī॰) ( ) sabbattha natthi mai nau, nautipi mai nau, nau nautipi mai nau’ti| tatra, sandaka, viñ‍ñū purisau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā mandau maumūhau| sau mandattā maumūhattā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ  – aivantipi mai nau, tathātipi mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi mai nau, nautipi mai nau, nau nautipi mai nau’ti| sau ‘anassāsikaṃ idaṃ brahmacariya’nti – iti viditvā  tasmā brahmacariyā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamati| idaṃ khau, sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina catutthaṃ anassāsikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ akkhātaṃ yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘imāni khau, (tāni sandaka, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattāri anassāsikāni brahmacariyāni akkhātāni yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau ānanda! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattāri anassāsikānaiva brahmacariyāni anassāsikāni brahmacariyānīti akkhātāni yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na vasaiyya, vasantau ca nārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ| sau pana, bhau ānanda, satthā kiṃ vādī kiṃ akkhāyī yattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti|

233. ‘‘idha, sandaka, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… vitthārau ma॰ ni॰ 2.9-10 kandarakasuttai sau imai  pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi  dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yasmiṃ khau yasmiṃ khau pana (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha  viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰.. dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sandaka, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sandaka, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai  vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati  tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| yasmiṃ khau, sandaka, satthari sāvakau aivarūpaṃ uḷāravisaisaṃ adhigacchati tattha viñ‍ñū purisau sasak‍kaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vasaiyya, vasantau ca ārādhaiyya ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti|

234. ‘‘yau pana sau, bhau ānanda, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau paribhuñ‍jaiyya  sau kāmai’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, sandaka, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau abhabbau sau pañ‍caṭṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ| abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā bhāsituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ, saiyyathāpi pubbai agāriyabhūtau| yau sau, sandaka, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau abhabbau sau  imāni pañ‍caṭṭhānāni ajjhācaritu’’nti|

235. ‘‘yau  pana sau, bhau ānanda, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau tassa caratau caiva tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, sandaka, upamaṃ tai karissāmi; upamāyapidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti| saiyyathāpi, sandaka, purisassa hatthapādā chin‍nā; tassa caratau caiva tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ (jānāti – ‘chin‍nā mai hatthapādā’ti, udāhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau jānāti – ‘chin‍nā mai hatthapādā’’’ti? ‘‘na khau, bhau ānanda, sau purisau satataṃ samitaṃ jānāti – ‘chin‍nā mai hatthapādā’ ti|) (chin‍nāva hatthapādā,) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) api ca khau pana naṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau jānāti – ‘chin‍nā mai hatthapādā’’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, sandaka, yau sau  bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau tassa caratau caiva tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ (ñāṇadassanaṃ na pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti;) (khīṇāva āsavā,) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) api ca khau pana naṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau jānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

236. ‘‘kīvabahukā pana, bhau ānanda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai niyyātārau’’ti? ‘‘na khau, sandaka, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva yai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai niyyātārau’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau ānanda! na ca nāma sadhammauk‍kaṃsanā bhavissati, na  paradhammavambhanā, āyatanai ca dhammadaisanā tāva bahukā  ca niyyātārau pañ‍ñāyissanti| imai panājīvakā puttamatāya puttā attānañ‍caiva uk‍kaṃsainti, parai ca vambhainti tayau caiva niyyātārau pañ‍ñapainti, saiyyathidaṃ – nandaṃ vacchaṃ, kisaṃ saṃkic‍caṃ, makkhaliṃ gausāla’’nti| atha khau sandakau paribbājakau sakaṃ parisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘carantu bhauntau samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyavāsau| na dāni sukaraṃ amhaihi lābhasak‍kārasilaukai paric‍cajitu’’nti| iti hidaṃ sandakau paribbājakau sakaṃ parisaṃ uyyaujaisi bhagavati brahmacariyaiti|

sandakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahāsakuludāyisuttaṃ

237. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā mauranivāpai paribbājakārāmai paṭivasanti, saiyyathidaṃ – an‍nabhārau varadharau sakuludāyī ca paribbājakau añ‍ñai ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva rājagahai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina mauranivāpau paribbājakārāmau yaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā yaina mauranivāpau paribbājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ  nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ  iti vā| addasā khau sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaiti – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu; mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ samaṇau gautamau āgacchati; appasaddakāmau khau pana sau āyasmā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādī| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| atha khau tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā yaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā| svāgataṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā; idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| sakuludāyīpi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sakuludāyiṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

238. ‘‘kāyanuttha, udāyi, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘tiṭṭhataisā, bhantai, kathā yāya mayaṃ aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā| naisā, bhantai, kathā bhagavatau dul‍labhā bhavissati pacchāpi savanāya| purimāni, bhantai, divasāni purimatarāni nānātitthiyānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ kutūhalasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā  udapādi – ‘lābhā vata, bhau, aṅgamagadhānaṃ, suladdhalābhā vata, bhau, aṅgamagadhānaṃ! tatrimai yatthimai (sī॰) samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa rājagahaṃ vassāvāsaṃ ausaṭā| ayampi khau pūraṇau kassapau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; saupi rājagahaṃ vassāvāsaṃ ausaṭau| ayampi khau makkhali gausālau…pai॰… ajitau kaisakambalau… pakudhau kac‍cāyanau… sañ‍jayau bailaṭṭhaputtau… nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau  yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; saupi rājagahaṃ vassāvāsaṃ ausaṭau| ayampi khau samaṇau gautamau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; saupi rājagahaṃ vassāvāsaṃ ausaṭau| kau nu khau imaisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ saṅghīnaṃ gaṇīnaṃ gaṇācariyānaṃ ñātānaṃ yasassīnaṃ titthakarānaṃ sādhusammatānaṃ bahujanassa sāvakānaṃ sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau, kañ‍ca pana sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā garukatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upanissāya viharantī’’’ti?
