239. ‘‘tatraikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayaṃ khau pūraṇau kassapau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; sau ca khau sāvakānaṃ na sak‍katau na garukatau na mānitau na  pūjitau, na ca pana pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| bhūtapubbaṃ pūraṇau kassapau anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| tatrañ‍ñatarau pūraṇassa kassapassa sāvakau saddamakāsi – ‘‘mā bhauntau pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchittha; naisau aitaṃ jānāti; mayamaitaṃ jānāma, amhai aitamatthaṃ pucchatha; mayamaitaṃ bhavantānaṃ byākarissāmā’’ti| bhūtapubbaṃ pūraṇau kassapau  bāhā paggayha kandantau na labhati – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| naitai, bhavantai, pucchanti, amhai aitai pucchanti; mayamaitaisaṃ byākarissāmā’’ti| bahū khau pana pūraṇassa kassapassa sāvakā vādaṃ āraupaitvā apak‍kantā – ‘‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi, kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau, sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai, puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca, adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ, āraupitau tai vādau, niggahitausi, cara vādappamaukkhāya, nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’’ti| iti pūraṇau kassapau sāvakānaṃ na sak‍katau na garukatau na mānitau na pūjitau, na ca pana pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| ak‍kuṭṭhau ca pana pūraṇau kassapau dhammak‍kausainā’’’ti|

‘‘aikac‍cai  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi khau makkhali gausālau…pai॰… ajitau kaisakambalau… pakudhau kac‍cāyanau… sañ‍jayau  bailaṭṭhaputtau… nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; sau ca khau sāvakānaṃ na sak‍katau na garukatau na mānitau na pūjitau, na ca pana nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| bhūtapubbaṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| tatrañ‍ñatarau nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa sāvakau saddamakāsi – mā bhauntau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchittha; naisau aitaṃ jānāti; mayamaitaṃ jānāma, amhai aitamatthaṃ pucchatha; mayamaitaṃ bhavantānaṃ byākarissāmāti| bhūtapubbaṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau bāhā paggayha kandantau na labhati – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| naitai bhavantai pucchanti, amhai aitai pucchanti; mayamaitaisaṃ byākarissāmā’’ti| bahū khau pana nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa sāvakā vādaṃ āraupaitvā apak‍kantā – ‘‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi| kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi| ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau| sahitaṃ mai asahitaṃ tai, puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca, adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ, āraupitau tai vādau, niggahitausi, cara vādappamaukkhāya, nibbaiṭhaihi  vā sacai pahausī’’ti| iti nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sāvakānaṃ na sak‍katau na garukatau na mānitau na pūjitau, na ca pana nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ  sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| ak‍kuṭṭhau ca pana nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau dhammak‍kausainā’’’ti|

240. ‘‘aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi khau samaṇau gautamau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau  ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; sau ca khau sāvakānaṃ sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau, samaṇañ‍ca pana gautamaṃ sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| bhūtapubbaṃ samaṇau gautamau anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| tatrañ‍ñatarau samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau uk‍kāsi| tamaināñ‍ñatarau sabrahmacārī jaṇṇukaina jaṇṇukai (sī॰) ghaṭṭaisi – ‘‘appasaddau āyasmā  hautu, māyasmā saddamakāsi, satthā nau bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaisī’’ti| yasmiṃ samayai samaṇau gautamau anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakānaṃ khipitasaddau vā hauti uk‍kāsitasaddau vā| tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau pac‍cāsīsamānarūpau pac‍cāsiṃ samānarūpau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti – ‘‘yaṃ nau bhagavā dhammaṃ bhāsissati taṃ nau saussāmā’’ti| saiyyathāpi nāma purisau cātummahāpathai khuddamadhuṃ khuddaṃ madhuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anailakaṃ pīḷaiyya uppīḷaiyya (sī॰)| tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau pac‍cāsīsamānarūpau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau assa| aivamaiva yasmiṃ samayai samaṇau gautamau anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakānaṃ khipitasaddau vā hauti uk‍kāsitasaddau vā| tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau pac‍cāsīsamānarūpau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti  – ‘‘yaṃ nau bhagavā dhammaṃ bhāsissati taṃ nau saussāmā’’ti| yaipi samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā sabrahmacārīhi sampayaujaitvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattanti taipi satthu caiva vaṇṇavādinau haunti, dhammassa ca vaṇṇavādinau haunti, saṅghassa ca vaṇṇavādinau haunti, attagarahinauyaiva haunti anañ‍ñagarahinau, ‘‘mayamaivamhā alakkhikā mayaṃ appapuñ‍ñā tai mayaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā nāsakkhimhā yāvajīvaṃ  paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti| tai ārāmikabhūtā vā upāsakabhūtā vā pañ‍casikkhāpadai samādāya vattanti| iti samaṇau gautamau sāvakānaṃ sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau, samaṇañ‍ca pana gautamaṃ sāvakā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantī’’’ti|

241. ‘‘kati pana tvaṃ, udāyi, mayi dhammai samanupassasi, yaihi mamaṃ mama (sabbattha) sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti garukaraunti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍ca khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati dhammai samanupassāmi yaihi bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| katamai pañ‍ca? bhagavā hi, bhantai, appāhārau, appāhāratāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā appāhārau, appāhāratāya ca vaṇṇavādī imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati  paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ samanupassāmi yaina bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā  upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati dutiyaṃ dhammaṃ samanupassāmi yaina bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati tatiyaṃ dhammaṃ samanupassāmi yaina bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā santuṭṭhau itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, imaṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati catutthaṃ dhammaṃ samanupassāmi yaina bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā pavivittau, pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī| yampi, bhantai, bhagavā pavivittau, pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī, imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati pañ‍camaṃ dhammaṃ samanupassāmi yaina bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘imai khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati pañ‍ca dhammai samanupassāmi yaihi bhagavantaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti  garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantī’’ti|

242. ‘‘‘appāhārau samaṇau gautamau, appāhāratāya ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, santi khau pana mai, udāyi, sāvakā kausakāhārāpi aḍḍhakausakāhārāpi bailuvāhārāpi aḍḍhabailuvāhārāpi| ahaṃ  khau pana, udāyi, appaikadā iminā pattaina samatittikampi bhuñ‍jāmi bhiyyaupi bhuñ‍jāmi| ‘appāhārau samaṇau gautamau, appāhāratāya ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, yai tai, udāyi, mama sāvakā kausakāhārāpi aḍḍhakausakāhārāpi bailuvāhārāpi aḍḍhabailuvāhārāpi na maṃ tai iminā dhammaina sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ|

‘‘‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā  ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, santi khau pana mai, udāyi, sāvakā paṃsukūlikā lūkhacīvaradharā tai susānā vā saṅkārakūṭā vā pāpaṇikā vā nantakāni pāpaṇikāni vā nantakāni vā (sī॰) uc‍cinitvā ucchinditvā (ka॰) saṅghāṭiṃ karitvā dhārainti| ahaṃ khau panudāyi, appaikadā gahapaticīvarāni dhāraimi  daḷhāni satthalūkhāni alābulaumasāni| ‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, yai tai, udāyi, mama sāvakā paṃsukūlikā lūkhacīvaradharā tai susānā vā saṅkārakūṭā vā pāpaṇikā vā nantakāni uc‍cinitvā saṅghāṭiṃ karitvā dhārainti, na maṃ tai iminā dhammaina sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ|

‘‘‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā  garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, santi khau pana mai, udāyi, sāvakā piṇḍapātikā sapadānacārinau uñchāsakai vatai ratā, tai antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhā samānā āsanainapi nimantiyamānā na sādiyanti| ahaṃ khau panudāyi, appaikadā nimantanaipi nimantanassāpi (ka॰) bhuñ‍jāmi sālīnaṃ audanaṃ vicitakāḷakaṃ  anaikasūpaṃ anaikabyañ‍janaṃ| ‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, yai tai, udāyi, mama sāvakā piṇḍapātikā sapadānacārinau uñchāsakai vatai ratā tai antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhā samānā āsanainapi nimantiyamānā na sādiyanti, na maṃ tai iminā dhammaina sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ|

‘‘‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, santi khau pana mai, udāyi, sāvakā rukkhamūlikā abbhaukāsikā, tai aṭṭhamāsai chan‍naṃ na upainti| ahaṃ khau panudāyi, appaikadā kūṭāgāraisupi viharāmi ul‍littāvalittaisu nivātaisu phusitaggaḷaisu phussitaggaḷaisu (sī॰ pī॰) pihitavātapānaisu| ‘santuṭṭhau samaṇau gautamau itarītaraina  saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, yai tai, udāyi, mama sāvakā rukkhamūlikā abbhaukāsikā tai aṭṭhamāsai chan‍naṃ na upainti, na maṃ tai iminā dhammaina sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā  garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ|

‘‘‘pavivittau samaṇau gautamau, pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, santi khau pana mai, udāyi, sāvakā ārañ‍ñikā pantasaināsanā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharanti, tai anvaddhamāsaṃ saṅghamajjhai ausaranti pātimaukkhuddaisāya| ahaṃ khau panudāyi, appaikadā ākiṇṇau viharāmi bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi  upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| ‘pavivittau samaṇau gautamau, pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī’ti, iti cai  maṃ, udāyi, sāvakā sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ, yai tai, udāyi, mama sāvakā ārañ‍ñakā pantasaināsanā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharanti tai anvaddhamāsaṃ saṅghamajjhai ausaranti pātimaukkhuddaisāya, na maṃ tai iminā dhammaina sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyuṃ|

‘‘iti khau, udāyi, na mamaṃ sāvakā imaihi pañ‍cahi dhammaihi sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

243. ‘‘atthi khau, udāyi, añ‍ñai ca pañ‍ca dhammā yaihi pañ‍cahi dhammaihi mamaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idhudāyi, mamaṃ sāvakā adhisīlai sambhāvainti – ‘sīlavā samaṇau gautamau paramaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau’ti| yampudāyi yamudāyi (syā॰ ka॰), mamaṃ sāvakā adhisīlai sambhāvainti – ‘sīlavā samaṇau gautamau paramaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau’ti, ayaṃ khau, udāyi, paṭhamau dhammau yaina mamaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

244. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, mamaṃ sāvakā abhik‍kantai ñāṇadassanai sambhāvainti – ‘jānaṃyaivāha samaṇau gautamau – jānāmīti, passaṃyaivāha samaṇau gautamau – passāmīti; abhiñ‍ñāya samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau anabhiñ‍ñāya; sanidānaṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau anidānaṃ; sappāṭihāriyaṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau appāṭihāriya’nti| yampudāyi, mamaṃ sāvakā abhik‍kantai ñāṇadassanai sambhāvainti – ‘jānaṃyaivāha samaṇau gautamau – jānāmīti, passaṃyaivāha samaṇau gautamau – passāmīti; abhiñ‍ñāya samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau anabhiñ‍ñāya; sanidānaṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau anidānaṃ; sappāṭihāriyaṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti nau appāṭihāriya’nti, ayaṃ khau, udāyi, dutiyau dhammau yaina mamaṃ sāvakā  sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

245. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, mamaṃ sāvakā adhipañ‍ñāya sambhāvainti – ‘pañ‍ñavā samaṇau gautamau paramaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau; taṃ vata anāgataṃ vādapathaṃ na dakkhati, uppan‍naṃ vā parappavādaṃ na sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, api nu mai sāvakā aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaiyyu’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘na khau panāhaṃ, udāyi, sāvakaisu anusāsaniṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi pac‍cāsiṃsāmi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); añ‍ñadatthu mamayaiva sāvakā anusāsaniṃ pac‍cāsīsanti|

‘‘yampudāyi, mamaṃ sāvakā adhipañ‍ñāya sambhāvainti – ‘pañ‍ñavā samaṇau gautamau paramaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau; taṃ vata anāgataṃ vādapathaṃ na dakkhati, uppan‍naṃ vā parappavādaṃ na sahadhammaina niggahitaṃ niggaṇhissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| ayaṃ khau, udāyi, tatiyau dhammau yaina mamaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

246. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, mama sāvakā yaina dukkhaina dukkhautiṇṇā dukkhaparaitā tai maṃ upasaṅkamitvā dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pucchanti, taisāhaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi, taisāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaimi pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina; tai maṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ… dukkhaniraudhaṃ… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pucchanti, taisāhaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi, taisāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaimi pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina| yampudāyi, mama sāvakā yaina dukkhaina dukkhautiṇṇā dukkhaparaitā tai maṃ upasaṅkamitvā dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pucchanti, taisāhaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi, taisāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaimi pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina| tai maṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ … dukkhaniraudhaṃ… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pucchanti| taisāhaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi| taisāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaimi pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina| ayaṃ khau, udāyi, catutthau dhammau yaina  mamaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

247. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati… cittai cittānupassī viharati… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, vīriyaṃ ārabhati, cittaṃ paggaṇhāti, padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, vīriyaṃ ārabhati, cittaṃ paggaṇhāti, padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, vīriyaṃ ārabhati, cittaṃ paggaṇhāti, padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, vīriyaṃ ārabhati, cittaṃ paggaṇhāti, padahati| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā cattārau iddhipādai bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ; vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti  upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā pañ‍ca balāni bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ; vīriyabalaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… satibalaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhibalaṃ bhāvaiti… pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā sattabaujjhaṅgai bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti, sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvaiti, sammāvācaṃ bhāvaiti, sammākammantaṃ bhāvaiti, sammāājīvaṃ bhāvaiti, sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvaiti, sammāsatiṃ  bhāvaiti, sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

248. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā aṭṭha vimaukkhai bhāvainti| rūpī rūpāni passati, ayaṃ paṭhamau vimaukkhau; ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati, ayaṃ dutiyau vimaukkhau; subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti, ayaṃ tatiyau vimaukkhau; sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ  samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ catutthau vimaukkhau; sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ pañ‍camau vimaukkhau; sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ chaṭṭhau vimaukkhau; sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ sattamau vimaukkhau; sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ aṭṭhamau vimaukkhau| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

249. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā  mai sāvakā aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni bhāvainti| ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma umāpupphaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathāpi vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ; aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma kaṇikārapupphaṃ  pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathāpi vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ; aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathāpi vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ; aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃ sañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma ausadhitārakā audātā audātavaṇṇā audātanidassanā audātanibhāsā, saiyyathāpi vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ audātaṃ audātavaṇṇaṃ audātanidassanaṃ audātanibhāsaṃ; aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi, passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

250. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā dasa kasiṇāyatanāni bhāvainti| pathavīkasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ; āpaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…pai॰… taijaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… vāyaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… nīlakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… pītakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… lauhitakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… audātakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… ākāsakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti … viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

251. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā cattāri jhānāni bhāvainti| idhudāyi, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, dakkhau nhāpakau nahāpakau (sī॰ pī॰) vā nhāpakantaivāsī  vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni nahānīyacuṇṇāni (sī॰ pī॰) ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ san‍naiyya, sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi sāssa nahānīyapiṇḍī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) snaihānugatā snaihaparaitau santarabāhirā phuṭā snaihaina na ca pagghariṇī; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, udakarahadau gambhīrau ubbhidaudakau ubbhitaudakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tassa naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na uttarāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa  āyamukhaṃ, daivau ca na kālaina kālaṃ sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya; atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya, nāssa na naisaṃ (sī॰) kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau  kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antau nimuggapausīni, tāni yāva caggā yāva ca mūlā sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni paripūrāni paripphuṭāni, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ, uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisin‍nau assa, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa audātaina vatthaina  apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ  parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina  caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

252. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā aivaṃ pajānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau; idañ‍ca pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddhaṃ’| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau sabbākārasampan‍nau; tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘ayaṃ khau maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau sabbākārasampan‍nau; tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā’ti| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā aivaṃ pajānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau; idañ‍ca  pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddha’nti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

253. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimminanti rūpiṃ manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau muñ‍jamhā īsikaṃ pabbāhaiyya; tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ muñ‍jau, ayaṃ īsikā; añ‍ñau muñ‍jau, añ‍ñā īsikā; muñ‍jamhātvaiva īsikā pabbāḷhā’ti| saiyyathā vā panudāyi, purisau asiṃ kausiyā pabbāhaiyya; tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ asi, ayaṃ kausi; añ‍ñau asi añ‍ñā kausi; kausiyātvaiva asi pabbāḷhau’ti| saiyyathā vā, panudāyi, purisau ahiṃ karaṇḍā uddharaiyya; tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ ahi, ayaṃ karaṇḍau; añ‍ñau  ahi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍau; karaṇḍātvaiva ahi ubbhatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ  abhinimminanti rūpiṃ manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

254. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaunti – aikaupi  hutvā bahudhā haunti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā gacchanti, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaunti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai abhij‍jamānā (ka॰) gacchanti, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamanti, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasanti parimaj‍janti, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattainti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, dakkhau kumbhakārau vā kumbhakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatāya mattikāya yaṃ yadaiva bhājanavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya; saiyyathā vā panudāyi, dakkhau dantakārau vā dantakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ dantasmiṃ yaṃ yadaiva dantavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya; saiyyathā vā panudāyi, dakkhau suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ suvaṇṇasmiṃ yaṃ yadaiva suvaṇṇavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaunti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haunti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā gacchanti, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaunti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchanti, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamanti, saiyyathāpi pakkhī  sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasanti parimaj‍janti, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina  vasaṃ vattainti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

255. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇanti – dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, balavā saṅkhadhamau appakasirainaiva cātuddisā viñ‍ñāpaiyya; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā  mai sāvakā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇanti – dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

256. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānanti – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānanti; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānanti; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti  pajānanti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānanti; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṅkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānanti; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānanti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānanti; sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānanti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānanti; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānanti; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānanti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udakapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sakaṇikaṃ vā ‘sakaṇika’nti sakaṇikaṅgaṃ vā sakaṇikaṅganti (sī॰) jānaiyya, akaṇikaṃ vā ‘akaṇika’nti akaṇikaṅgaṃ vā akaṇikaṅganti (sī॰) jānaiyya; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānanti – sarāgaṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānanti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṅkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā  cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānanti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

257. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā  anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau sakamhā gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya, tamhāpi gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya; sau tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgacchaiyya; tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau sakamhā gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ  agacchiṃ, tatra aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ; tamhāpi gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ, tatrāpi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ  aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ, saumhi tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaranti| tatra ca pana mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

258. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai  passanti cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānanti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ  upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passanti cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai  sattai pajānanti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, dvai agārā sadvārā san‍nadvārā (ka॰)| tatra cakkhumā purisau majjhai ṭhitau passaiyya manussai gaihaṃ pavisantaipi nikkhamantaipi anucaṅkamantaipi anuvicarantaipi; aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passanti cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānanti…pai॰… tatra ca pa mai sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti|

259. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā mai sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, pabbatasaṅkhaipai udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau, tattha cakkhumā purisau tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya sippisambukampi sippikasambukampi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sakkharakaṭhalampi macchagumbampi carantampi tiṭṭhantampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau, tatrimai sippisambukāpi sakkharakaṭhalāpi macchagumbāpi carantipi tiṭṭhantipī’ti| aivamaiva khau, udāyi, akkhātā mayā sāvakānaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nā  mai sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tatra ca pana mai  sāvakā bahū abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattā viharanti| ayaṃ khau, udāyi, pañ‍camau dhammau yaina mama sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharanti|

‘‘imai khau, udāyi, pañ‍ca dhammā yaihi mamaṃ sāvakā sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahāsakuludāyisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. samaṇamuṇḍikasuttaṃ

260. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau samaṇamaṇḍikāputtau (sī॰ pī॰) samayappavādakai tindukācīrai aikasālakai mal‍likāya ārāmai paṭivasati  mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi paribbājakasataihi| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati sāvatthiyā nikkhami divā divassa bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau tāva bhagavantaṃ dassanāya; paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā| manaubhāvaniyānampi bhikkhūnaṃ asamayau dassanāya; paṭisal‍līnā manaubhāvaniyā bhikkhū| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samayappavādakau tindukācīrau aikasālakau mal‍likāya ārāmau yaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yaina samayappavādakau tindukācīrau aikasālakau mal‍likāya ārāmau yaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau tainupasaṅkami|

taina khau pana samayaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ  yuddhakathaṃ  an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā|

addasā khau uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha; ayaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau āgacchati pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati| yāvatā khau pana samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti ayaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau  pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati| appasaddakāmā khau pana tai āyasmantau appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādinau; appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| atha khau tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

261. atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā uggāhamānaina paribbājakaina samaṇamuṇḍikāputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘catūhi khau ahaṃ, gahapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi sampan‍nakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ  uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjhaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gahapati, na kāyaina pāpakammaṃ karauti, na pāpakaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, na pāpakaṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti, na pāpakaṃ ājīvaṃ ājīvati – imaihi khau ahaṃ, gahapati, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi sampan‍nakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjha’’nti|

atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati uggāhamānassa paribbājakassa samaṇamuṇḍikāputtassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandi nappaṭik‍kausi| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’’ti| atha khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yāvatakau ahausi uggāhamānaina  paribbājakaina samaṇamuṇḍikāputtaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

262. aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivaṃ santai khau, thapati, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau sampan‍nakusalau bhavissati paramakusalau uttamapattipattau samaṇau ayaujjhau, yathā uggāhamānassa paribbājakassa samaṇamuṇḍikāputtassa vacanaṃ| daharassa hi, thapati, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa kāyautipi na hauti, kutau pana kāyaina pāpakammaṃ karissati, añ‍ñatra phanditamattā! daharassa hi, thapati, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa  vācātipi na hauti, kutau pana pāpakaṃ vācaṃ bhāsissati, añ‍ñatra rauditamattā! daharassa hi, thapati, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa saṅkappautipi na hauti, kutau pana pāpakaṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappissati, añ‍ñatra vikūjitamattā vikuj‍jitamattā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)! daharassa hi, thapati, kumārassa mandassa uttānasaiyyakassa ājīvautipi na hauti, kutau pana pāpakaṃ  ājīvaṃ ājīvissati, añ‍ñatra mātuthañ‍ñā! aivaṃ santai khau, thapati, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau sampan‍nakusalau bhavissati paramakusalau uttamapattipattau samaṇau ayaujjhau, yathā uggāhamānassa paribbājakassa samaṇamuṇḍikāputtassa vacanaṃ|

263. ‘‘catūhi khau ahaṃ, thapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi na caiva sampan‍nakusalaṃ na paramakusalaṃ na uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjhaṃ, api cimaṃ daharaṃ kumāraṃ mandaṃ uttānasaiyyakaṃ samadhigayha tiṭṭhati| katamaihi catūhi? idha, thapati, na kāyaina pāpakammaṃ karauti, na pāpakaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, na pāpakaṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti, na pāpakaṃ ājīvaṃ ājīvati – imaihi khau ahaṃ, thapati, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi na caiva sampan‍nakusalaṃ na paramakusalaṃ na uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjhaṃ, api cimaṃ daharaṃ kumāraṃ mandaṃ uttānasaiyyakaṃ samadhigayha tiṭṭhati|

‘‘dasahi  khau ahaṃ, thapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi sampan‍nakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjhaṃ| imai akusalā sīlā; tamahaṃ kahaṃ (sī॰), tahaṃ (pī॰), thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| itausamuṭṭhānā akusalā  sīlā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| idha akusalā sīlā aparisaisā nirujjhanti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau akusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘imai kusalā sīlā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| itausamuṭṭhānā kusalā sīlā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| idha kusalā sīlā aparisaisā nirujjhanti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau kusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘imai akusalā saṅkappā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| itausamuṭṭhānā akusalā saṅkappā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| idha akusalā  saṅkappā aparisaisā nirujjhanti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau akusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘imai kusalā saṅkappā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| itausamuṭṭhānā kusalā saṅkappā; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| idha kusalā saṅkappā aparisaisā nirujjhanti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau kusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti; tamahaṃ, thapati, vaiditabbanti vadāmi|

264. ‘‘katamai ca, thapati, akusalā sīlā? akusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ, akusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ, pāpakau ājīvau – imai vuc‍canti, thapati, akusalā sīlā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, akusalā sīlā kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? samuṭṭhānampi naisaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘cittasamuṭṭhānā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| katamaṃ cittaṃ? cittampi hi bahuṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ sarāgaṃ sadausaṃ samauhaṃ, itausamuṭṭhānā akusalā sīlā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, akusalā sīlā kuhiṃ aparisaisā nirujjhanti? niraudhaupi naisaṃ vuttau| idha, thapati, bhikkhu kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ  bhāvaiti, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, micchājīvaṃ pahāya sammājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappaiti – aitthaitai akusalā sīlā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘kathaṃ paṭipan‍nau, thapati, akusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti? idha, thapati, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ  kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti  vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau  khau, thapati, akusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

265. ‘‘katamai ca, thapati, kusalā sīlā? kusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ, kusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ, ājīvaparisuddhampi khau ahaṃ, thapati, sīlasmiṃ vadāmi| imai vuc‍canti, thapati, kusalā sīlā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, kusalā sīlā kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? samuṭṭhānampi naisaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘cittasamuṭṭhānā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| katamaṃ cittaṃ? cittampi hi bahuṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ vītadausaṃ vītamauhaṃ, itausamuṭṭhānā kusalā sīlā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, kusalā sīlā kuhiṃ aparisaisā nirujjhanti? niraudhaupi naisaṃ vuttau| idha, thapati, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti nau ca sīlamayau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; yatthassa tai kusalā sīlā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘kathaṃ paṭipan‍nau ca, thapati, kusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti? idha, thapati, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau khau, thapati, kusalānaṃ sīlānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

266. ‘‘katamai ca, thapati, akusalā saṅkappā? kāmasaṅkappau, byāpādasaṅkappau, vihiṃsāsaṅkappau – imai vuc‍canti, thapati, akusalā saṅkappā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, akusalā saṅkappā kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? samuṭṭhānampi naisaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘sañ‍ñāsamuṭṭhānā’tissa  vacanīyaṃ| katamā sañ‍ñā? sañ‍ñāpi hi bahū anaikavidhā nānappakārakā| kāmasañ‍ñā, byāpādasañ‍ñā, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñā – itausamuṭṭhānā akusalā saṅkappā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, akusalā saṅkappā kuhiṃ aparisaisā nirujjhanti? niraudhaupi naisaṃ vuttau| idha, thapati, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati; aitthaitai akusalā saṅkappā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘kathaṃ paṭipan‍nau ca, thapati, akusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti? idha, thapati, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya  bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau khau, thapati, akusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

267. ‘‘katamai ca, thapati, kusalā saṅkappā? naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau – imai vuc‍canti, thapati, kusalā saṅkappā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, kusalā saṅkappā kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? samuṭṭhānampi naisaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘sañ‍ñāsamuṭṭhānā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| katamā sañ‍ñā? sañ‍ñāpi hi bahū anaikavidhā  nānappakārakā| naikkhammasañ‍ñā, abyāpādasañ‍ñā, avihiṃsāsañ‍ñā – itausamuṭṭhānā kusalā saṅkappā|

‘‘imai ca, thapati, kusalā saṅkappā kuhiṃ aparisaisā nirujjhanti? niraudhaupi naisaṃ vuttau| idha, thapati, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; aitthaitai kusalā saṅkappā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘kathaṃ  paṭipan‍nau ca, thapati, kusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti? idha, thapati, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati  cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau khau, thapati, kusalānaṃ saṅkappānaṃ niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

268. ‘‘katamaihi cāhaṃ, thapati, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi  sampan‍nakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjhaṃ? idha, thapati, bhikkhu asaikhāya sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsaṅkappaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāvācāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammākammantaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāvāyāmaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāsatiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāsamādhinā saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina sammāñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhāya sammāvimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau ahaṃ, thapati, dasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi sampan‍nakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayaujjha’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

samaṇamuṇḍikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. cūḷasakuludāyisuttaṃ

269. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau mauranivāpai paribbājakārāmai paṭivasati mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva rājagahai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina mauranivāpau paribbājakārāmau yaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā yaina mauranivāpau paribbājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami|

taina khau pana samayaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya  anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| addasā khau sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ samaṇau gautamau āgacchati; appasaddakāmau khau pana sau āyasmā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādī| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| atha khau tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

270. atha khau bhagavā yaina sakuludāyī paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā| svāgataṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā; idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| sakuludāyīpi khau  paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sakuludāyiṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kāya nuttha, udāyi, aitarahi  kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘tiṭṭhataisā, bhantai, kathā yāya mayaṃ aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā| naisā, bhantai, kathā bhagavatau dul‍labhā bhavissati pacchāpi savanāya| yadāhaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ parisaṃ anupasaṅkantau haumi athāyaṃ parisā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintī nisin‍nā hauti; yadā ca khau ahaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ parisaṃ upasaṅkantau haumi athāyaṃ parisā mamañ‍ñaiva mukhaṃ ul‍laukaintī nisin‍nā hauti – ‘yaṃ nau samaṇau udāyī dhammaṃ bhāsissati taṃ taṃ nau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saussāmā’ti; yadā pana, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ parisaṃ upasaṅkantau hauti athāhañ‍caiva ayañ‍ca parisā bhagavatau mukhaṃ ul‍laukaintā aulaukaintī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nisin‍nā hauma – ‘yaṃ nau bhagavā dhammaṃ bhāsissati taṃ saussāmā’’’ti|

271. ‘‘tainahudāyi, taṃyaivaittha paṭibhātu yathā maṃ paṭibhāsaiyyā’’si| ‘‘purimāni, bhantai, divasāni purimatarāni sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānamānau ‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| sau mayā pac‍cupaṭṭhita’’nti mayā (?) pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaisi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva ārabbha sati udapādi – ‘ahau nūna bhagavā, ahau nūna sugatau! yau imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sukusalau’’’ti| ‘‘kau pana sau, udāyi, sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānamānau ‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti, yau tayā pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaisi kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsī’’ti? ‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau’ti|

‘‘yau khau, udāyi, anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya, sau vā maṃ pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, taṃ vāhaṃ pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ; sau vā mai pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhassa  vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, tassa vāhaṃ pubbantaṃ ārabbha pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ  ārādhaiyyaṃ|

‘‘yau  sau (sī॰ pī॰) khau, udāyi, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyya cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya, sau vā maṃ aparantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ  pucchaiyya, taṃ vāhaṃ aparantaṃ ārabbha pañhaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ; sau vā mai aparantaṃ ārabbha pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, tassa vāhaṃ aparantaṃ ārabbha pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyyaṃ|

‘‘api ca, udāyi, tiṭṭhatu pubbantau, tiṭṭhatu aparantau| dhammaṃ tai daisaissāmi – imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti|

‘‘ahañhi, bhantai, yāvatakampi mai iminā attabhāvaina pac‍canubhūtaṃ tampi nappahaumi sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anussarituṃ, kutau panāhaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissāmi, saiyyathāpi bhagavā? ahañhi, bhantai, aitarahi paṃsupisācakampi na passāmi, kutau panāhaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passissāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānissāmi, saiyyathāpi bhagavā? yaṃ pana maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha – ‘api ca, udāyi, tiṭṭhatu pubbantau, tiṭṭhatu  aparantau; dhammaṃ tai daisaissāmi – imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhatī’ti tañ‍ca pana mai bhiyyausaumattāya na pakkhāyati| appaiva nāmāhaṃ, bhantai, sakai ācariyakai bhagavatau cittaṃ ārādhaiyyaṃ pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇainā’’ti|

272. ‘‘kinti pana tai, udāyi, sakai ācariyakai hautī’’ti? ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, sakai ācariyakai aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau, ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau’’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ  pana tai aitaṃ, udāyi, sakai ācariyakai aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau, ayaṃ  paramau vaṇṇau’ti, katamau sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti? ‘‘yasmā, bhantai, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana sau paramau vaṇṇau yasmā vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthī’’ti? ‘‘yasmā, bhantai, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti|

‘‘dīghāpi khau tai aisā, udāyi, pharaiyya – ‘yasmā, bhantai, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi, tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisi| saiyyathāpi, udāyi, purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ yā imasmiṃ janapadai janapadakalyāṇī taṃ icchāmi, taṃ kāmaimī’ti| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau  purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ – khattiyī vā brāhmaṇī vā vaissī vā suddī vā’’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ – aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttāti vāti…pai॰… dīghā vā rassā vā majjhimā vā kāḷī vā sāmā vā maṅguracchavī vāti… amukasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā nagarai vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi, taṃ tvaṃ icchasi kāmaisī’’’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘āmā’ti vadaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi – nanu aivaṃ santai, tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, udāyi, ‘yasmā, bhantai, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi, tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau paṇḍukambalai  nikkhittau bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca, aivaṃ vaṇṇau attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti|

273. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yau vā maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau  jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau paṇḍukambalai nikkhittau bhāsatai  ca tapatai ca viraucati ca, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya mahāaggikkhandhau – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, rattandhakāratimisāya mahāaggikkhandhau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya mahāaggikkhandhau, yā vā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau  ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yā vā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā, yau vā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau abhidai (ka॰ sī॰), abhidausaṃ (ka॰) abhidauti abhisaddaina samānatthanipātapadaṃ (chak‍kaṅguttaraṭīkā mahāvagga aṭṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā) aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau  – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau  aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yau vā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau, yau vā vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau majjhanhikasamayaṃ sūriyau – imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau majjhanhikasamayaṃ sūriyau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘atau khau tai, udāyi, bahū hi bahutarā daivā yai imaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tyāhaṃ  pajānāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘yasmā vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthī’ti| atha ca pana tvaṃ, udāyi, ‘yvāyaṃ vaṇṇau kiminā khaj‍jaupanakaina nihīnatarau hīnatarau (sī॰ pī॰) ca patikiṭṭhatarau ca sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi, tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisī’’ti| ‘‘acchidaṃ acchira (ka॰), acchida (?) bhagavā kathaṃ, acchidaṃ sugatau katha’’nti!

‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, udāyi, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘acchidaṃ bhagavā kathaṃ, acchidaṃ sugatau kathaṃ’’’ti? ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, sakai ācariyakai aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau, ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau’ti| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā sakai ācariyakai samanuyuñ‍jiyamānā samanuggāhiyamānā samanubhāsiyamānā rittā tucchā aparaddhā’’ti|

274. ‘‘kiṃ panudāyi, atthi aikantasukhau laukau, atthi ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, sakai ācariyakai aivaṃ hauti – ‘atthi aikantasukhau laukau, atthi ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘katamā  pana sā, udāyi, ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau  hauti, musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, añ‍ñataraṃ vā pana tapauguṇaṃ samādāya vattati| ayaṃ khau sā, bhantai, ākāravatī  paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yasmiṃ samayai pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, aikantasukhī vā tasmiṃ samayai attā hauti sukhadukkhī vā’’ti? ‘‘sukhadukkhī, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yasmiṃ samayai adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, aikantasukhī vā tasmiṃ samayai attā hauti sukhadukkhī vā’’ti? ‘‘sukhadukkhī, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yasmiṃ samayai kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, aikantasukhī vā tasmiṃ samayai attā hauti sukhadukkhī vā’’ti? ‘‘sukhadukkhī, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yasmiṃ samayai musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, aikantasukhī vā tasmiṃ samayai attā hauti sukhadukkhī vā’’ti? ‘‘sukhadukkhī, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, yasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñataraṃ tapauguṇaṃ samādāya vattati, aikantasukhī vā tasmiṃ samayai attā hauti sukhadukkhī vā’’ti? ‘‘sukhadukkhī, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, udāyi, api nu khau vaukiṇṇasukhadukkhaṃ paṭipadaṃ āgamma aikantasukhassa  laukassa sacchikiriyā hautī’’ti sacchikiriyāyāti (ka॰)? ‘‘acchidaṃ bhagavā kathaṃ, acchidaṃ sugatau katha’’nti!

‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, udāyi, vadaisi – ‘acchidaṃ bhagavā kathaṃ, acchidaṃ sugatau kathaṃ’’’ti? ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, sakai ācariyakai aivaṃ hauti – ‘atthi aikantasukhau laukau, atthi ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa  sacchikiriyāyā’ti| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā sakai ācariyakai samanuyuñ‍jiyamānā samanuggāhiyamānā samanubhāsiyamānā rittā tucchā aparaddhā’’ti aparaddhā (sī॰), aparaddhāpi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

275. ‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhantai, atthi aikantasukhau laukau, atthi ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi  khau, udāyi, aikantasukhau laukau, atthi ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

‘‘katamā pana sā, bhantai, ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘idhudāyi, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ khau sā, udāyi, ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

‘‘na kiṃ nu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) khau sā, bhantai, ākāravatī paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāya, sacchikatau hissa, bhantai, aittāvatā aikantasukhau laukau hautī’’ti| ‘‘na khvāssa, udāyi, aittāvatā aikantasukhau laukau sacchikatau hauti; ākāravatītvaiva sā paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, sakuludāyissa paribbājakassa parisā un‍nādinī uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā ahausi – ‘‘aittha mayaṃ anassāma sācariyakā, aittha mayaṃ anassāma panassāma (sī॰) sācariyakā! na mayaṃ itau bhiyyau uttaritaraṃ pajānāmā’’ti|

atha khau sakuludāyī paribbājakau tai paribbājakai appasaddai  katvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā panāssa, bhantai, aikantasukhau laukau sacchikatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhudāyi, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… upasampaj‍ja viharati| yā tā daivatā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā tāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati sal‍lapati sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati| aittāvatā khvāssa, udāyi, aikantasukhau laukau sacchikatau hautī’’ti|

276. ‘‘aitassa nūna, bhantai, aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, udāyi, aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti| atthi khau, udāyi, añ‍ñaiva dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti|

‘‘katamai  pana tai, bhantai, dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti? ‘‘idhudāyi, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, udāyi, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, udāyi, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, udāyi, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| ayampi khau, udāyi, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| ayampi khau, udāyi, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya  cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti… ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti…  ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti… ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayampi khau, udāyi, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti| imai khau, udāyi, dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti|

277. aivaṃ vuttai, sakuludāyī paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, sakuludāyissa paribbājakassa parisā sakuludāyiṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ, udāyi, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ cari; mā bhavaṃ, udāyi, ācariyau hutvā antaivāsīvāsaṃ vasi| saiyyathāpi nāma udakamaṇikau maṇikau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) hutvā udañ‍canikau uddaikanikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) assa, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ aivaṃ sampadamaitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) bhautau udāyissa bhavissati| mā bhavaṃ, udāyi, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ cari; mā bhavaṃ, udāyi, ācariyau hutvā antaivāsīvāsaṃ vasī’’ti| iti hidaṃ sakuludāyissa paribbājakassa parisā sakuludāyiṃ paribbājakaṃ antarāyamakāsi bhagavati brahmacariyaiti|

cūḷasakuludāyisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. vaikhanasasuttaṃ

278. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau vaikhanasau vaikhanassau (sī॰ pī॰) paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau vaikhanasau paribbājakau bhagavatau santikai udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau, ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau, ayaṃ paramau vaṇṇau’ti? katamau, kac‍cāna, sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti?

‘‘yasmā, bhau gautama, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana sau, kac‍cāna, vaṇṇau yasmā vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthī’’ti?

‘‘yasmā, bhau gautama, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’’ti|

‘‘dīghāpi khau tai aisā, kac‍cāna, pharaiyya – ‘yasmā, bhau gautama, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi, tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisi| saiyyathāpi, kac‍cāna, purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ yā imasmiṃ janapadai  janapadakalyāṇī, taṃ icchāmi taṃ kāmaimī’ti| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ – khattiyī vā brāhmaṇī vā vaissī  vā suddī vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ ‘aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttāti vāti…pai॰… dīghā vā rassā vā majjhimā vā kāḷī vā sāmā vā maṅguracchavī vāti… amukasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā nagarai vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi, taṃ tvaṃ icchasi kāmaisī’’’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘āmā’ti vadaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ  mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, nanu aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhau gautama, aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ  bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, ‘yasmā, bhau gautama, vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthi sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi; tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisī’’ti| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau paṇḍukambalai nikkhittau bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca, aivaṃ vaṇṇau attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti|

279. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yau vā maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau  jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau paṇḍukambalai nikkhittau bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau, ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya kimi khaj‍jaupanakau, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau, imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau, ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya tailappadīpau, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya  mahāaggikkhandhau, imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, rattandhakāratimisāya mahāaggikkhandhau, ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yau vā rattandhakāratimisāya mahāaggikkhandhau, yā vā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ viddhai  vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā, imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ  viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā, ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yā vā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ausadhitārakā, yau vā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai  daivai abhidau aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau, imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau, ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kac‍cāna, yau vā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau aḍḍharattasamayaṃ candau, yau vā vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau majjhanhikasamayaṃ sūriyau, imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ katamau vaṇṇau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau gautama, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai abhidau majjhanhikasamayaṃ sūriyau – ayaṃ imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti| ‘‘atau khau tai, kac‍cāna, bahū hi bahutarā daivā yai imaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tyāhaṃ pajānāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘yasmā vaṇṇā añ‍ñau vaṇṇau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau  ca natthī’ti| atha ca pana tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, ‘yvāyaṃ vaṇṇau kiminā khaj‍jaupanakaina nihīnatarau ca patikiṭṭhatarau ca sau paramau vaṇṇau’ti vadaisi; tañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ na pañ‍ñapaisi’’|

280. ‘‘pañ‍ca khau imai, kac‍cāna, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā  kantā manāpā  piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, kac‍cāna, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, kac‍cāna, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ| iti kāmaihi kāmasukhaṃ, kāmasukhā kāmaggasukhaṃ tattha aggamakkhāyatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, vaikhanasau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ bhautā gautamaina – ‘kāmaihi kāmasukhaṃ, kāmasukhā kāmaggasukhaṃ tattha aggamakkhāyatī’ti| (‘kāmaihi, bhau gautama, kāmasukhaṃ, kāmasukhā kāmaggasukhaṃ, tattha aggamakkhāyatī’ti) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi – ‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau aitaṃ, kac‍cāna, tayā  añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrayaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina – kāmā kāmaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā kāmasukhaṃ vā kāmaggasukhaṃ vā| yai khau tai, kac‍cāna, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā tai khau  aitaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – kāmā vā kāmasukhaṃ vā kāmaggasukhaṃ vā’’ti|

281. aivaṃ vuttai, vaikhanasau paribbājakau kupitau anattamanau bhagavantaṃyaiva khuṃsaintau bhagavantaṃyaiva vambhaintau bhagavantaṃyaiva vadamānau ‘‘samaṇau samaṇau ca (sī॰ pī॰) gautamau pāpitau bhavissatī’’ti bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaiva panidhaikac‍cai panidhaikai (sī॰ pī॰), panimaikai (uparisubhasuttai) samaṇabrāhmaṇā ajānantā pubbantaṃ, apassantā aparantaṃ atha ca pana ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti – pajānāmā’ti – paṭijānanti itthattāyāti paṭijānanti (pī॰)| taisamidaṃ bhāsitaṃ hassakaṃyaiva sampaj‍jati, nāmakaṃyaiva sampaj‍jati, rittakaṃyaiva sampaj‍jati, tucchakaṃyaiva sampaj‍jatī’’ti| ‘‘yai khau tai, kac‍cāna, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ajānantā pubbantaṃ, apassantā aparantaṃ, ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti – pajānāmā’ti – paṭijānanti; taisaṃ sauyaiva taisaṃ taisāyaṃ (sī॰), taisaṃyaiva sau (?) sahadhammikau niggahau hauti| api ca, kac‍cāna, tiṭṭhatu pubbantau, tiṭṭhatu aparantau| aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau, ahamanusāsāmi ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānau (?) nacirassaiva sāmañ‍ñaiva ñassati sāmaṃ dakkhiti – aivaṃ kira sammā aivaṃ kirāyasmā (syā॰ ka॰) bandhanā vippamaukkhau hauti, yadidaṃ avij‍jā bandhanā| saiyyathāpi, kac‍cāna, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi baddhau assa suttabandhanaihi; tassa vuddhimanvāya indriyānaṃ paripākamanvāya  tāni bandhanāni muc‍caiyyuṃ; sau maukkhaumhīti khau jānaiyya nau ca bandhanaṃ| aivamaiva khau, kac‍cāna, aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi; yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau nacirassaiva sāmañ‍ñai ñassati, sāmaṃ dakkhiti – ‘aivaṃ kira sammā bandhanā vippamaukkhau hauti, yadidaṃ avij‍jā bandhanā’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, vaikhanasau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

vaikhanasasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

paribbājakavaggau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

puṇḍarī-aggisaha-kathināmau, dīghanakhau puna bhāradvājagauttau|

sandakaudāyimuṇḍikaputtau, maṇikau tathākac‍cānau varavaggau||


4. rājavaggau

1. ghaṭikārasuttaṃ
