359. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina aciravatiyā nadiyā tīraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yāvatikā nāgassa bhūmi nāgaina gantvā nāgā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau rājā pasainadi kausalau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, āyasmā  ānandau hatthattharai nisīdatū’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mahārāja| nisīda tvaṃ; nisin‍nau ahaṃ sakai āsanai’’ti| nisīdi khau rājā pasainadi kausalau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau rājā pasainadi kausalau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai ānanda, sau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ kāyasamācāraṃ samācaraiyya, yvāssa kāyasamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihī’’ti brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā? ‘‘na khau, mahārāja, sau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ kāyasamācāraṃ samācaraiyya, yvāssa kāyasamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhantai ānanda, sau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ vacīsamācāraṃ…pai॰… manausamācāraṃ samācaraiyya, yvāssa manausamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihī’’ti brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā? ‘‘na khau, mahārāja, sau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ manausamācāraṃ samācaraiyya, yvāssa manausamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yañhi mayaṃ, bhantai, nāsakkhimhā pañhaina paripūraituṃ taṃ, bhantai, āyasmatā ānandaina pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina paripūritaṃ| yai tai, bhantai, bālā abyattā ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā paraisaṃ vaṇṇaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ vā bhāsanti, na mayaṃ taṃ sāratau pac‍cāgacchāma; yai pana yai ca khau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tai, bhantai, paṇḍitā viyattā byattā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) maidhāvinau anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā paraisaṃ vaṇṇaṃ vā avaṇṇaṃ vā bhāsanti, mayaṃ taṃ sāratau pac‍cāgacchāma’’|

360. ‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, kāyasamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau akusalau’’|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau akusalau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau sāvaj‍jau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau sāvaj‍jau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau sabyābajjhau’’ sabyāpajjhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabyāpaj‍jau (ka॰)|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau sabyābajjhau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau dukkhavipākau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau dukkhavipākau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati tassa akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; aivarūpau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, vacīsamācārau…pai॰… manausamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau akusalau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau akusalau’’? ‘‘yau  khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau sāvaj‍jau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau sāvaj‍jau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau sabyābajjhau’’|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, manausamācārau sabyābajjhau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau dukkhavipākau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau dukkhavipākau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau  attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati tassa akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; aivarūpau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau aupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai ānanda, sau bhagavā sabbaisaṃyaiva akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti? ‘‘sabbākusaladhammapahīnau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau kusaladhammasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

361. ‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, kāyasamācārau anaupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau kusalau’’|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau kusalau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau anavaj‍jau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau anavaj‍jau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau abyābajjhau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau abyābajjhau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau sukhavipākau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasamācārau sukhavipākau’’?

‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati tassa akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā  abhivaḍḍhanti; aivarūpau khau, mahārāja, kāyasamācārau anaupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, vacīsamācārau…pai॰… manausamācārau anaupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau kusalau’’|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, manausamācārau kusalau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau anavaj‍jau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau anavaj‍jau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau abyābajjhau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau abyābajjhau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau sukhavipākau’’|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, manausamācārau sukhavipākau’’? ‘‘yau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati| tassa akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| aivarūpau khau, mahārāja, manausamācārau anaupārambhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai ānanda, sau bhagavā sabbaisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti? ‘‘sabbākusaladhammapahīnau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau kusaladhammasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

362. ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ subhāsitamidaṃ (sī॰), bhantai, āyasmatā ānandaina| iminā ca mayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmatau ānandassa subhāsitaina attamanābhiraddhā| aivaṃ attamanābhiraddhā ca mayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmatau ānandassa subhāsitaina| sacai, bhantai, āyasmatau ānandassa hatthiratanaṃ kappaiyya, hatthiratanampi mayaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa dadaiyyāma| sacai, bhantai, āyasmatau ānandassa assaratanaṃ kappaiyya, assaratanampi mayaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa dadaiyyāma| sacai, bhantai, āyasmatau ānandassa gāmavaraṃ kappaiyya, gāmavarampi mayaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa dadaiyyāma| api ca, bhantai, mayampaitaṃ mayamaiva taṃ (sī॰), mayampanaitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) jānāma – ‘naitaṃ āyasmatau ānandassa kappatī’ti| ayaṃ mai, bhantai, bāhitikā rañ‍ñā māgadhaina ajātasattunā vaidaihiputtaina vatthanāḷiyā chattanāḷiyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pakkhipitvā pahitā sauḷasasamā āyāmaina, aṭṭhasamā vitthāraina| taṃ, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mahārāja, paripuṇṇaṃ mai ticīvara’’nti|

‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, aciravatī nadī diṭṭhā āyasmatā caiva ānandaina amhaihi ca| yadā uparipabbatai mahāmaighau abhippavuṭṭhau hauti, athāyaṃ aciravatī nadī ubhatau kūlāni saṃvissandantī gacchati; aivamaiva khau, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau imāya bāhitikāya attanau ticīvaraṃ karissati| yaṃ panāyasmatau ānandassa purāṇaṃ ticīvaraṃ taṃ sabrahmacārīhi saṃvibhajissati| aivāyaṃ amhākaṃ dakkhiṇā saṃvissandantī mañ‍ñai gamissati| paṭiggaṇhātu, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau bāhitika’’nti| paṭiggahaisi khau āyasmā ānandau  bāhitikaṃ|

atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, gacchāma; bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

363. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau acirapak‍kantassa rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau ahausi rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| tañ‍ca bāhitikaṃ bhagavatau pādāsi| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘lābhā, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa, suladdhalābhā, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa; yaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau labhati ānandaṃ dassanāya, labhati payirupāsanāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

bāhitikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammacaitiyasuttaṃ

364. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati maidāḷupaṃ maitaḷūpaṃ (sī॰), maidaḷumpaṃ (pī॰) nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau nagarakaṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau dīghaṃ kārāyanaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yaujaihi, samma kārāyana, bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni, uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmiṃ dassanāyā’’ti subhūmidassanāyāti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.43)| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau dīghau kārāyanau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭissutvā bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘‘yuttāni khau tai, daiva, bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni| yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhadraṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi nagarakamhā niyyāsi mahac‍cā rājānubhāvaina| yaina ārāmau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva ārāmaṃ pāvisi| addasā khau rājā pasainadi kausalau ārāmai jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau  rukkhamūlāni pāsādikāni pasādanīyāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni manussarāhasaiyyakāni (sī॰ pī॰) paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni| disvāna bhagavantaṃyaiva ārabbha sati udapādi – ‘‘imāni khau tāni rukkhamūlāni pāsādikāni pasādanīyāni appasaddāni  appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni, yattha sudaṃ mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsāma arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti|

365. atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau dīghaṃ kārāyanaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘imāni khau, samma kārāyana, tāni rukkhamūlāni pāsādikāni pasādanīyāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni, yattha sudaṃ mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsāma arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ| kahaṃ nu khau, samma kārāyana, aitarahi sau bhagavā  viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti? ‘‘atthi, mahārāja, maidāḷupaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| tattha sau bhagavā aitarahi viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti| ‘‘kīvadūrai kīvadūrau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pana, samma kārāyana, nagarakamhā maidāḷupaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau hautī’’ti? ‘‘na dūrai, mahārāja; tīṇi yaujanāni; sak‍kā divasāvasaisaina gantu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, samma kārāyana, yaujaihi bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni, gamissāma mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau dīghau kārāyanau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭissutvā bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘‘yuttāni khau tai, daiva, bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni| yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhadraṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi nagarakamhā yaina maidāḷupaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ  nigamau taina pāyāsi| tainaiva divasāvasaisaina maidāḷupaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamaṃ sampāpuṇi| yaina ārāmau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva ārāmaṃ pāvisi|

366. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū abbhaukāsai caṅkamanti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai, aitarahi  sau bhagavā viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau? dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘aisau, mahārāja, vihārau saṃvutadvārau| taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaihi| vivarissati bhagavā tai dvāra’’nti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau tatthaiva khaggañ‍ca uṇhīsañ‍ca dīghassa kārāyanassa pādāsi| atha khau dīghassa kārāyanassa aitadahausi – ‘‘rahāyati khau dāni rājā mahārājā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), idhaiva tainidhaiva (sī॰) dāni mayā ṭhātabba’’nti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina sau vihārau saṃvutadvārau taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaisi| vivari bhagavā dvāraṃ| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau vihāraṃ  pavisitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavatau pādāni mukhaina ca paricumbati, pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati, nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘‘rājāhaṃ, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau; rājāhaṃ, bhantai, pasainadi  kausalau’’ti|

367. ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, mahārāja, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau imasmiṃ sarīrai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karausi, mittūpahāraṃ cittūpahāraṃ (sī॰) upadaṃsaisī’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau  – ‘hauti sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti| idhāhaṃ, bhantai, passāmi aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇai pariyantakataṃ brahmacariyaṃ carantai dasapi vassāni, vīsampi vassāni, tiṃsampi vassāni, cattārīsampi vassāni| tai aparaina samayaina sunhātā suvilittā kappitakaisamassū pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārainti| idha panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū passāmi yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carantai| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ aivaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ samanupassāmi| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’’ti|

368. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, rājānaupi rājūhi vivadanti, khattiyāpi khattiyaihi vivadanti, brāhmaṇāpi brāhmaṇaihi vivadanti, gahapatayaupi gahapatīhi  vivadanti, mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati| idha panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū passāmi samaggai sammaudamānai avivadamānai khīraudakībhūtai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  piyacakkhūhi sampassantai viharantai| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ aivaṃ samaggaṃ parisaṃ samanupassāmi| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

369. ‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhantai, ārāmaina ārāmaṃ, uyyānaina uyyānaṃ anucaṅkamāmi anuvicarāmi| sauhaṃ tattha passāmi aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇai kisai lūkhai dubbaṇṇai uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātai dhamanisanthatagattai, na viya mañ‍ñai cakkhuṃ bandhantai janassa dassanāya| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘addhā imai āyasmantau anabhiratā vā brahmacariyaṃ caranti, atthi vā taisaṃ kiñ‍ci pāpaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ paṭicchan‍naṃ; tathā hi imai āyasmantau kisā lūkhā dubbaṇṇā uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā dhamanisanthatagattā, na viya mañ‍ñai cakkhuṃ bandhanti janassa dassanāyā’ti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kiṃ nu khau tumhai āyasmantau kisā lūkhā dubbaṇṇā uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā dhamanisanthatagattā, na viya mañ‍ñai cakkhuṃ bandhatha janassa dassanāyā’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘bandhukaraugau nau paṇḍukarauginau (ka॰), mahārājā’ti| idha panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū  passāmi  haṭṭhapahaṭṭhai udaggudaggai abhiratarūpai pīṇindriyai pīṇitindriyai (sī॰ pī॰) appaussuk‍kai pan‍nalaumai paradattavuttai migabhūtaina caitasā viharantai| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘addhā imai āyasmantau tassa bhagavatau sāsanai uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ jānanti; tathā hi imai āyasmantau haṭṭhapahaṭṭhā udaggudaggā abhiratarūpā pīṇindriyā appaussuk‍kā pan‍nalaumā paradattavuttā migabhūtaina caitasā viharantī’ti| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

370. ‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhantai, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau; pahaumi  ghātaitāyaṃ vā ghātaituṃ, jāpaitāyaṃ vā jāpaituṃ, pabbājaitāyaṃ vā pabbājaituṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aḍḍakaraṇai nisin‍nassa antarantarā kathaṃ aupātainti| sauhaṃ na labhāmi – ‘mā mai bhauntau aḍḍakaraṇai nisin‍nassa antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaitha aupātaintu (sī॰) uparisailasuttai pana ‘‘aupātaithā’’tiyaiva dissati, kathāpariyausānaṃ mai bhauntau āgamaintū’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, antarantarā kathaṃ aupātainti| idha panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū passāmi; yasmiṃ samayai bhagavā anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai bhagavatau sāvakānaṃ khipitasaddau vā hauti uk‍kāsitasaddau vā| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| tatrañ‍ñatarau bhagavatau sāvakau uk‍kāsi| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau sabrahmacārī  jaṇṇukaina ghaṭṭaisi – ‘appasaddau āyasmā hautu, māyasmā saddamakāsi; satthā nau bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaitī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! adaṇḍaina vata kira, bhau, asatthaina aivaṃ suvinītā parisā bhavissatī’ti! na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ aivaṃ suvinītaṃ parisaṃ samanupassāmi| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

371. ‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhantai, passāmi idhaikac‍cai khattiyapaṇḍitai nipuṇai kataparappavādai vālavaidhirūpai| tai bhindantā vaubhindantā (sī॰) mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausarissatī’ti| tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharaunti – ‘imaṃ mayaṃ pañhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāma| aivaṃ cai nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivamassa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāma; aivaṃ caipi nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivampissa mayaṃ vādaṃ  āraupaissāmā’ti| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausaṭau’ti| tai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamanti| tai bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā na caiva bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ  pucchanti, kutau vādaṃ āraupaissanti? añ‍ñadatthu bhagavatau sāvakā sampaj‍janti| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau  bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

372. ‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhantai, passāmi idhaikac‍cai brāhmaṇapaṇḍitai…pai॰… gahapatipaṇḍitai…pai॰… samaṇapaṇḍitai nipuṇai kataparappavādai vālavaidhirūpai| tai bhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausarissatī’ti| tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharaunti – ‘imaṃ mayaṃ pañhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāma| aivaṃ cai nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivamassa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāma; aivaṃ caipi nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivampissa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāmā’ti| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausaṭau’ti| tai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamanti| tai bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā na caiva bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti, kutau vādaṃ āraupaissanti? añ‍ñadatthu bhagavantaṃyaiva aukāsaṃ yācanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya| tai bhagavā pabbājaiti| tai tathāpabbajitā  samānā aikā vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharantā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘manaṃ vata, bhau, anassāma; manaṃ vata, bhau, panassāma’| mayañhi pubbai assamaṇāva samānā samaṇāmhāti paṭijānimhā, abrāhmaṇāva samānā brāhmaṇāmhāti paṭijānimhā, anarahantauva samānā arahantāmhāti paṭijānimhā| ‘idāni khaumha samaṇā, idāni khaumha brāhmaṇā, idāni khaumha arahantau’ti| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

373. ‘‘puna  caparāhaṃ, bhantai, imai isidattapurāṇā thapatayau mamabhattā mamayānā, ahaṃ naisaṃ jīvikāya jīvitassa (sī॰), jīvikaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), jīvitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) dātā, yasassa āhattā; atha  ca pana nau tathā mayi nipac‍cakāraṃ  karaunti yathā bhagavati| bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, sainaṃ abbhuyyātau samānau imai ca isidattapurāṇā thapatayau vīmaṃsamānau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ sambādhai āvasathai vāsaṃ upagacchiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, imai isidattapurāṇā thapatayau bahudaiva rattiṃ dhammiyā kathāya vītināmaitvā, yatau ahausi bhagavā  assausuṃ khau bhagavantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tatau sīsaṃ katvā maṃ pādatau karitvā nipaj‍jiṃsu| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! imai isidattapurāṇā thapatayau mamabhattā mamayānā, ahaṃ naisaṃ jīvikāya dātā, yasassa āhattā; atha ca pana nau tathā mayi nipac‍cakāraṃ karaunti yathā bhagavati| addhā imai āyasmantau tassa bhagavatau sāsanai uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ jānantī’ti| ayampi khau mai, bhantai, bhagavati dhammanvayau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

374. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavāpi khattiyau, ahampi khattiyau; bhagavāpi kausalau, ahampi kausalau; bhagavāpi āsītikau, ahampi āsītikau| yampi, bhantai, bhagavāpi khattiyau ahampi khattiyau, bhagavāpi kausalau ahampi kausalau, bhagavāpi āsītikau ahampi āsītikau; imināvārahāmaivāhaṃ imināpāhaṃ (ka॰), bhantai, bhagavati paramanipac‍cakāraṃ kātuṃ, mittūpahāraṃ upadaṃsaituṃ| handa, ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma; bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantassa rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aisau, bhikkhavai, rājā pasainadi kausalau dhammacaitiyāni  bhāsitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kantau| uggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, dhammacaitiyāni; pariyāpuṇātha, bhikkhavai, dhammacaitiyāni; dhāraitha, bhikkhavai, dhammacaitiyāni| atthasaṃhitāni, bhikkhavai, dhammacaitiyāni ādibrahmacariyakānī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

dhammacaitiyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kaṇṇakatthalasuttaṃ

375. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruñ‍ñāyaṃ ujuñ‍ñāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), udañ‍ñāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharati kaṇṇakatthalai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina rājā pasainadi kausalau uruñ‍ñaṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘rājā, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘aj‍ja kira, bhantai, rājā pasainadi kausalau pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau purisau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘rājā, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘aj‍ja kira bhantai, rājā pasainadi kausalau pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti| assausuṃ  khau saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī – ‘‘aj‍ja kira  rājā pasainadi kausalau pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti| atha khau saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ bhattābhihārai upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘taina hi, mahārāja, amhākampi vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘saumā ca, bhantai, bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’’’ti|

376. atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā  pasainadi kausalau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saumā ca, bhantai, bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati vandanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’’ti pucchantīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| ‘‘kiṃ pana, mahārāja, saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī añ‍ñaṃ dūtaṃ nālatthu’’nti? ‘‘assausuṃ khau, bhantai, saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī – ‘aj‍ja kira rājā pasainadi kausalau pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’ti| atha khau, bhantai, saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī maṃ bhattābhihārai upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘taina hi, mahārāja, amhākampi vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi, appābādhaṃ  appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’’’ti| ‘‘sukhiniyau hauntu tā, mahārāja, saumā ca bhaginī sakulā ca bhaginī’’ti|

377. atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – ‘natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānissati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau aivamāha  – natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānissati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti; kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘yai tai, mahārāja, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānissati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti; na mai tai vuttavādinau, abbhācikkhanti ca pana maṃ tai asatā abhūtainā’’ti|

378. atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau viṭaṭūbhaṃ saināpatiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kau nu khau, saināpati, imaṃ kathāvatthuṃ rājantaipurai abbhudāhāsī’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍jayau, mahārāja, brāhmaṇau ākāsagauttau’’ti| atha  khau rājā pasainadi kausalau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, mama vacanaina sañ‍jayaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ākāsagauttaṃ āmantaihi – ‘rājā taṃ, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau purisau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa  kausalassa paṭissutvā yaina sañ‍jayau brāhmaṇau ākāsagauttau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sañ‍jayaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ākāsagauttaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘rājā taṃ, bhantai, pasainadi kausalau āmantaitī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, bhagavatā añ‍ñadaiva kiñ‍ci sandhāya bhāsitaṃ, tañ‍ca janau añ‍ñathāpi pac‍cāgacchaiyya pac‍cāgacchaiyyāti, abhijānāmi mahārāja vācaṃ bhāsitāti (sī॰)| yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā abhijānāti vācaṃ bhāsitā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, mahārāja, abhijānāmi vācaṃ bhāsitā – ‘natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau sakidaiva sabbaṃ ñassati, sabbaṃ dakkhiti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’’ti| ‘‘haiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha; sahaiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha – ‘natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau  sakidaiva sabbaṃ ñassati, sabbaṃ dakkhiti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’’ti| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhantai, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| imaisaṃ nu khau, bhantai, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ siyā visaisau siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘cattāraumai, mahārāja, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| imaisaṃ khau, mahārāja, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ dvai vaṇṇā  aggamakkhāyanti – khattiyā ca brāhmaṇā ca – yadidaṃ abhivādanapac‍cuṭṭhānaañ‍jalikammasāmīcikammānī’’ti sāmicikammānanti (sī॰)| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ pucchāmi; samparāyikāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ pucchāmi| cattāraumai, bhantai, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| imaisaṃ nu khau, bhantai, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ siyā visaisau siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti?

379. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, mahārāja, padhāniyaṅgāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, mahārāja, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti; appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya; asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu; āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā – imāni khau, mahārāja, pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgāni| cattāraumai, mahārāja, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| tai cassu imaihi pañ‍cahi padhāniyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā; aittha pana naisaṃ assa  dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhantai, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| tai cassu imaihi pañ‍cahi padhāniyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā; aittha pana naisaṃ, bhantai, siyā visaisau siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘aittha khau naisāhaṃ, mahārāja, padhānavaimattataṃ vadāmi| saiyyathāpissu, mahārāja, dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā, dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā adantā avinītā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, yai tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā, api nu tai dantāva dantakāraṇaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ, dantāva dantabhūmiṃ sampāpuṇaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā adantā avinītā, api nu tai adantāva dantakāraṇaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ, adantāva dantabhūmiṃ sampāpuṇaiyyuṃ, saiyyathāpi tai dvai hatthidammā vā assadammā vā gaudammā vā sudantā suvinītā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, yaṃ taṃ saddhaina pattabbaṃ appābādhaina asaṭhaina amāyāvinā āraddhavīriyaina pañ‍ñavatā taṃ vata taṃ tathā sau (ka॰) assaddhau bahvābādhau saṭhau māyāvī kusītau duppañ‍ñau pāpuṇissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti|

380. ‘‘haiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha; sahaiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha| cattāraumai, bhantai, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| tai cassu imaihi pañ‍cahi padhāniyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā tai cassu sammappadhānā; aittha pana naisaṃ, bhantai, siyā visaisau siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘aittha khau aittha khau pana (sī॰) naisāhaṃ, mahārāja, na kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ vadāmi – yadidaṃ vimuttiyā vimuttiṃ| saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau sukkhaṃ sākakaṭṭhaṃ ādāya aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyya; athāparau purisau sukkhaṃ sālakaṭṭhaṃ ādāya  aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyya; athāparau purisau sukkhaṃ ambakaṭṭhaṃ ādāya aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyya; athāparau purisau sukkhaṃ udumbarakaṭṭhaṃ ādāya aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, siyā nu khau taisaṃ aggīnaṃ nānādārutau abhinibbattānaṃ kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ ac‍ciyā vā ac‍ciṃ, vaṇṇaina vā vaṇṇaṃ, ābhāya vā ābha’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, yaṃ taṃ taijaṃ vīriyā nimmathitaṃ padhānābhinibbattaṃ viriyaṃ nippharati, taṃ pacchābhinibbattaṃ (sī॰), nāhaṃ tattha kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ vadāmi – yadidaṃ vimuttiyā vimutti’’nti| ‘‘haiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha; sahaiturūpaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā āha| kiṃ  pana, bhantai, atthi daivā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, mahārāja, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, atthi daivā’’’ti? ‘‘yadi vā tai, bhantai, daivā āgantārau itthattaṃ yadi vā anāgantārau itthattaṃ’’? ‘‘yai tai, mahārāja, daivā sabyābajjhā tai daivā āgantārau itthattaṃ, yai tai daivā abyābajjhā tai daivā anāgantārau itthatta’’nti|

381. aivaṃ  vuttai, viṭṭūbhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yai tai, bhantai, daivā sabyābajjhā āgantārau itthattaṃ tai daivā, yai tai daivā abyābajjhā anāgantārau itthattaṃ tai daivai tamhā ṭhānā cāvaissanti vā pabbājaissanti vā’’ti?

atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau viṭaṭūbhau saināpati rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa puttau; ahaṃ bhagavatau puttau| ayaṃ khau kālau yaṃ puttau puttaina mantaiyyā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau viṭaṭūbhaṃ saināpatiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, saināpati, taṃ yaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi; yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, saināpati, yāvatā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa vijitaṃ yattha ca rājā pasainadi kausalau issariyādhipac‍caṃ  raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, pahauti tattha rājā pasainadi kausalau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā puñ‍ñavantaṃ vā apuñ‍ñavantaṃ vā brahmacariyavantaṃ vā abrahmacariyavantaṃ vā tamhā ṭhānā cāvaituṃ vā pabbājaituṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘yāvatā, bhau, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa vijitaṃ yattha ca rājā pasainadi  kausalau issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, pahauti tattha rājā pasainadi kausalau  samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā puñ‍ñavantaṃ vā apuñ‍ñavantaṃ vā brahmacariyavantaṃ vā abrahmacariyavantaṃ vā tamhā ṭhānā cāvaituṃ vā pabbājaituṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, saināpati, yāvatā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa avijitaṃ yattha ca rājā pasainadi kausalau na issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, tattha pahauti rājā pasainadi kausalau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā puñ‍ñavantaṃ vā apuñ‍ñavantaṃ vā brahmacariyavantaṃ vā abrahmacariyavantaṃ vā tamhā ṭhānā cāvaituṃ vā pabbājaituṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘yāvatā, bhau, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa avijitaṃ yattha ca rājā pasainadi kausalau na issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, na tattha pahauti rājā  pasainadi kausalau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā puñ‍ñavantaṃ vā apuñ‍ñavantaṃ vā brahmacariyavantaṃ vā abrahmacariyavantaṃ vā tamhā ṭhānā cāvaituṃ vā pabbājaituṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, saināpati, sutā tai daivā tāvatiṃsā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau| sutā mai daivā tāvatiṃsā| idhāpi bhautā rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina sutā daivā tāvatiṃsā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, saināpati, pahauti rājā pasainadi kausalau daivai tāvatiṃsai tamhā ṭhānā cāvaituṃ vā pabbājaituṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dassanampi, bhau, rājā pasainadi kausalau daivai tāvatiṃsai nappahauti, kutau pana tamhā ṭhānā cāvaissati vā pabbājaissati vā’’ti? ‘‘aivamaiva khau, saināpati, yai tai daivā sabyābajjhā āgantārau itthattaṃ tai daivā, yai tai daivā abyābajjhā anāgantārau itthattaṃ tai daivai dassanāyapi nappahaunti; kutau pana tamhā ṭhānā cāvaissanti vā pabbājaissanti vā’’ti?

382. atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaunāmau ayaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ānandau  nāma, mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘ānandau vata, bhau, ānandarūpau vata, bhau! haiturūpaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau āha; sahaiturūpaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau āha| kiṃ pana, bhantai, atthi brahmā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, mahārāja, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, atthi brahmā’’’ti? ‘‘yadi vā sau, bhantai, brahmā āgantā itthattaṃ, yadi vā anāgantā itthatta’’nti? ‘‘yau sau, mahārāja, brahmā sabyābajjhau sau brahmā āgantā itthattaṃ, yau sau brahmā abyābajjhau sau brahmā anāgantā itthatta’’nti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau purisau rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ  kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sañ‍jayau, mahārāja, brāhmaṇau ākāsagauttau āgatau’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau sañ‍jayaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ākāsagauttaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ kathāvatthuṃ rājantaipurai abbhudāhāsī’’ti? ‘‘viṭaṭūbhau, mahārāja, saināpatī’’ti| viṭaṭūbhau saināpati aivamāha – ‘‘sañ‍jayau, mahārāja, brāhmaṇau ākāsagauttau’’ti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau purisau rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yānakālau, mahārājā’’ti|

atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sabbañ‍ñutaṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ  apucchimhā, sabbañ‍ñutaṃ bhagavā byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā| cātuvaṇṇisuddhiṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ apucchimhā, cātuvaṇṇisuddhiṃ  bhagavā byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā| adhidaivai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ apucchimhā, adhidaivai bhagavā byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā| adhibrahmānaṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ apucchimhā, adhibrahmānaṃ bhagavā byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā| yaṃ yadaiva ca mayaṃ bhagavantaṃ apucchimhā taṃ tadaiva bhagavā byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camhā attamanā| handa, ca  dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma; bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmīti|

kaṇṇakatthalasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

rājavaggau niṭṭhitau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ghaṭikārau raṭṭhapālau, maghadaivau madhuriyaṃ|

baudhi aṅgulimālau ca, piyajātaṃ bāhitikaṃ|

dhammacaitiyasuttañ‍ca, dasamaṃ kaṇṇakatthalaṃ||


5. brāhmaṇavaggau

1. brahmāyusuttaṃ

383. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vidaihaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina brahmāyu brāhmaṇau mithilāyaṃ paṭivasati jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, vīsavassasatikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ baidānaṃ (ka॰) pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau| assausi khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vidaihaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu  khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’’ti|

384. taina  khau pana samayaina brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa uttarau nāma māṇavau antaivāsī hauti tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau uttaraṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ, tāta uttara, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vidaihaisu  cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ  hautī’ti| aihi tvaṃ, tāta uttara, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jānāhi yadi vā taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathā santaṃyaiva saddau abbhuggatau, yadi vā nau tathā; yadi vā sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau, yadi vā na tādisau| tathā mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ vaidissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ panāhaṃ, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ jānissāmi yadi vā taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathā santaṃyaiva saddau abbhuggatau, yadi vā nau tathā; yadi vā sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau, yadi vā na tādisau’’ti| ‘‘āgatāni khau, tāta uttara, amhākaṃ mantaisu dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiyaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti, saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina dhammaina samaina (ka॰) abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau| ahaṃ khau pana, tāta uttara, mantānaṃ dātā; tvaṃ mantānaṃ paṭiggahaitā’’ti|

385. ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau uttarau māṇavau brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā vidaihaisu yaina bhagavā taina cārikaṃ  pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uttarau māṇavau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi| addasā  khau uttarau māṇavau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina thapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati  vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya ca| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ uttarau māṇavau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina thapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi yathā addasa uttarau māṇavau bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni anumasi paṭimasi parimasi (sī॰ ka॰); ubhaupi nāsikasautāni nāsikāsautāni (sī॰) anumasi paṭimasi; kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi| atha khau uttarassa māṇavassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saman‍nāgatau khau samaṇau gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ anubandhaiyyaṃ, iriyāpathamassa passaiyya’’nti| atha khau uttarau māṇavau sattamāsāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhi chāyāva anapāyinī anupāyinī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)|

386. atha khau uttarau māṇavau sattan‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina vidaihaisu yaina mithilā taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina mithilā yaina brahmāyu brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau uttaraṃ māṇavaṃ brahmāyu brāhmaṇau aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, tāta uttara, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathā santaṃyaiva saddau abbhuggatau, nau añ‍ñathā? kac‍ci pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau, nau añ‍ñādisau’’ti? ‘‘tathā santaṃyaiva, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saddau abbhuggatau, nau añ‍ñathā; tādisauva tādisauva bhau (sī॰ pī॰), tādisau ca khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sau bhavaṃ gautamau, nau añ‍ñādisau| saman‍nāgatau ca saman‍nāgatau ca bhau (sabbattha) sau bhavaṃ gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi|

‘‘suppatiṭṭhitapādau khau pana bhavaṃ gautamau; idampi tassa bhautau gautamassa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘haiṭṭhā khau pana tassa bhautau gautamassa pādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni…

‘‘āyatapaṇhi  khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘dīghaṅguli khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘mudutalunahatthapādau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘jālahatthapādau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘ussaṅkhapādau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘aiṇijaṅghau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘ṭhitakau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau anaunamantau ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi jaṇṇukāni parimasati parimaj‍jati…

‘‘kausauhitavatthaguyhau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau…

‘‘sukhumacchavi khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau| sukhumattā chaviyā rajaujal‍laṃ kāyai na upalimpati…

‘‘aikaikalaumau khau pana sau bhavaṃ  gautamau; aikaikāni laumāni laumakūpaisu jātāni…

‘‘uddhaggalaumau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau; uddhaggāni laumāni jātāni nīlāni añ‍janavaṇṇāni kuṇḍalāvaṭṭāni dakkhiṇāvaṭṭakajātāni…

‘‘brahmujugattau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘sattussadau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘sīhapubbaddhakāyau  khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘citantaraṃsau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau; yāvatak‍vassa kāyau tāvatak‍vassa byāmau, yāvatak‍vassa byāmau tāvatak‍vassa kāyau…

‘‘samavaṭṭakkhandhau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘rasaggasaggī khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘sīhahanu  khau pana  sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘cattālīsadantau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘samadantau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘aviraḷadantau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘susuk‍kadāṭhau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘pahūtajivhau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘brahmassarau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau karavikabhāṇī…

‘‘abhinīlanaittau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘gaupakhumau  khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau…

‘‘uṇṇā khau panassa bhautau gautamassa bhamukantarai jātā audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā…

‘‘uṇhīsasīsau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau; idampi tassa bhautau gautamassa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhau, sau bhavaṃ gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau|

387. ‘‘gacchantau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau dakkhiṇainaiva pādaina  paṭhamaṃ pak‍kamati| sau nātidūrai pādaṃ uddharati, nāc‍cāsan‍nai pādaṃ nikkhipati; sau nātisīghaṃ gacchati, nātisaṇikaṃ gacchati; na ca adduvaina adduvaṃ saṅghaṭṭaintau gacchati, na ca gaupphakaina gaupphakaṃ saṅghaṭṭaintau gacchati| sau gacchantau na satthiṃ un‍nāmaiti, na satthiṃ aunāmaiti; na satthiṃ san‍nāmaiti, na satthiṃ vināmaiti| gacchatau khau pana tassa bhautau gautamassa adharakāyauva aḍḍhakāyauva (ka॰), āraddhakāyauva (syā॰ kaṃ॰) iñ‍jati, na ca kāyabalaina gacchati| apalaukaintau khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau sabbakāyainaiva apalaukaiti; sau na uddhaṃ ul‍laukaiti, na adhau aulaukaiti; na ca vipaikkhamānau gacchati, yugamattañ‍ca paikkhati; tatau cassa uttari anāvaṭaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ bhavati| sau antaragharaṃ pavisantau na kāyaṃ un‍nāmaiti, na kāyaṃ aunāmaiti; na kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiti, na  kāyaṃ vināmaiti| sau nātidūrai nāc‍cāsan‍nai āsanassa parivattati, na ca pāṇinā ālambitvā āsanai nisīdati, na ca āsanasmiṃ kāyaṃ pakkhipati| sau antaragharai nisin‍nau samānau na hatthakuk‍kuc‍caṃ āpaj‍jati, na pādakuk‍kuc‍caṃ āpaj‍jati; na adduvaina adduvaṃ āraupaitvā nisīdati; na ca gaupphakaina gaupphakaṃ āraupaitvā nisīdati; na ca pāṇinā hanukaṃ upadahitvā upādiyitvā (sī॰ pī॰) nisīdati| sau antaragharai nisin‍nau samānau na chambhati na kampati na vaidhati na paritassati| sau achambhī akampī avaidhī aparitassī vigatalaumahaṃsau| vivaikavattau ca sau bhavaṃ gautamau antaragharai nisin‍nau hauti| sau pattaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau  na pattaṃ un‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ aunāmaiti; na pattaṃ san‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ vināmaiti| sau pattaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nātithaukaṃ nātibahuṃ| sau na khulukhulukārakaṃ bulubulukārakaṃ (sī॰) pattaṃ dhauvati, na samparivattakaṃ pattaṃ dhauvati, na  pattaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipitvā hatthai dhauvati; hatthaisu dhautaisu pattau dhautau hauti, pattai dhautai hatthā dhautā haunti| sau pattaudakaṃ chaḍḍaiti nātidūrai nāc‍cāsan‍nai, na ca vicchaḍḍayamānau| sau audanaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau na pattaṃ un‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ aunāmaiti; na pattaṃ san‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ vināmaiti| sau audanaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nātithaukaṃ nātibahuṃ| byañ‍janaṃ khau pana bhavaṃ gautamau byañ‍janamattāya āhāraiti, na ca byañ‍janaina ālaupaṃ atināmaiti| dvattikkhattuṃ khau bhavaṃ gautamau mukhai ālaupaṃ samparivattaitvā ajjhauharati; na cassa kāci audanamiñ‍jā asambhin‍nā kāyaṃ pavisati, na cassa kāci audanamiñ‍jā mukhai avasiṭṭhā hauti; athāparaṃ ālaupaṃ upanāmaiti| rasapaṭisaṃvaidī khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau āhāraṃ āhāraiti, nau ca rasarāgapaṭisaṃvaidī|

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau (ka॰) khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – naiva davāya, na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya – ‘iti purāṇañ‍ca  vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai  bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| sau bhuttāvī pattaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau na pattaṃ un‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ aunāmaiti; na pattaṃ san‍nāmaiti, na pattaṃ vināmaiti| sau pattaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nātithaukaṃ nātibahuṃ| sau na khulukhulukārakaṃ pattaṃ dhauvati, na samparivattakaṃ pattaṃ dhauvati, na pattaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipitvā hatthai dhauvati; hatthaisu dhautaisu pattau dhautau hauti, pattai dhautai hatthā dhautā haunti| sau pattaudakaṃ chaḍḍaiti nātidūrai nāc‍cāsan‍nai, na ca vicchaḍḍayamānau| sau bhuttāvī na pattaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipati nātidūrai nāc‍cāsan‍nai, na ca anatthikau pattaina hauti, na ca ativailānurakkhī pattasmiṃ| sau bhuttāvī muhuttaṃ tuṇhī nisīdati, na ca anumaudanassa kālamatināmaiti| sau bhuttāvī anumaudati, na taṃ bhattaṃ garahati, na añ‍ñaṃ bhattaṃ paṭikaṅkhati; añ‍ñadatthu dhammiyā kathāya taṃ parisaṃ sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| sau taṃ parisaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamati| sau nātisīghaṃ gacchati, nātisaṇikaṃ gacchati, na ca muc‍citukāmau gacchati; na ca tassa bhautau gautamassa kāyai cīvaraṃ ac‍cuk‍kaṭṭhaṃ hauti na ca ac‍cauk‍kaṭṭhaṃ, na ca kāyasmiṃ al‍līnaṃ na ca kāyasmā apakaṭṭhaṃ; na ca tassa bhautau gautamassa kāyamhā vātau cīvaraṃ apavahati; na ca tassa bhautau  gautamassa kāyai rajaujal‍laṃ upalimpati| sau ārāmagatau nisīdati pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja pādai pakkhālaiti; na ca sau bhavaṃ gautamau pādamaṇḍanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau pādai pakkhālaitvā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau naiva attabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti; attahitaparahitaubhayahitasabbalaukahitamaiva  sau bhavaṃ gautamau cintaintau nisin‍nau hauti| sau ārāmagatau parisati dhammaṃ daisaiti, na taṃ parisaṃ ussādaiti, na taṃ parisaṃ apasādaiti; añ‍ñadatthu dhammiyā kathāya taṃ parisaṃ sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti|

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau khau panassa bhautau gautamassa mukhatau ghausau niccharati – vissaṭṭhau ca, viñ‍ñaiyyau ca, mañ‍ju ca, savanīyau ca, bindu ca, avisārī ca, gambhīrau ca, nin‍nādī ca| yathāparisaṃ khau pana sau bhavaṃ  gautamau saraina viñ‍ñāpaiti, na cassa bahiddhā parisāya ghausau niccharati| tai taina bhautā gautamaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamanti avalaukayamānāyaiva apalaukayamānāyaiva (sī॰ ka॰) avijahitattā avijahantābhāvaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| addasāma khau mayaṃ, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ gacchantaṃ, addasāma ṭhitaṃ, addasāma antaragharaṃ pavisantaṃ, addasāma antaragharai nisin‍naṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ, addasāma antaragharai bhuñ‍jantaṃ, addasāma bhuttāviṃ nisin‍naṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ, addasāma bhuttāviṃ anumaudantaṃ, addasāma ārāmaṃ  gacchantaṃ, addasāma ārāmagataṃ nisin‍naṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ, addasāma ārāmagataṃ parisati dhammaṃ daisaintaṃ| aidisau ca aidisau ca sau bhavaṃ gautamau, tatau ca bhiyyau’’ti|

388. aivaṃ vuttai, brahmāyu brāhmaṇau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaiti –

‘‘namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

‘‘namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

‘‘namau  tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti|

‘‘appaiva nāma mayaṃ kadāci karahaci taina bhautā gautamaina samāgacchaiyyāma? appaiva nāma siyā kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti!

389. atha khau bhagavā vidaihaisu anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina mithilā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā mithilāyaṃ viharati maghadaivambavanai| assausuṃ khau mithilaiyyakā maithilaiyyakā (sī॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau  sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vidaihaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi mithilaṃ anuppattau, mithilāyaṃ viharati maghadaivambavanai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ  pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’’ti|

atha khau mithilaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

390. assausi khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau mithilaṃ anuppattau, mithilāyaṃ viharati maghadaivambavanai’’ti| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau sambahulaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ yaina maghadaivambavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| atha khau brahmāyunau brāhmaṇassa avidūrai ambavanassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau  samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau añ‍ñataraṃ māṇavakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, māṇavaka, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘brahmāyu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘brahmāyu, bhau  gautama, brāhmaṇau jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, vīsavassasatikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau| yāvatā, bhau, brāhmaṇagahapatikā mithilāyaṃ paṭivasanti, brahmāyu taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ bhaugaihi; brahmāyu taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ mantaihi; brahmāyu  taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ āyunā caiva yasasā ca| sau bhautau gautamassa dassanakāmau’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau māṇavakau brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau māṇavakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brahmāyu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘brahmāyu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, vīsavassasatikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu  anavayau| yāvatā, bhau, brāhmaṇagahapatikā mithilāyaṃ paṭivasanti, brahmāyu taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ bhaugaihi; brahmāyu taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ mantaihi; brahmāyu taisaṃ brāhmaṇau aggamakkhāyati – yadidaṃ āyunā caiva yasasā ca| sau bhautau gautamassa dassanakāmau’’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni, māṇava, brahmāyu brāhmaṇau kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| atha khau sau māṇavakau yaina brahmāyu brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katāvakāsau khaumhi bhavatā samaṇaina gautamaina| yassadāni bhavaṃ kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

391. atha  khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sā parisā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna auramiya auramattha (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), auramatha, auramati (ka॰), atha naṃ (sī॰), auramiyāti pana tvāpac‍cayantatathasaṃvaṇṇanānurūpaṃ visaudhitapadaṃ aukāsamakāsi yathā taṃ ñātassa yasassinau| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau taṃ parisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhau! nisīdatha tumhai sakai āsanai| idhāhaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa santikai nisīdissāmī’’ti|

atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavatau  kāyai  dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi| addasā khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavatau  kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya ca| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yai mai dvattiṃsāti sutā, mahāpurisalakkhaṇā|

duvai taisaṃ na passāmi, bhautau kāyasmiṃ gautama||

‘‘kac‍ci kausauhitaṃ bhautau, vatthaguyhaṃ naruttama|

nārīsamānasavhayā, kac‍ci jivhā na dassakā nārīsahanāma savhayā, kac‍ci jivhā narassikā| (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘kac‍ci pahūtajivhausi, yathā taṃ jāniyāmasai|

nin‍nāmayaitaṃ pahūtaṃ, kaṅkhaṃ vinaya nau isai||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

katāvakāsā pucchāma, yaṃ kiñ‍ci abhipatthita’’nti||

392. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ brahmāyu brāhmaṇau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi yathā addasa brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni anumasi paṭimasi; ubhaupi nāsikasautāni  anumasi paṭimasi; kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi| atha khau bhagavā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘yai tai dvattiṃsāti sutā, mahāpurisalakkhaṇā|

sabbai tai mama kāyasmiṃ, mā tai mā vau (ka॰) kaṅkhāhu brāhmaṇa||

‘‘abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñātaṃ, bhāvaitabbañ‍ca bhāvitaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ mai, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇa||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

katāvakāsau pucchassu, yaṃ kiñ‍ci abhipatthita’’nti||

393. atha khau brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘katāvakāsau khaumhi samaṇaina gautamaina| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ – ‘diṭṭhadhammikaṃ vā atthaṃ samparāyikaṃ vā’’’ti| atha khau brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kusalau khau ahaṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ atthānaṃ| añ‍ñaipi maṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ atthaṃ pucchanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ samparāyikaṃyaiva atthaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kathaṃ khau brāhmaṇau hauti, kathaṃ bhavati vaidagū|

taivij‍jau bhau kathaṃ hauti, sautthiyau kinti vuc‍cati||

‘‘arahaṃ bhau kathaṃ hauti, kathaṃ bhavati kaivalī|

muni ca bhau kathaṃ hauti, buddhau kinti pavuc‍catī’’ti||

394. atha  khau bhagavā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ  yau vaidi, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, abhiñ‍ñā vausitau muni||

‘‘cittaṃ visuddhaṃ jānāti, muttaṃ rāgaihi sabbasau|

pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī|

pāragū sabbadhammānaṃ, buddhau tādī pavuc‍catī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, brahmāyu brāhmaṇau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavatau pādāni mukhaina ca paricumbati, pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati, nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘‘brahmāyu ahaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau; brahmāyu ahaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau’’ti| atha khau sā parisā acchariyabbhutacittajātā ahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! yatra hi nāmāyaṃ brahmāyu brāhmaṇau ñātau yasassī aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘alaṃ, brāhmaṇa, uṭṭhaha nisīda tvaṃ sakai āsanai yatau tai mayi cittaṃ pasan‍na’’nti| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau uṭṭhahitvā sakai āsanai nisīdi|

395. atha  khau bhagavā brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ, sīlakathaṃ, saggakathaṃ; kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā  añ‍ñāsi brahmāyuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaivaṃ bhautā  gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| adhivāsaitu ca mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina brahmāyussa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina vidaihaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| atha khau brahmāyu brāhmaṇau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau kālamakāsi| atha khau sambahulā  bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘brahmāyu, bhantai, brāhmaṇau kālaṅkatau| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, brahmāyu brāhmaṇau pac‍capādi dhammassānudhammaṃ, na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihaisaisi| brahmāyu, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī, anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

brahmāyusuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sailasuttaṃ

396. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi yaina āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅguttarāpānaṃ nigamau tadavasari| assausi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi āpaṇaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’’ti|

atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau’’ti| dutiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi khau, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau; adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau’’ti| tatiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi khau, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau; adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina sakau assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mittāmac‍cai ñātisālauhitai āmantaisi – ‘‘suṇantu mai bhauntau, mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā; samaṇau mai gautamau nimantitau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| yaina mai kāyavaiyyāvaṭikaṃ kāyavaiyāvaṭṭikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), kāyavaiyyāvatikaṃ (ka॰) karaiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau  kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa paṭissutvā appaikac‍cai uddhanāni khaṇanti, appaikac‍cai kaṭṭhāni phālainti, appaikac‍cai bhājanāni dhauvanti, appaikac‍cai udakamaṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāpainti, appaikac‍cai āsanāni pañ‍ñapainti| kaiṇiyau pana jaṭilau sāmaṃyaiva maṇḍalamālaṃ paṭiyādaiti|

397. taina khau pana samayaina sailau brāhmaṇau āpaṇai paṭivasati tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau, tīṇi ca māṇavakasatāni mantai vācaiti| taina khau pana samayaina kaiṇiyau jaṭilau sailai brāhmaṇai abhippasan‍nau hauti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tīhi māṇavakasataihi parivutau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sailau brāhmaṇau kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamai appaikac‍cai uddhanāni khaṇantai, appaikac‍cai kaṭṭhāni phālaintai, appaikac‍cai bhājanāni dhauvantai, appaikac‍cai udakamaṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāpaintai, appaikac‍cai āsanāni pañ‍ñapaintai, kaiṇiyaṃ pana jaṭilaṃ sāmaṃyaiva maṇḍalamālaṃ paṭiyādaintaṃ| disvāna kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu bhautau kaiṇiyassa āvāhau vā bhavissati vivāhau vā bhavissati mahāyañ‍ñau vā pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyainā’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhau saila, āvāhau  bhavissati napi vivāhau bhavissati napi rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyaina; api ca khau mai mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau| atthi, bhau, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi āpaṇaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau mai nimantitau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti|

‘‘buddhauti  – bhau kaiṇiya, vadaisi’’?

‘‘buddhauti – bhau saila, vadāmi’’|

‘‘buddhauti – bhau kaiṇiya, vadaisi’’?

‘‘buddhauti – bhau saila, vadāmī’’ti|

398. atha khau sailassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ghausaupi khau aisau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ – yadidaṃ ‘buddhau’ti yadidaṃ buddhau buddhauti (ka॰)| āgatāni khau panamhākaṃ mantaisu dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa  dvaiyaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti, saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau  imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau’’|

‘‘kahaṃ pana, bhau kaiṇiya, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaiṇiyau jaṭilau dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ paggahaitvā sailaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yainaisā, bhau saila, nīlavanarājī’’ti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tīhi māṇavakasataihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tai māṇavakai āmantaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau āgacchantu padai padaṃ pādai pādaṃ (sī॰) nikkhipantā; durāsadā dūrasaddā (ka॰) hi tai bhagavantau sīhāva aikacarā| yadā cāhaṃ, bhau, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ mantaiyyaṃ, mā mai bhauntau antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaitha| kathāpariyausānaṃ mai bhavantau āgamaintū’’ti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi|

addasā khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai  ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya ca| atha  khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ sailau brāhmaṇau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai  ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi, yathā addasa sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni anumasi paṭimasi; ubhaupi nāsikasautāni anumasi paṭimasi; kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi| atha khau sailassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saman‍nāgatau khau samaṇau gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi paripuṇṇaihi, nau aparipuṇṇaihi; nau ca khau naṃ jānāmi buddhau vā nau vā| sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yai tai bhavanti arahantau sammāsambuddhā tai sakai vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attānaṃ pātukarauntī’ti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

399. atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘paripuṇṇakāyau suruci, sujātau cārudassanau|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇausi bhagavā, susuk‍kadāṭhausi vīriyavā viriyavā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘narassa hi sujātassa, yai bhavanti viyañ‍janā|

sabbai tai tava kāyasmiṃ, mahāpurisalakkhaṇā||

‘‘pasan‍nanaittau  sumukhau, brahā brahmā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) uju patāpavā|

majjhai samaṇasaṅghassa, ādic‍cauva viraucasi||

‘‘kalyāṇadassanau bhikkhu, kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau|

kiṃ tai samaṇabhāvaina, aivaṃ uttamavaṇṇinau||

‘‘rājā arahasi bhavituṃ, cak‍kavattī rathaisabhau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambusaṇḍassa jambumaṇḍassa (ka॰) issarau||

‘‘khattiyā  bhaugirājānau, anuyantā anuyuttā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavantu tai|

rājābhirājā manujindau, raj‍jaṃ kāraihi gautama’’||

‘‘rājāhamasmi  sailāti, dhammarājā anuttarau|

dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi, cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ’’||

‘‘sambuddhau paṭijānāsi, dhammarājā anuttarau|

‘dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi’, iti bhāsasi gautama||

‘‘kau nu saināpati bhautau, sāvakau satthuranvayau|

kau tai tamanuvattaiti, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ’’||

‘‘mayā pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā dhammacak‍kaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāriputtau anuvattaiti, anujātau tathāgataṃ||

‘‘abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñātaṃ, bhāvaitabbañ‍ca bhāvitaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ mai, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇa||

‘‘vinayassu mayi kaṅkhaṃ, adhimuc‍cassu brāhmaṇa|

dul‍labhaṃ dassanaṃ hauti, sambuddhānaṃ abhiṇhasau||

‘‘yaisaṃ  vai dul‍labhau laukai, pātubhāvau abhiṇhasau|

sauhaṃ brāhmaṇa sambuddhau, sal‍lakattau anuttarau||

‘‘brahmabhūtau atitulau, mārasainappamaddanau|

sabbāmittai vasī katvā, maudāmi akutaubhayau’’||

‘‘imaṃ bhauntau nisāmaitha, yathā bhāsati cakkhumā|

sal‍lakattau mahāvīrau, sīhauva nadatī vanai||

‘‘brahmabhūtaṃ  atitulaṃ, mārasainappamaddanaṃ|

kau disvā nappasīdaiyya, api kaṇhābhijātikau||

‘‘yau maṃ icchati anvaitu, yau vā nicchati gacchatu|

idhāhaṃ pabbajissāmi, varapañ‍ñassa santikai’’||

‘‘aitañ‍cai aivañ‍cai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ruc‍cati bhautau, sammāsambuddhasāsanaṃ sammāsambuddhasāsanai (katthaci suttanipātai)|

mayampi pabbajissāma, varapañ‍ñassa santikai’’||

‘‘brāhmaṇā tisatā imai, yācanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

brahmacariyaṃ carissāma, bhagavā tava santikai’’||

‘‘svākkhātaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ|

yattha amaughā pabbaj‍jā, appamattassa sikkhatau’’ti||

alattha khau sailau brāhmaṇau saparisau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ|

400. atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai assamai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ  āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi, sampavāraisi| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘aggihuttamukhā yañ‍ñā, sāvittī chandasau mukhaṃ|

rājā mukhaṃ manussānaṃ, nadīnaṃ sāgarau mukhaṃ||

‘‘nakkhattānaṃ  mukhaṃ candau, ādic‍cau tapataṃ mukhaṃ|

puñ‍ñaṃ ākaṅkhamānānaṃ, saṅghau vai yajataṃ mukha’’nti||

atha khau bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau āyasmā sailau saparisau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā sailau  saparisau arahataṃ ahausi| atha khau āyasmā sailau saparisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yaṃ  taṃ saraṇamāgamma, itau aṭṭhami cakkhumā|

sattarattaina anuttaraina (ka॰) bhagavā, dantamha tava sāsanai||

‘‘tuvaṃ buddhau tuvaṃ satthā, tuvaṃ mārābhibhū muni|

tuvaṃ anusayai chaitvā, tiṇṇau tāraisimaṃ pajaṃ||

‘‘upadhī tai samatik‍kantā, āsavā tai padālitā|

sīhauva anupādānau, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

‘‘bhikkhavau tisatā imai, tiṭṭhanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

pādai vīra pasāraihi, nāgā vandantu satthunau’’ti||

sailasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. assalāyanasuttaṃ

401. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina nānāvairaj‍jakānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ pañ‍camattāni brāhmaṇasatāni sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau taisaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau cātuvaṇṇiṃ suddhiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| kau nu khau pahauti samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti? taina khau pana samayaina assalāyanau nāma māṇavau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati daharau, vuttasirau, sauḷasavassuddaisikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau| atha khau taisaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau assalāyanau māṇavau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati daharau, vuttasirau, sauḷasavassuddaisikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū…pai॰… anavayau| sau khau pahauti samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti|

atha khau tai brāhmaṇā yaina assalāyanau māṇavau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā assalāyanaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhau assalāyana, samaṇau gautamau cātuvaṇṇiṃ suddhiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| aitu bhavaṃ assalāyanau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitū’’ti paṭimantaitunti (pī॰ ka॰)|

aivaṃ vuttai, assalāyanau māṇavau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca  – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dhammavādī; dhammavādinau ca pana duppaṭimantiyā bhavanti| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti| dutiyampi khau tai brāhmaṇā assalāyanaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhau assalāyana, samaṇau gautamau cātuvaṇṇiṃ suddhiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| aitu bhavaṃ assalāyanau samaṇaina gautamaina  saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu paṭimantaituṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| caritaṃ khau pana bhautā assalāyanaina paribbājaka’’nti| dutiyampi khau assalāyanau māṇavau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dhammavādī; dhammavādinau ca pana duppaṭimantiyā bhavanti| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti| tatiyampi khau tai brāhmaṇā assalāyanaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhau assalāyana, samaṇau gautamau cātuvaṇṇiṃ suddhiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| aitu bhavaṃ assalāyanau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu paṭimantaituṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| caritaṃ khau pana bhautā assalāyanaina paribbājakaṃ| mā bhavaṃ assalāyanau ayuddhaparājitaṃ parājayī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, assalāyanau māṇavau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘‘addhā khau ahaṃ bhavantau na labhāmi| samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dhammavādī; dhammavādinau ca pana duppaṭimantiyā bhavanti| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitunti| api cāhaṃ bhavantānaṃ vacanaina gamissāmī’’ti|

402. atha khau assalāyanau māṇavau mahatā brāhmaṇagaṇaina saddhiṃ  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau assalāyanau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhau gautama, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇauva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā  aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’ti| idha bhavaṃ gautamau kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘dissanti dissantai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khau pana, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇiyau utuniyaupi gabbhiniyaupi vijāyamānāpi pāyamānāpi| tai ca brāhmaṇiyaunijāva samānā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’’’ti| ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi  bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

403. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, sutaṃ tai – ‘yaunakambaujaisu añ‍ñaisu ca pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu dvaiva vaṇṇā – ayyau caiva dāsau ca; ayyau hutvā dāsau hauti, dāsau  hutvā ayyau hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau, sutaṃ taṃ mai – ‘yaunakambaujaisu añ‍ñaisu ca pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu dvaiva vaṇṇā – ayyau caiva dāsau ca; ayyau hutvā dāsau hauti, dāsau hutvā ayyau hautī’’’ti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|
