404. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, khattiyauva nu khau pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhi kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, nau brāhmaṇau? vaissauva nu khau…pai॰… suddauva nu khau pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhi kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, nau brāhmaṇau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama| khattiyaupi hi, bhau  gautama, pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhi  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… suddaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pāṇātipātinau adin‍nādāyinau  kāmaisumicchācārinau musāvādinau pisuṇavācā pharusavācā samphappalāpinau abhijjhālū byāpan‍nacittā micchādiṭṭhī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyu’’nti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

405. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, brāhmaṇauva nu khau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā  paṭiviratau pisuṇāya vācāya  paṭiviratau pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau samphappalāpā paṭiviratau anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhi kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, nau nau ca (ka॰) khattiyau nau vaissau, nau suddau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama! khattiyaupi hi, bhau gautama, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā paṭiviratau pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau samphappalāpā paṭiviratau anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhi kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… suddaupi hi, bhau gautama…pai॰… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭiviratā pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratā pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā samphappalāpā paṭiviratā anabhijjhālū abyāpan‍nacittā sammādiṭṭhī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyu’’nti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā  aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

406. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, brāhmaṇauva nu khau pahauti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaituṃ, nau khattiyau, nau vaissau nau suddau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama! khattiyaupi hi, bhau gautama, pahauti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaituṃ; brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama… suddaupi hi, bhau gautama… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pahaunti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaitu’’nti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

407. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, brāhmaṇauva nu khau pahauti sauttisināniṃ ādāya  nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaituṃ, nau khattiyau, nau vaissau, nau suddau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama! khattiyaupi hi, bhau gautama, pahauti sauttisināniṃ ādāya nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaituṃ, brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama… suddaupi hi, bhau gautama… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pahaunti sauttisināniṃ ādāya nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaitu’’nti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰…  brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

408. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, idha rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau  nānājac‍cānaṃ purisānaṃ purisasataṃ san‍nipātaiyya – ‘āyantu bhauntau yai  tattha khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍nā, sākassa vā sālassa vā uppan‍nā sālassa vā (sī॰ pī॰) salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya, aggiṃ abhinibbattaintu, taijau pātukarauntu| āyantu pana bhauntau yai tattha caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā vaiṇakulā (sī॰ pī॰), vaiṇukulā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍nā, sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya, aggiṃ abhinibbattaintu, taijau pātukarauntū’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, yau aivaṃ nu khau sau yau ca nu khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍naihi sākassa vā sālassa vā salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau, taijau pātukatau, sau aiva nu khvāssa aggi ac‍cimā caiva ca (sī॰ pī॰) vaṇṇavā vaṇṇimā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca pabhassarau ca, taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātuṃ; yau pana sau caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍naihi sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau, taijau pātukatau svāssa aggi na caiva ac‍cimā na ca vaṇṇavā na ca pabhassarau, na ca taina sak‍kā agginā  aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama! yaupi hi sau yau sau (sī॰ pī॰), bhau gautama, khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍naihi sākassa vā sālassa vā salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau, taijau pātukatau svāssa sau cassa (sī॰ pī॰), saupissa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca, taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātuṃ; yaupi sau caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍naihi sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi  abhinibbattau, taijau pātukatau, svāssa aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca, taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātuṃ| sabbaupi hi, bhau gautama, aggi ac‍cimā caiva  vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca, sabbainapi sak‍kā  agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātu’’nti| ‘‘aittha, assalāyana, brāhmaṇānaṃ kiṃ balaṃ, kau assāsau yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’’’ti? ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha, atha khvaittha brāhmaṇā aivamaitaṃ mañ‍ñanti – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’’’ti|

409. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, idha khattiyakumārau brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāya saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaiyya, taisaṃ saṃvāsamanvāya puttau jāyaitha; yau sau khattiyakumāraina brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, siyā sau mātupi sadisau pitupi sadisau, ‘khattiyau’tipi vattabbau ‘brāhmaṇau’tipi vattabbau’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhau gautama, khattiyakumāraina brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, siyā sau mātupi sadisau pitupi sadisau, ‘khattiyau’tipi vattabbau ‘brāhmaṇau’tipi vattabbau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, idha brāhmaṇakumārau khattiyakañ‍ñāya saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaiyya, taisaṃ saṃvāsamanvāya puttau jāyaitha; yau sau brāhmaṇakumāraina khattiyakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, siyā sau mātupi sadisau pitupi sadisau, ‘khattiyau’tipi vattabbau ‘brāhmaṇau’tipi vattabbau’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇakumāraina khattiyakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, siyā sau mātupi sadisau pitupi sadisau, ‘khattiyau’tipi vattabbau ‘brāhmaṇau’tipi vattabbau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana idha vaḷavaṃ gadrabhaina sampayaujaiyyuṃ saṃyaujaiyya (ka॰), taisaṃ sampayaugamanvāya kisaurau jāyaitha; yau sau vaḷavāya gadrabhaina kisaurau uppan‍nau, siyā sau mātupi sadisau pitupi sadisau, ‘assau’tipi vattabbau ‘gadrabhau’tipi vattabbau’’ti? ‘‘kuṇḍañhi  sau vaikurañ‍jāya hi sau (sī॰ pī॰), sau kumāraṇḍupi sau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vaikulajau hi sau (?), bhau gautama, assatarau hauti| idaṃ hissa, bhau gautama, nānākaraṇaṃ  passāmi; amutra ca panaisānaṃ na kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ passāmī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, idhāssu dvai māṇavakā bhātarau saudariyā, aikau ajjhāyakau upanītau aikau anajjhāyakau anupanītau| kamaittha brāhmaṇā paṭhamaṃ bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhau gautama, māṇavakau ajjhāyakau upanītau tamaittha brāhmaṇā paṭhamaṃ bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā| kiñhi, bhau gautama, anajjhāyakai anupanītai din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assalāyana, idhāssu dvai māṇavakā bhātarau saudariyā, aikau ajjhāyakau upanītau dussīlau pāpadhammau, aikau anajjhāyakau anupanītau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau| kamaittha brāhmaṇā paṭhamaṃ bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhau gautama, māṇavakau anajjhāyakau anupanītau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau tamaittha brāhmaṇā paṭhamaṃ bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā| kiñhi, bhau gautama, dussīlai pāpadhammai din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, assalāyana, jātiṃ agamāsi; jātiṃ gantvā mantai agamāsi; mantai gantvā  tapai agamāsi; tapai gantvā mantai gantvā tamaitaṃ tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), mantai gantvā tamaiva ṭhapaitvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) cātuvaṇṇiṃ suddhiṃ pac‍cāgatau, yamahaṃ pañ‍ñapaimī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, assalāyanau māṇavau tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi|

410. atha khau bhagavā assalāyanaṃ māṇavaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā assalāyanaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, assalāyana, sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu sammantānaṃ vasantānaṃ (sī॰) aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti  – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau  vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’ti| assausi khau, assalāyana, asitau daivalau isi – ‘sattan‍naṃ kira brāhmaṇisīnaṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu sammantānaṃ aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau…pai॰… brahmadāyādā’ti| atha khau, assalāyana, asitau daivalau isi kaisamassuṃ kappaitvā mañ‍jiṭṭhavaṇṇāni dussāni nivāsaitvā paṭaliyau aṭaliyau (sī॰ pī॰), agaliyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upāhanā āruhitvā jātarūpamayaṃ daṇḍaṃ gahaitvā sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ patthaṇḍilai pāturahausi| atha khau, assalāyana, asitau daivalau isi sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ patthaṇḍilai caṅkamamānau aivamāha – ‘handa, kau nu khau imai bhavantau brāhmaṇisayau gatā gantā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰); handa, kau nu khau imai bhavantau brāhmaṇisayau gatā’ti? atha khau, assalāyana, sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘kau  nāyaṃ gāmaṇḍalarūpau viya sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ patthaṇḍilai caṅkamamānau aivamāha – ‘handa, kau nu khau imai bhavantau brāhmaṇisayau gatā; handa, kau nu khau imai bhavantau brāhmaṇisayau gatāti? handa, naṃ abhisapāmā’ti| atha khau, assalāyana, satta brāhmaṇisayau asitaṃ daivalaṃ isiṃ abhisapiṃsu – ‘bhasmā, vasala vasalī (pī॰), vasali (ka॰), capalī (syā॰ kaṃ॰), hauhi; bhasmā, vasala, hauhī’ti bhasmā vasala hauhīti abhisapavacanaṃ sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu sakidaiva āgataṃ| yathā yathā khau, assalāyana, satta brāhmaṇisayau asitaṃ daivalaṃ isiṃ abhisapiṃsu tathā tathā asitau daivalau isi abhirūpatarau caiva hauti dassanīyatarau ca pāsādikatarau ca| atha khau, assalāyana, sattan‍naṃ brāhmaṇisīnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘maughaṃ vata nau tapau, aphalaṃ brahmacariyaṃ| mayañhi pubbai yaṃ abhisapāma – bhasmā, vasala, hauhi; bhasmā, vasala, hauhīti bhasmāva bhavati aikac‍cau| imaṃ pana mayaṃ yathā yathā abhisapāma tathā tathā abhirūpatarau caiva hauti dassanīyatarau ca pāsādikatarau cā’ti| ‘na bhavantānaṃ maughaṃ tapau, nāphalaṃ brahmacariyaṃ| iṅgha bhavantau, yau mayi manaupadausau taṃ pajahathā’ti| ‘yau  bhavati manaupadausau taṃ pajahāma| kau nu bhavaṃ hautī’ti? ‘sutau nu bhavataṃ – asitau daivalau isī’ti? ‘aivaṃ, bhau’| ‘sau khvāhaṃ, bhau, haumī’ti| atha khau, assalāyana, satta brāhmaṇisayau asitaṃ daivalaṃ isiṃ abhivādaituṃ upak‍kamiṃsu|

411. ‘‘atha  khau, assalāyana, asitau daivalau isi satta brāhmaṇisayau aitadavauca – ‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau, sattan‍naṃ kira brāhmaṇisīnaṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu sammantānaṃ aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ  – brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhau’|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – yā janikā mātā janimātā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇaṃyaiva agamāsi, nau abrāhmaṇa’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, bhau’|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – yā janikāmātu janimātu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mātā yāva sattamā mātumātāmahayugā brāhmaṇaṃyaiva agamāsi, nau abrāhmaṇa’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, bhau’|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – yau janakau pitā janipitā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇiṃyaiva agamāsi, nau abrāhmaṇi’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, bhau’|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – yau janakapitu janipitu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pitā yāva sattamā pitupitāmahayugā brāhmaṇiṃyaiva agamāsi, nau abrāhmaṇi’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, bhau’|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – yathā gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautī’ti na mayaṃ jānāma bhau yathā gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautīti| yathā kathaṃ pana bhau gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautīti| (ka॰)? ‘jānāma mayaṃ, bhau – yathā gabbhassa avak‍kanti hauti na mayaṃ jānāma bhau yathā gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautīti| yathā kathaṃ pana bhau gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautīti| (ka॰)| idha  mātāpitarau ca san‍nipatitā haunti, mātā ca utunī hauti, gandhabbau ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti; aivaṃ tiṇṇaṃ san‍nipātā gabbhassa avak‍kanti hautī’ti|

‘‘‘jānanti pana bhauntau – taggha yagghai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sau gandhabbau khattiyau vā brāhmaṇau vā vaissau vā suddau vā’ti? ‘na mayaṃ, bhau, jānāma – taggha sau gandhabbau khattiyau vā brāhmaṇau vā vaissau vā suddau vā’ti| ‘aivaṃ santai, bhau, jānātha – kai tumhai hauthā’ti? ‘aivaṃ santai, bhau, na mayaṃ jānāma  – kai mayaṃ haumā’ti| tai hi nāma, assalāyana, satta brāhmaṇisayau asitaina daivalaina isinā sakai jātivādai samanuyuñ‍jīyamānā samanuggāhīyamānā samanubhāsīyamānā na sampāyissanti; kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi mayā sakasmiṃ jātivādai samanuyuñ‍jīyamānau  samanuggāhīyamānau samanubhāsīyamānau sampāyissasi, yaisaṃ tvaṃ sācariyakau na puṇṇau dabbigāhau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, assalāyanau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

assalāyanasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. ghauṭamukhasuttaṃ

412. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā udainau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati khaimiyambavanai| taina khau pana samayaina ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau bārāṇasiṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau jaṅghāvihāraṃ  anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina khaimiyambavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udainau abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| atha khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau yaināyasmā udainau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā udainaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā āyasmantaṃ udainaṃ caṅkamantaṃ anucaṅkamamānau aivamāha – ‘‘ambhau samaṇa, ‘natthi dhammikau paribbajau’ paribbājau (sī॰ pī॰) – aivaṃ mai aittha hauti| tañ‍ca khau bhavantarūpānaṃ vā adassanā, yau vā panaittha dhammau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā udainau caṅkamā aurauhitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| ghauṭamukhaupi khau brāhmaṇau caṅkamā aurauhitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau ghauṭamukhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ āyasmā udainau aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃvij‍janti saṃvij‍jantai (bahūsu) khau, brāhmaṇa, āsanāni| sacai ākaṅkhasi, nisīdā’’ti| ‘‘aitadaiva khau pana mayaṃ  bhautau udainassa āgamayamānā (na) nisīdāma| kathañhi nāma mādisau pubbai animantitau āsanai  nisīditabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti? atha khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ udainaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ambhau samaṇa, ‘natthi dhammikau paribbajau’ – aivaṃ mai aittha hauti| tañ‍ca khau bhavantarūpānaṃ vā adassanā, yau vā panaittha dhammau’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau pana mai tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anuñ‍ñaiyyaṃ anujānaiyyāsi, paṭik‍kausitabbañ‍ca paṭik‍kausaiyyāsi; yassa ca pana mai bhāsitassa atthaṃ na jānaiyyāsi, mamaṃyaiva tattha uttari paṭipucchaiyyāsi – ‘idaṃ, bhau udaina, kathaṃ, imassa k‍vatthau’ti? aivaṃ katvā siyā nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘anuñ‍ñaiyyaṃ khvāhaṃ bhautau udainassa anujānissāmi, paṭik‍kausitabbañ‍ca paṭik‍kausissāmi; yassa ca panāhaṃ bhautau  udainassa  bhāsitassa atthaṃ na jānissāmi, bhavantaṃyaiva tattha udainaṃ uttari paṭipucchissāmi – ‘idaṃ, bhau udaina, kathaṃ, imassa k‍vatthau’ti? aivaṃ katvā hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

413. ‘‘cattāraumai, brāhmaṇa, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| imaisaṃ, brāhmaṇa, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti?

‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau ayaṃ mai puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti; yaupāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau ayampi mai puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti; yaupāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau  parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau ayampi mai puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti; yau ca khau ayaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| ayamaiva mai puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti|

‘‘kasmā pana tai, brāhmaṇa, imai tayau puggalā cittaṃ nārādhaintī’’ti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau attānaṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti; iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau paraṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ  ātāpaiti paritāpaiti; iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yaupāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau  attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti; iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ nārādhaiti| yau ca khau ayaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati, sau attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭik‍kūlaṃ naiva ātāpaiti na paritāpaiti; iminā mai ayaṃ puggalau cittaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti|

414. ‘‘dvaimā, brāhmaṇa, parisā| katamā dvai? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cā parisā sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttabhariyaṃ pariyaisati, dāsidāsaṃ pariyaisati, khaittavatthuṃ pariyaisati, jātarūparajataṃ pariyaisati|

‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cā parisā asārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttabhariyaṃ pahāya, dāsidāsaṃ pahāya, khaittavatthuṃ pahāya, jātarūparajataṃ pahāya, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| svāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau  nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī  brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| idha katamaṃ tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, puggalaṃ katamāya parisāya bahulaṃ samanupassasi – yā cāyaṃ parisā sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttabhariyaṃ pariyaisati dāsidāsaṃ pariyaisati khaittavatthuṃ pariyaisati jātarūparajataṃ pariyaisati, yā cāyaṃ parisā asārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttabhariyaṃ pahāya dāsidāsaṃ pahāya khaittavatthuṃ pahāya jātarūparajataṃ pahāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā’’ti?

‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhau udaina, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati; imāhaṃ puggalaṃ yāyaṃ parisā asārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttabhariyaṃ pahāya dāsidāsaṃ pahāya khaittavatthuṃ pahāya jātarūparajataṃ pahāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā imissaṃ parisāyaṃ bahulaṃ samanupassāmī’’ti|

‘‘idānaiva  khau pana tai, brāhmaṇa, bhāsitaṃ – ‘mayaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ambhau samaṇa, natthi dhammikau paribbajau, aivaṃ mai aittha hauti| tañ‍ca khau bhavantarūpānaṃ vā adassanā, yau vā panaittha dhammau’’’ti| ‘‘addhā maisā, bhau udaina, sānuggahā vācā bhāsitā| ‘atthi dhammikau paribbajau’ – aivaṃ mai aittha hauti| aivañ‍ca pana maṃ bhavaṃ udainau dhāraitu| yai ca mai bhautā udainaina cattārau puggalā saṃkhittaina vuttā vitthāraina avibhattā, sādhu mai bhavaṃ, udainau imai cattārau puggalai vitthāraina  vibhajatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau āyasmatau udainassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā udainau aitadavauca –

415. ‘‘katamau ca, brāhmaṇa, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau naaihibhaddantikau natiṭṭhabhaddantikau, nābhihaṭaṃ na uddissakataṃ na nimantanaṃ sādiyati| sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ, na daṇḍamantaraṃ, na musalamantaraṃ, na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, na gabbhiniyā, na pāyamānāya, na  purisantaragatāya, na saṅkittīsu, na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti, na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī, na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ, na suraṃ na mairayaṃ na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau; aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti…pai॰… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti…pai॰… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti – iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikaṃ pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā hauti, vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti; kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti; sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati – iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, puggalau attantapau attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

416. ‘‘katamau  ca, brāhmaṇa, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau aurabbhikau hauti sūkarikau sākuṇikau māgavikau luddau macchaghātakau caurau cauraghātakau gaughātakau bandhanāgārikau – yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kurūrakammantā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, puggalau parantapau paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

417. ‘‘katamau  ca, brāhmaṇa, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau rājā vā hauti khattiyau muddhāvasittau, brāhmaṇau vā mahāsālau| sau puratthimaina nagarassa navaṃ santhāgāraṃ kārāpaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kharājinaṃ nivāsaitvā sappitailaina kāyaṃ abbhañ‍jitvā magavisāṇaina piṭṭhiṃ kaṇḍuvamānau navaṃ santhāgāraṃ pavisati saddhiṃ mahaisiyā brāhmaṇaina ca purauhitaina| sau tattha anantarahitāya bhūmiyā haritupalittāya saiyyaṃ kappaiti| aikissāya gāviyā sarūpavacchāya yaṃ aikasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina rājā yāpaiti, yaṃ dutiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina mahaisī yāpaiti, yaṃ tatiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina brāhmaṇau purauhitau yāpaiti, yaṃ catutthasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina aggiṃ juhati, avasaisaina vacchakau yāpaiti| sau aivamāha – ‘aittakā usabhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatarā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatariyau hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā ajā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya’, aittakā urabbhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā assā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā rukkhā chij‍jantu yūpatthāya, aittakā dabbhā lūyantu barihisatthāyā’ti| yaipissa tai haunti ‘dāsā’ti vā ‘paissā’ti vā ‘kammakarā’ti vā taipi daṇḍataj‍jitā bhayataj‍jitā assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni karaunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, puggalau attantapau ca attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

418. ‘‘katamau ca, brāhmaṇa, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau; sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau  sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati? idha, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ  pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajaupathau abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya  mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī| athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā|

‘‘musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusaṃ  vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ  pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

‘‘sau bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti| aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti| jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti| tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti| uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti, aivamaiva bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

419. ‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ  asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ  manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau,) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.296 iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ  kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

420. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau; sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau; sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi  cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, puggalau naivattantapau nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, na parantapau na  paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharatī’’ti|

421. aivaṃ vuttai, ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ udainaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau udaina, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau udaina! saiyyathāpi, bhau udaina, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaivaṃ bhautā udainaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ udainaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ udainau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| ‘‘mā khau maṃ tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, saraṇaṃ agamāsi| tamaiva bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāhi yamahaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana, bhau udaina, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti? ‘‘parinibbutau khau, brāhmaṇa, aitarahi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti|

‘‘sacaipi sacai hi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mayaṃ, bhau udaina, suṇaiyyāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dasasu yaujanaisu, dasapi mayaṃ yaujanāni gacchaiyyāma  taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya  arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ| sacaipi sacai (sī॰ pī॰), sacai hi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) mayaṃ, bhau udaina, suṇaiyyāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ vīsatiyā yaujanaisu… tiṃsāya yaujanaisu… cattārīsāya yaujanaisu… pañ‍ñāsāya yaujanaisu, pañ‍ñāsampi mayaṃ yaujanāni gacchaiyyāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ| yaujanasatai caipi yaujanasataipi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mayaṃ, bhau udaina, suṇaiyyāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ, yaujanasatampi mayaṃ gacchaiyyāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhau udaina, parinibbutau sau bhavaṃ gautamau, parinibbutampi mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ udainau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| atthi ca mai, bhau udaina, aṅgarājā daivasikaṃ nic‍cabhikkhaṃ dadāti, tatau ahaṃ bhautau udainassa aikaṃ nic‍cabhikkhaṃ dadāmī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tai, brāhmaṇa, aṅgarājā daivasikaṃ nic‍cabhikkhaṃ dadātī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍ca, bhau udaina, kahāpaṇasatānī’’ti| ‘‘na khau nau, brāhmaṇa, kappati jātarūparajataṃ paṭiggahaitu’’nti| ‘‘sacai taṃ bhautau udainassa na kappati vihāraṃ bhautau udainassa kārāpaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau mai tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vihāraṃ, kārāpaitukāmau, pāṭaliputtai saṅghassa upaṭṭhānasālaṃ kārāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ bhautau udainassa bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau yaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ udainau saṅghai dānai samādapaiti| aisāhaṃ, bhau udaina, aitissā ca nic‍cabhikkhāya aparāya ca nic‍cabhikkhāya pāṭaliputtai saṅghassa upaṭṭhānasālaṃ kārāpaissāmī’’ti| atha  khau ghauṭamukhau brāhmaṇau aitissā ca nic‍cabhikkhāya aparāya ca nic‍cabhikkhāya pāṭaliputtai saṅghassa upaṭṭhānasālaṃ kārāpaisi| sā aitarahi ‘ghauṭamukhī’ti vuc‍catīti|

ghauṭamukhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. caṅkīsuttaṃ

422. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina aupāsādaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ  brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā aupāsādai viharati uttaraina aupāsādaṃ daivavanai sālavanai| taina khau pana samayaina caṅkī brāhmaṇau aupāsādaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| assausuṃ khau aupāsādakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aupāsādaṃ anuppattau, aupāsādai viharati uttaraina aupāsādaṃ daivavanai sālavanai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ  sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

423. atha khau aupāsādakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā aupāsādā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtā uttarainamukhā gacchanti yaina daivavanaṃ sālavanaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina caṅkī brāhmaṇau uparipāsādai divāsaiyyaṃ upagatau| addasā khau caṅkī brāhmaṇau aupāsādakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aupāsādā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtai uttaraina mukhaṃ yaina daivavanaṃ sālavanaṃ tainupasaṅkamantai| disvā khattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau khattai, aupāsādakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā aupāsādā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtā uttarainamukhā gacchanti yaina daivavanaṃ sālavana’’nti? ‘‘atthi, bhau caṅkī, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aupāsādaṃ anuppattau, aupāsādai  viharati uttaraina aupāsādaṃ daivavanai sālavanai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| tamaitai bhavantaṃ  gautamaṃ dassanāya gacchantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhau khattai, yaina aupāsādakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā aupāsādakai  brāhmaṇagahapatikai aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘caṅkī, bhau, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – āgamaintu kira bhauntau, caṅkīpi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau khattau caṅkissa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā  yaina aupāsādakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aupāsādakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aitadavauca – ‘‘caṅkī, bhau, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘āgamaintu kira bhauntau, caṅkīpi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti|

424. taina khau pana samayaina nānāvairaj‍jakānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ pañ‍camattāni brāhmaṇasatāni aupāsādai paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau tai brāhmaṇā – ‘‘caṅkī kira brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti| atha khau tai brāhmaṇā yaina caṅkī brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā caṅkiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira bhavaṃ caṅkī samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, bhau, hauti – ‘ahaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmī’’’ti| ‘‘mā bhavaṃ caṅkī samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkami| na arahati bhavaṃ caṅkī samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ caṅkiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| bhavañhi caṅkī ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi bhavaṃ caṅkī ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau  jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ caṅkī samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ caṅkiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| bhavañhi caṅkī aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau  brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī brahmavac‍casī (sī॰ pī॰) akhuddāvakāsau  dassanāya…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī sīlavā vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau  pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau, tīṇi māṇavakasatāni mantai vācaiti…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰… bhavañhi caṅkī aupāsādaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| yampi bhavaṃ caṅkī aupāsādaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ  sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ caṅkī samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ caṅkiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

425. aivaṃ vuttai, caṅkī brāhmaṇau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘‘taina hi, bhau, mamapi suṇātha, yathā mayamaiva arahāma taṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; natvaiva arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi, bhau, samaṇau gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ aittha dī॰ ni॰ 1.304 añ‍ñampi guṇapadaṃ dissati| samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau pahūtaṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ auhāya pabbajitau bhūmigatañ‍ca vaihāsaṭṭhañ‍ca…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau daharauva samānau yuvā susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau akāmakānaṃ  mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya  saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī  brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sīlavā ariyasīlī kusalasīlī kusalaina sīlaina  saman‍nāgatau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau khīṇakāmarāgau vigatacāpal‍lau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau kammavādī kiriyavādī apāpapuraikkhārau brahmañ‍ñāya pajāya…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau uc‍cā kulā pabbajitau asambhin‍nā khattiyakulā…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aḍḍhā kulā pabbajitau mahaddhanā mahābhaugā…pai॰… samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ tirauraṭṭhā tiraujanapadā saṃpucchituṃ āgacchanti…pai॰… samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ anaikāni daivatāsahassāni pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatāni…pai॰… samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau…pai॰… aitthāpi dī॰ ni॰ 1.304 añ‍ñānipi guṇapadānaṃ dissanti samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau saputtadārau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰… samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau saputtadārau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰… samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti saputtadārau  pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰… samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aupāsādaṃ anuppattau aupāsādai viharati uttaraina aupāsādaṃ daivavanai sālavanai| yai khau tai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā amhākaṃ gāmakkhaittaṃ āgacchanti, atithī nau tai haunti| atithī khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā| yampi samaṇau gautamau aupāsādaṃ anuppattau  aupāsādai viharati uttaraina aupāsādaṃ daivavanai sālavanai, atithimhākaṃ samaṇau gautamau| atithi khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbau garukātabbau mānaitabbau pūjaitabbau| imināpaṅgaina  na arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| aittakai khau ahaṃ, bhau, tassa bhautau gautamassa vaṇṇai pariyāpuṇāmi, nau ca khau sau bhavaṃ gautamau aittakavaṇṇau; aparimāṇavaṇṇau hi sau bhavaṃ gautamau| aikamaikainapi taina aikamaikainapi bhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau na arahati, sau, bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitunti| taina hi, bhau, sabbaiva mayaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmā’’ti|

426. atha  khau caṅkī brāhmaṇau mahatā brāhmaṇagaṇaina saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina  bhagavā vuddhaihi vuddhaihi brāhmaṇaihi saddhiṃ kiñ‍ci kiñ‍ci kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā nisin‍nau hauti| taina khau pana samayaina kāpaṭikau kāpaṭhikau (sī॰ pī॰), kāpadikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāma māṇavau daharau vuttasirau sauḷasavassuddaisikau jātiyā, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| sau vuddhānaṃ vuddhānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ bhagavatā saddhiṃ mantayamānānaṃ antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaiti| atha khau bhagavā kāpaṭikaṃ māṇavaṃ apasādaiti – ‘‘māyasmā bhāradvājau vuddhānaṃ vuddhānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mantayamānānaṃ antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaitu| kathāpariyausānaṃ āyasmā bhāradvājau āgamaitū’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, caṅkī brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ gautamau kāpaṭikaṃ māṇavaṃ apasādaisi| kulaputtau ca kāpaṭikau māṇavau, bahussutau ca kāpaṭikau māṇavau, paṇḍitau ca kāpaṭikau māṇavau, kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca kāpaṭikau māṇavau, pahauti ca kāpaṭikau māṇavau bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti| atha khau bhagavatau  aitadahausi – ‘‘addhā khau  kāpaṭikassa aitadahausi ‘‘kāpaṭikassa (ka॰) māṇavassa taivij‍jakai pāvacanai kathā kathaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), kathaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavissati| tathā hi naṃ brāhmaṇā saṃpuraikkharauntī’’ti| atha khau kāpaṭikassa māṇavassa aitadahausi  – ‘‘yadā mai samaṇau gautamau cakkhuṃ upasaṃharissati, athāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchissāmī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā kāpaṭikassa māṇavassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya yaina kāpaṭikau māṇavau taina cakkhūni upasaṃhāsi|

427. atha khau kāpaṭikassa māṇavassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saman‍nāharati khau maṃ samaṇau gautamau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau kāpaṭikau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yadidaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ itihitihaparamparāya piṭakasampadāya, tattha ca brāhmaṇā aikaṃsaina niṭṭhaṃ gacchanti – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti| idha bhavaṃ gautamau kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhāradvāja, atthi kauci brāhmaṇānaṃ aikabrāhmaṇaupi yau aivamāha – ‘ahamaitaṃ jānāmi, ahamaitaṃ passāmi| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhāradvāja, atthi kauci brāhmaṇānaṃ aikācariyaupi, aikācariyapācariyaupi, yāva sattamā ācariyamahayugāpi, yau aivamāha – ‘ahamaitaṃ jānāmi, ahamaitaṃ passāmi| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhāradvāja, yaipi tai brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau yaisamidaṃ aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti vācitamanuvācainti saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau  vāmakau vāmadaivau vaissāmittau yamataggi aṅgīrasau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhau kassapau bhagu, taipi aivamāhaṃsu – ‘mayamaitaṃ jānāma, mayamaitaṃ passāma| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘iti kira, bhāradvāja, natthi kauci brāhmaṇānaṃ aikabrāhmaṇaupi yau aivamāha – ‘ahamaitaṃ jānāmi, ahamaitaṃ passāmi| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti; natthi kauci brāhmaṇānaṃ aikācariyaupi aikācariyapācariyaupi, yāva sattamā ācariyamahayugāpi, yau aivamāha – ‘ahamaitaṃ  jānāmi, ahamaitaṃ passāmi| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti; yaipi tai brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau yaisamidaṃ aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti vācitamanuvācainti saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau vāmakau vāmadaivau vaissāmittau yamataggi aṅgīrasau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhau kassapau bhagu, taipi na aivamāhaṃsu – ‘mayamaitaṃ jānāma, mayamaitaṃ passāma| idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti|

428. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhāradvāja, andhavaiṇi paramparāsaṃsattā purimaupi na passati majjhimaupi na passati pacchimaupi na passati; aivamaiva khau, bhāradvāja, andhavaiṇūpamaṃ mañ‍ñai brāhmaṇānaṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jati – purimaupi na passati majjhimaupi na passati pacchimaupi na passati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhāradvāja, nanu aivaṃ santai brāhmaṇānaṃ amūlikā saddhā sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘na khvaittha, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā saddhāyaiva payirupāsanti, anussavāpaittha brāhmaṇā payirupāsantī’’ti| ‘‘pubbaiva khau tvaṃ, bhāradvāja, saddhaṃ agamāsi, anussavaṃ idāni vadaisi| pañ‍ca khau imai, bhāradvāja, dhammā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dvaidhā vipākā| katamai pañ‍ca? saddhā, ruci, anussavau, ākāraparivitak‍kau, diṭṭhinijjhānakkhanti – imai khau, bhāradvāja, pañ‍ca dhammā diṭṭhaiva dhammai dvaidhā vipākā| api ca, bhāradvāja, susaddahitaṃyaiva hauti, tañ‍ca hauti rittaṃ tucchaṃ musā; nau caipi susaddahitaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anañ‍ñathā| api ca, bhāradvāja, surucitaṃyaiva hauti…pai॰… svānussutaṃyaiva hauti…pai॰… suparivitak‍kitaṃyaiva hauti…pai॰… sunijjhāyitaṃyaiva hauti, tañ‍ca hauti rittaṃ tucchaṃ musā; nau caipi sunijjhāyitaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anañ‍ñathā| sac‍camanurakkhatā, bhāradvāja, viñ‍ñunā purisaina nālamaittha aikaṃsaina niṭṭhaṃ gantuṃ – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

429. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhau gautama, sac‍cānurakkhaṇā hauti, kittāvatā sac‍camanurakkhati? sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘saddhā caipi, bhāradvāja, purisassa hauti; ‘aivaṃ mai saddhā’ti – iti vadaṃ sac‍camanurakkhati aivamaiva sijjhatīti iti vā, taṃ sac‍camanurakkhati (ka॰), natvaiva tāva aikaṃsaina niṭṭhaṃ gacchati  – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti  ( ) (aittāvatā khau bhāradvāja sac‍cānurakkhaṇā hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanurakkhati, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaima, na tvaiva tāva sac‍cānubaudhau hauti) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| ruci caipi, bhāradvāja, purisassa hauti…pai॰… anussavau caipi, bhāradvāja, purisassa hauti…pai॰… ākāraparivitak‍kau caipi, bhāradvāja, purisassa hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhinijjhānakkhanti caipi, bhāradvāja, purisassa hauti; ‘aivaṃ mai diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantī’ti – iti vadaṃ sac‍camanurakkhati, natvaiva tāva aikaṃsaina niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, maughamañ‍ña’nti| aittāvatā khau, bhāradvāja, sac‍cānurakkhaṇā hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanurakkhati, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaima; na tvaiva tāva sac‍cānubaudhau hautī’’ti|

430. ‘‘aittāvatā, bhau gautama, sac‍cānurakkhaṇā hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanurakkhati, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ paikkhāma| kittāvatā pana, bhau gautama, sac‍cānubaudhau hauti, kittāvatā sac‍camanubujjhati? sac‍cānubaudhaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘idha idha kira (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhāradvāja, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| tamainaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upasaṅkamitvā tīsu dhammaisu saman‍naisati – laubhanīyaisu  dhammaisu, dausanīyaisu dhammaisu, mauhanīyaisu dhammaisu| atthi nu khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā laubhanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau  ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya yaṃ paraisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya  dukkhāyāti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā laubhanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya yaṃ paraisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya dukkhāyāti (sabbattha)| tathārūpau tathā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khau panimassāyasmatau kāyasamācārau tathārūpau tathā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacīsamācārau yathā taṃ aluddhassa| yaṃ khau pana ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti, gambhīrau sau dhammau duddasau duranubaudhau  santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau; na sau dhammau sudaisiyau luddhainā’’’ti|

431. ‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau visuddhaṃ laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi samanupassati tatau naṃ uttari saman‍naisati dausanīyaisu dhammaisu| atthi nu khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā dausanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi dausanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya yaṃ paraisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyāti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā dausanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi dausanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya  yaṃ paraisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| tathārūpau khau panimassāyasmatau kāyasamācārau tathārūpau vacīsamācārau yathā taṃ aduṭṭhassa| yaṃ khau pana ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti, gambhīrau sau dhammau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau; na sau dhammau sudaisiyau duṭṭhainā’’’ti|

432. ‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau visuddhaṃ dausanīyaihi dhammaihi samanupassati, tatau naṃ uttari saman‍naisati mauhanīyaisu dhammaisu| atthi nu khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā mauhanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi mauhanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya yaṃ paraisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyāti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi khau imassāyasmatau tathārūpā mauhanīyā dhammā yathārūpaihi mauhanīyaihi dhammaihi pariyādin‍nacittau ajānaṃ vā vadaiyya – jānāmīti, apassaṃ vā vadaiyya – passāmīti, paraṃ vā tadatthāya samādapaiyya yaṃ paraisaṃ  assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| tathārūpau khau panimassāyasmatau kāyasamācārau tathārūpau vacīsamācārau yathā taṃ amūḷhassa| yaṃ khau pana ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti, gambhīrau sau dhammau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau  atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau; na sau dhammau sudaisiyau mūḷhainā’’’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau visuddhaṃ mauhanīyaihi dhammaihi samanupassati; atha tamhi saddhaṃ nivaisaiti, saddhājātau upasaṅkamati, upasaṅkamantau payirupāsati, payirupāsantau sautaṃ audahati, auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti, dhatānaṃ dhāritānaṃ (ka॰) dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, atthaṃ upaparikkhatau dhammā nijjhānaṃ khamanti, dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā sati chandau jāyati, chandajātau ussahati, ussahitvā tulaiti, tulayitvā padahati, pahitattau samānau kāyaina caiva paramasac‍caṃ sacchikarauti pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ ativijjha passati| aittāvatā khau, bhāradvāja, sac‍cānubaudhau hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanubujjhati, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānubaudhaṃ pañ‍ñapaima; na tvaiva tāva sac‍cānuppatti hautī’’ti|

433. ‘‘aittāvatā, bhau gautama, sac‍cānubaudhau hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanubujjhati, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānubaudhaṃ paikkhāma| kittāvatā pana, bhau gautama, sac‍cānuppatti hauti, kittāvatā sac‍camanupāpuṇāti? sac‍cānuppattiṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘taisaṃyaiva, bhāradvāja, dhammānaṃ āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ sac‍cānuppatti hauti| aittāvatā khau, bhāradvāja, sac‍cānuppatti hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanupāpuṇāti, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānuppattiṃ pañ‍ñapaimā’’ti|

434. ‘‘aittāvatā, bhau gautama, sac‍cānuppatti hauti, aittāvatā sac‍camanupāpuṇāti, aittāvatā ca mayaṃ sac‍cānuppattiṃ paikkhāma| sac‍cānuppattiyā pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? sac‍cānuppattiyā bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘sac‍cānuppattiyā  khau, bhāradvāja, padhānaṃ bahukāraṃ| nau caitaṃ padahaiyya, nayidaṃ sac‍camanupāpuṇaiyya| yasmā ca khau padahati tasmā sac‍camanupāpuṇāti| tasmā sac‍cānuppattiyā padhānaṃ bahukāra’’nti|

‘‘padhānassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? padhānassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ  gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘padhānassa khau, bhāradvāja, tulanā  bahukārā| nau caitaṃ tulaiyya, nayidaṃ padahaiyya| yasmā ca khau tulaiti tasmā padahati| tasmā padhānassa tulanā bahukārā’’ti|

‘‘tulanāya pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? tulanāya bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘tulanāya khau, bhāradvāja, ussāhau bahukārau| nau caitaṃ ussahaiyya, nayidaṃ tulaiyya| yasmā ca khau ussahati tasmā tulaiti| tasmā tulanāya ussāhau bahukārau’’ti|

‘‘ussāhassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? ussāhassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘ussāhassa khau, bhāradvāja, chandau bahukārau| nau caitaṃ chandau jāyaitha, nayidaṃ ussahaiyya| yasmā ca khau chandau jāyati tasmā ussahati| tasmā ussāhassa chandau bahukārau’’ti|

‘‘chandassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? chandassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘chandassa khau, bhāradvāja, dhammanijjhānakkhanti bahukārā| nau caitai dhammā nijjhānaṃ khamaiyyuṃ, nayidaṃ chandau jāyaitha| yasmā ca khau dhammā  nijjhānaṃ khamanti tasmā chandau jāyati| tasmā chandassa dhammanijjhānakkhanti bahukārā’’ti|

‘‘dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā khau, bhāradvāja, atthūpaparikkhā bahukārā| nau caitaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhaiyya, nayidaṃ dhammā nijjhānaṃ khamaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau atthaṃ upaparikkhati tasmā dhammā nijjhānaṃ khamanti| tasmā dhammanijjhānakkhantiyā atthūpaparikkhā bahukārā’’ti|

‘‘atthūpaparikkhāya pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? atthūpaparikkhāya bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘atthūpaparikkhāya khau, bhāradvāja, dhammadhāraṇā  bahukārā| nau caitaṃ dhammaṃ dhāraiyya, nayidaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhaiyya| yasmā ca khau dhammaṃ dhāraiti tasmā atthaṃ upaparikkhati| tasmā atthūpaparikkhāya dhammadhāraṇā bahukārā’’ti|

‘‘dhammadhāraṇāya pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? dhammadhāraṇāya bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘dhammadhāraṇāya  khau, bhāradvāja, dhammassavanaṃ bahukāraṃ| nau caitaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya, nayidaṃ dhammaṃ dhāraiyya| yasmā ca khau dhammaṃ suṇāti tasmā dhammaṃ dhāraiti| tasmā dhammadhāraṇāya dhammassavanaṃ bahukāra’’nti|

‘‘dhammassavanassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? dhammassavanassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘dhammassavanassa khau, bhāradvāja, sautāvadhānaṃ bahukāraṃ| nau caitaṃ sautaṃ audahaiyya, nayidaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya| yasmā ca khau sautaṃ audahati tasmā dhammaṃ suṇāti| tasmā dhammassavanassa sautāvadhānaṃ bahukāra’’nti|

‘‘sautāvadhānassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? sautāvadhānassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘sautāvadhānassa khau, bhāradvāja, payirupāsanā bahukārā| nau caitaṃ payirupāsaiyya, nayidaṃ sautaṃ audahaiyya| yasmā ca khau payirupāsati tasmā sautaṃ audahati| tasmā sautāvadhānassa payirupāsanā bahukārā’’ti|

‘‘payirupāsanāya pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? payirupāsanāya bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘payirupāsanāya khau, bhāradvāja, upasaṅkamanaṃ bahukāraṃ| nau caitaṃ upasaṅkamaiyya, nayidaṃ payirupāsaiyya| yasmā ca khau upasaṅkamati tasmā payirupāsati| tasmā payirupāsanāya upasaṅkamanaṃ bahukāra’’nti|

‘‘upasaṅkamanassa pana, bhau gautama, katamau dhammau bahukārau? upasaṅkamanassa bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāmā’’ti| ‘‘upasaṅkamanassa khau, bhāradvāja, saddhā bahukārā| nau caitaṃ saddhā jāyaitha, nayidaṃ upasaṅkamaiyya| yasmā ca khau saddhā jāyati tasmā upasaṅkamati| tasmā upasaṅkamanassa saddhā bahukārā’’ti|

435. ‘‘sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ  mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ apucchimha, sac‍cānurakkhaṇaṃ  bhavaṃ gautamau byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca taina camha attamanā| sac‍cānubaudhaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ apucchimha, sac‍cānubaudhaṃ bhavaṃ gautamau byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca taina camha attamanā| sac‍cānuppattiṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ apucchimha, sac‍cānuppattiṃ bhavaṃ gautamau byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca taina camha attamanā| sac‍cānuppattiyā bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ mayaṃ bhavantaṃ  gautamaṃ apucchimha, sac‍cānuppattiyā bahukāraṃ dhammaṃ bhavaṃ gautamau byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca taina camha attamanā| yaṃyadaiva ca mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ apucchimha taṃtadaiva bhavaṃ gautamau byākāsi; tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca taina camha attamanā| mayañhi, bhau gautama, pubbai aivaṃ jānāma – ‘kai ca muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kaṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā, kai ca dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’ti? ajanaisi vata mai bhavaṃ gautamau samaṇaisu samaṇapaimaṃ, samaṇaisu samaṇapasādaṃ, samaṇaisu samaṇagāravaṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

caṅkīsuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. aisukārīsuttaṃ

436. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau aisukārī brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau aisukārī brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhau gautama, catassau pāricariyā pañ‍ñapainti – brāhmaṇassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, khattiyassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, vaissassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, suddassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti – ‘brāhmaṇau vā brāhmaṇaṃ paricaraiyya, khattiyau vā brāhmaṇaṃ paricaraiyya, vaissau vā brāhmaṇaṃ paricaraiyya, suddau vā brāhmaṇaṃ paricaraiyyā’ti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇassa pāricariyaṃ  pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā khattiyassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti – ‘khattiyau vā khattiyaṃ paricaraiyya, vaissau vā khattiyaṃ paricaraiyya, suddau vā khattiyaṃ paricaraiyyā’ti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā khattiyassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā vaissassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti – ‘vaissau vā vaissaṃ paricaraiyya, suddau vā vaissaṃ  paricaraiyyā’ti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā vaissassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā suddassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti – ‘suddauva suddaṃ paricaraiyya| kau panañ‍ñau suddaṃ paricarissatī’ti? idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā suddassa pāricariyaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| brāhmaṇā, bhau gautama, imā catassau pāricariyā pañ‍ñapainti| idha bhavaṃ gautamau kimāhā’’ti?

437. ‘‘kiṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, sabbau laukau brāhmaṇānaṃ aitadabbhanujānāti – ‘imā catassau pāricariyā pañ‍ñapaintū’’’ti pañ‍ñapaintīti (sī॰ ka॰)? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, purisau daliddau daḷiddau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) assakau anāḷhiyau| tassa akāmassa bilaṃ aulaggaiyyuṃ – ‘idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, maṃsaṃ khāditabbaṃ, mūlañ‍ca anuppadātabba’nti| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā appaṭiñ‍ñāya taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ, atha ca panimā catassau pāricariyā pañ‍ñapainti| nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘sabbaṃ paricaritabba’nti  vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘sabbaṃ na paricaritabba’nti vadāmi| yaṃ hissa, brāhmaṇa, paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu pāpiyau assa na saiyyau, nāhaṃ taṃ ‘paricaritabba’nti vadāmi; yañ‍ca khvāssa, brāhmaṇa, paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saiyyau assa na pāpiyau tamahaṃ ‘paricaritabba’nti vadāmi| khattiyaṃ caipi, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yaṃ vā tai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu pāpiyau assa na saiyyau, yaṃ vā tai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saiyyau assa  na pāpiyau; kamaittha paricaraiyyāsī’ti, khattiyaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, sammā byākaramānau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yañhi mai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu pāpiyau assa na saiyyau, nāhaṃ taṃ paricaraiyyaṃ; yañ‍ca khau mai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saiyyau assa na pāpiyau tamahaṃ paricaraiyya’nti| brāhmaṇaṃ caipi, brāhmaṇa…pai॰… vaissaṃ caipi, brāhmaṇa…pai॰… suddaṃ caipi, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yaṃ vā tai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu pāpiyau assa na saiyyau, yaṃ vā tai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saiyyau assa na pāpiyau; kamaittha paricaraiyyāsī’ti, suddaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, sammā byākaramānau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yañhi mai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu pāpiyau assa na saiyyau, nāhaṃ taṃ paricaraiyyaṃ; yañ‍ca khau mai paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saiyyau assa na pāpiyau tamahaṃ paricaraiyya’nti| nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uc‍cākulīnatā saiyyaṃsau’ti vadāmi, na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uc‍cākulīnatā pāpiyaṃsau’ti  vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uḷāravaṇṇatā saiyyaṃsau’ti vadāmi, na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uḷāravaṇṇatā pāpiyaṃsau’ti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uḷārabhaugatā saiyyaṃsau’ti vadāmi, na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘uḷārabhaugatā pāpiyaṃsau’ti vadāmi|

438. ‘‘uc‍cākulīnaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇāvācau hauti, pharusāvācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, abhijjhālu hauti, byāpan‍nacittau hauti, micchādiṭṭhi hauti| tasmā ‘na uc‍cākulīnatā saiyyaṃsau’ti vadāmi| uc‍cākulīnaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti| tasmā ‘na uc‍cākulīnatā pāpiyaṃsau’ti vadāmi|

439. ‘‘uḷāravaṇṇaupi  hi, brāhmaṇa…pai॰… uḷārabhaugaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi hauti| tasmā  ‘na uḷārabhaugatā saiyyaṃsau’ti vadāmi| uḷārabhaugaupi hi, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti| tasmā ‘na uḷārabhaugatā  pāpiyaṃsau’ti vadāmi| nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘sabbaṃ paricaritabba’nti vadāmi, na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ‘sabbaṃ na paricaritabba’nti vadāmi| yaṃ hissa, brāhmaṇa, paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu saddhā vaḍḍhati, sīlaṃ vaḍḍhati, sutaṃ vaḍḍhati, cāgau vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñā vaḍḍhati, tamahaṃ ‘paricaritabba’nti (vadāmi| yaṃ hissa, brāhmaṇa, paricaratau pāricariyāhaitu na saddhā vaḍḍhati, na sīlaṃ vaḍḍhati, na sutaṃ vaḍḍhati, na cāgau vaḍḍhati, na pañ‍ñā vaḍḍhati, nāhaṃ taṃ ‘paricaritabba’nti) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi vadāmī’’ti|

440. aivaṃ vuttai, aisukārī brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhau gautama, cattāri dhanāni pañ‍ñapainti – brāhmaṇassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, khattiyassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, vaissassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, suddassa  sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti bhikkhācariyaṃ; bhikkhācariyañ‍ca pana brāhmaṇau sandhanaṃ atimañ‍ñamānau akic‍cakārī hauti gaupauva adin‍naṃ ādiyamānauti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā khattiyassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti dhanukalāpaṃ; dhanukalāpañ‍ca  pana khattiyau sandhanaṃ atimañ‍ñamānau akic‍cakārī hauti gaupauva adin‍naṃ ādiyamānauti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā khattiyassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā vaissassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti kasigaurakkhaṃ; kasigaurakkhañ‍ca pana vaissau sandhanaṃ atimañ‍ñamānau akic‍cakārī hauti gaupauva adin‍naṃ ādiyamānauti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā vaissassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tatridaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā suddassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti asitabyābhaṅgiṃ; asitabyābhaṅgiñ‍ca pana suddau sandhanaṃ atimañ‍ñamānau akic‍cakārī hauti gaupauva adin‍naṃ ādiyamānauti| idaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā suddassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| brāhmaṇā, bhau gautama, imāni cattāri dhanāni pañ‍ñapainti| idha bhavaṃ gautamau kimāhā’’ti?

441. ‘‘kiṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, sabbau laukau brāhmaṇānaṃ aitadabbhanujānāti – ‘imāni cattāri dhanāni pañ‍ñapaintū’’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, purisau daliddau  assakau anāḷhiyau| tassa akāmassa bilaṃ aulaggaiyyuṃ – ‘idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, maṃsaṃ khāditabbaṃ, mūlañ‍ca anuppadātabba’nti| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā appaṭiñ‍ñāya taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ, atha ca panimāni cattāri dhanāni pañ‍ñapainti| ariyaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ purisassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| paurāṇaṃ khau panassa mātāpaittikaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ anussaratau yattha yatthaiva  attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti taina tainaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| khattiyakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘khattiyau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; brāhmaṇakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘brāhmaṇau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; vaissakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘vaissau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; suddakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘suddau’tvaiva  saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, yaṃyadaiva pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca aggi jalati taina tainaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| kaṭṭhañ‍cai paṭic‍ca aggi jalati ‘kaṭṭhaggi’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; sakalikañ‍cai paṭic‍ca aggi jalati ‘sakalikaggi’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; tiṇañ‍cai paṭic‍ca aggi jalati ‘tiṇaggi’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; gaumayañ‍cai paṭic‍ca aggi jalati ‘gaumayaggi’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ purisassa sandhanaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| paurāṇaṃ khau panassa mātāpaittikaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ anussaratau yattha yatthaiva attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti taina tainaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘khattiyakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘khattiyau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; brāhmaṇakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘brāhmaṇau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; vaissakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘vaissau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; suddakulai cai attabhāvassa abhinibbatti hauti ‘suddau’tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘khattiyakulā  caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, abrahmacariyā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘brāhmaṇakulā  caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘vaissakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘suddakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰…  sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

442. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇauva nu khau pahauti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaituṃ, nau khattiyau nau vaissau nau suddau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama| khattiyaupi hi, bhau gautama, pahauti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaituṃ; brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama… suddaupi hi, bhau gautama… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pahaunti asmiṃ padaisai avairaṃ abyābajjhaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaitu’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, khattiyakulā caipi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘brāhmaṇakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… vaissakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… suddakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰…  sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

443. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇauva nu khau pahauti sauttisināniṃ ādāya nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaituṃ, nau khattiyau nau vaissau nau suddau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama| khattiyaupi hi, bhau gautama, pahauti sauttisināniṃ ādāya nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaituṃ; brāhmaṇaupi hi, bhau gautama… vaissaupi hi, bhau gautama … suddaupi hi, bhau gautama… sabbaipi hi, bhau gautama, cattārau vaṇṇā pahaunti sauttisināniṃ ādāya nadiṃ gantvā rajaujal‍laṃ pavāhaitu’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, khattiyakulā caipi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘brāhmaṇakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… vaissakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… suddakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

444. ‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, idha rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau nānājac‍cānaṃ purisānaṃ purisasataṃ san‍nipātaiyya – ‘āyantu bhauntau yai tattha khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍nā sākassa vā sālassa vā salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggiṃ abhinibbattaintu, taijau pātukarauntu; āyantu pana bhauntau yai tattha caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍nā sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggiṃ abhinibbattaintu, taijau pātukarauntū’’’ti?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, yau aivaṃ nu khau sau khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍naihi sākassa vā sālassa vā salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau taijau pātukatau sau aiva nu khvāssa aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ  kātuṃ; yau pana sau caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍naihi sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau taijau pātukatau svāssa aggi na caiva ac‍cimā na ca vaṇṇavā na ca  pabhassarau na ca taina sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama| yaupi hi sau, bhau gautama, khattiyakulā brāhmaṇakulā rājañ‍ñakulā uppan‍naihi sākassa vā sālassa  vā salaḷassa vā candanassa vā padumakassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau taijau pātukatau svāssa aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātuṃ; yaupi sau caṇḍālakulā naisādakulā vainakulā rathakārakulā puk‍kusakulā uppan‍naihi sāpānadauṇiyā vā sūkaradauṇiyā vā rajakadauṇiyā vā airaṇḍakaṭṭhassa vā uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya aggi abhinibbattau taijau pātukatau svāssa aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca taina ca sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātuṃ| sabbaupi hi, bhau gautama, aggi ac‍cimā caiva vaṇṇavā ca pabhassarau ca sabbainapi sak‍kā agginā aggikaraṇīyaṃ kātu’’nti|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, brāhmaṇa, khattiyakulā caipi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma  pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ| brāhmaṇakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… vaissakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa… suddakulā caipi, brāhmaṇa, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, abrahmacariyā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, aisukārī brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

aisukārīsuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dhanañ‍jānisuttaṃ
